# Numerical Reasoning



Formato deduplicado para estudio.



## Pregunta 1

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDp5VkVabFlLd1JFMnlYUWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=1

### Enunciado

In 2010, what was the ratio of GDP in the UK to that in Sweden?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0001-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 1 : 5
- **B.** 2 : 1
- **C.** 3 : 1
- **D.** 1 : 2
- **E.** 5 : 1

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **E**: 5 : 1

Step 1 – Calculate the GDP in the UK in 2010

644.000/0,368 = 1.750.000

Step 2 – Calculate the GDP in Sweden in 2010

161.000/0,46 = 350.000

Step 3 – Calculate the ratio of UK’s GDP to Sweden’s

1.750.000 : 350.000 = 5 : 1

## Pregunta 2

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDp5VkVabFlLd1JFMnlYUWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=2

### Enunciado

In 2007, the average productivity in the EU postal market, measured in terms of turnover per person, was 12% higher than in 2004. Given that employment in 2007 in the EU was down 2% from 2004, what was the turnover in 2004?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0002-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 65.856 million Euro
- **B.** 52.500.000 Euro
- **C.** 54.665 million Euro
- **D.** 54.664.723.030 Euro
- **E.** 52.500 million Euro

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **D**: 54.664.723.030 Euro

Step 1 – Work out the average productivity in the EU in 2007

60.000.000.000/1.100.000 = 54.545,45455

Step 2 – Work out the average productivity in the EU in 2004

54.545,45455/1,12 = 48.701,2987

Step 3 – Work out employment in the EU in 2004

1.100.000/0,98 = 1.122.448,98

Step 4 – Work out the turnover in the EU in 2004

1.122.448,98 x 48.701,2987 = 54.664.723.030

## Pregunta 3

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDp5VkVabFlLd1JFMnlYUWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=3

### Enunciado

In 2011 in the EU, in terms of average expenditure per trip, the oldest group spent a little less than the ’45-64’ group, € 580 and €610 respectively. Given that the average total expenditure per trip was € 560, what was the average expenditure per trip for the ’15-44’ group?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0003-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** € 620
- **B.** € 513
- **C.** € 504
- **D.** € 523
- **E.** € 540

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **C**: € 504

Step 1 – Calculate the total expenditure

470 x 560 = 263.200 million

Step 2 – Calculate the expenditure in the 45-64 and 65+ age groups

(remember we need to take into consideration the number of trips of each age group as the questions asks for expenditure per trip)

45-64: 160 x 610 = 97.600 million

65+: 90 x 580 = 52.200

Step 3 – Calculate the expenditure in the 15-44 age group

263.200 – (97.600 + 52.200) = 113.400

Step 4 – Calculate the average expenditure per trip in the 15-44 age group

113.400/225 = € 504

## Pregunta 4

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDp5VkVabFlLd1JFMnlYUWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=4

### Enunciado

Between 2011 and 2012, the number of natural gas companies with a market share of 5% or more in the EU decreased by ¹⁄₁₅, while the total number of natural gas companies increased by 12%. By what percentage have companies, with a market share of less than 5%, increased between 2003 and 2012 in the EU?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0004-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 29,6%
- **B.** 108,3%
- **C.** 109,2%
- **D.** 166,7%
- **E.** 145,0%

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **E**: 145,0%

Step 1 – Work out figures for 2012 in the EU

Companies 5%+: 75 x 14/15 = 70

Total companies: 325 x 1,12 = 364

Step 2 – Calculate the number of companies, with market share of less than 5% in 2003 and 2012

2003: 174 – 54 = 120

2012: 364 – 70 = 294

Step 3 – Work out the percentage increase between 2003 and 2012 for companies with market share of less than 5%

294/120 = 2,45 (145%)

## Pregunta 5

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDp5VkVabFlLd1JFMnlYUWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=5

### Enunciado

In 2010, the average personnel costs across all businesses in the EU were EUR 30,9 thousand per employee. By how much did the average cost per employee across the whole motor and motorcycle trade differ from this overall business average in 2010?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0005-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 7,7 thousand lower
- **B.** 0,9 thousand lower
- **C.** 4,6 thousand higher
- **D.** 7,3 thousand lower
- **E.** 8,9 thousand lower

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 7,7 thousand lower

Step 1 – Total the personnel costs across the motor trade

45.600 + 24.000 + 15.948 + 1.800 = 87.348 million

Step 2 – Total the number of persons employed across the motor trade

1.520 + 1.470 + 675 + 100 = 3.765 thousand

Step 3 – Work out the average cost per employee across the motor trade

87.348.000.000/3.765.000 = 23.200

Or 87.348/3.765 = 23,2 thousand

Step 4 – Work out the difference

30,9 – 23,2 = 7,7 thousand lower

## Pregunta 6

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDp5VkVabFlLd1JFMnlYUWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=6

### Enunciado

If the population aged 20 – 64 in Ireland is growing by 18 thousand a year, how many more people would have needed to be employed in 2012 to have achieved the target 68,5% employment rate for that age group?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0006-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 230 thousand
- **B.** 206 thousand
- **C.** 341 thousand
- **D.** 303 thousand
- **E.** 299 thousand

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **D**: 303 thousand

Step 1 – Calculate the total population, aged 20 – 64, in 2012

3.083 + 18 + 18 = 3.119 thousand

Step 2 – Calculate the number needed to be employed in 2012 to meet 68,5% target

3.119 x 0,685 = 2.136,515 thousand

Step 3 – Calculate the actual number employed in 2012

3.119 x 0,588 = 1.833,972 thousand

Step 4 – Calculate the difference

2.136,515 – 1.833,972 = 302,543 thousand

## Pregunta 7

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDp5VkVabFlLd1JFMnlYUWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=7

### Enunciado

The population of Romania decreased by 5% between 2005 and 2010. What percentage of Romania’s population were in tertiary education in 2010, given that 18,1% of the population were pupils or students in 2005?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0007-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 4,8%
- **B.** 18,5%
- **C.** 4,3%
- **D.** 19,6
- **E.** 4,2%

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 4,8%

Step 1 – Calculate the total population in Romania in 2005

3.847/0,181 = 21.254,14365 thousand

Step 2 – Calculate the total population in Romania in 2010

21.254,14365 x 0,95 = 20.191,43646 thousand

Step 3 – Calculate how students were in tertiary education in 2010

3.735 x 0,258 = 963,63

Step 4 – Calculate the students in tertiary education as a percentage of the total population

963,63/20.191,43646 x 100 = 4,772468774% (4,8%)

## Pregunta 8

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDp5VkVabFlLd1JFMnlYUWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=8

### Enunciado

In 2010 in the EU, the average turnover per employee in the motor market was ⅕ lower than the overall business average but 2% higher than the retail business average in the EU. What was the difference between the overall business average and the retail business average?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0008-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 72,4
- **B.** 73.849
- **C.** 73,8
- **D.** 67.135
- **E.** 72.400

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **E**: 72.400

Step 1 – Work out the average turnover per employee in the motor market

1.020.452.000.000/3.800.000 = 268.540

Step 2 – Work out the overall business average

268.540/(4/5) = 335.675

Step 3 – Work out the retail business average

268.540/1,02 = 263.274,5098

Step 4 – Work out the difference between them

335.675 - 263.274,5098 = 72.400,4902

## Pregunta 9

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDp5VkVabFlLd1JFMnlYUWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=9

### Enunciado

What is the size (in km2) of Canada and United States combined?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0009-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 3.646.000
- **B.** 19.500.000
- **C.** 10.450.000
- **D.** 9.500.000
- **E.** 13.000.000

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **B**: 19.500.000

Step 1 – Work out the area in km2 of Canada (you can use either year)

Population/area = population density

Area = Population/population density

1960: 17,9/1,8 = 9,944444444 million km2

2012: 34,8/3,5 = 9,942857143 million km2

The differences are due to rounding errors

Step 2 – Work out the area in sq km of the United States (you can use either year)

1960: 186,3/19,4 = 9,603092784 million km2

2012: 314,9/32,8 = 9,600609756 million km2

Step 3 – Add the two together

1960: 9,944444444 + 9,603092784 = 19,54753723 million km2

2012: 9,942857143 + 9,600609756 = 19,5434669 million km2

## Pregunta 10

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDp5VkVabFlLd1JFMnlYUWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=10

### Enunciado

In 2010 in the EU, 32% of teachers were in secondary education. How many teachers were not in secondary education in the EU in 2010?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0010-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 1.696.000
- **B.** 1.696
- **C.** 3.604
- **D.** 3.604.000
- **E.** 856.800

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **D**: 3.604.000

Step 1 – Work out the how many teachers were in education in the EU in 2010

79.500/15 = 5.300 thousand

Step 2 – Work out the number of pupils not in secondary education in 2010

100 – 32 = 68%

5.300 x 0,68 = 3.604 thousand

## Pregunta 11

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDp3bG0zNy1ZRmpFcXR3d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=1

### Enunciado

The top 5 countries in the table below represented 52% of all permits issued in the EU in 2013. As there was an increase of 5,6% permits in 2013 compared to the previous year, how many permits were issued in the EU in 2012?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0011-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 1.828.380
- **B.** 1.130.424
- **C.** 451.553
- **D.** 1.237.673
- **E.** 1.669.945

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **E**: 1.669.945

Step 1 – Add up the top 5 countries

214.000 + 202.000 + 174.000 + 168.000 + 159.000 = 917.000

Step 2 – Work out the total number of permits issued in 2013

917.000 represent 52%

So 917.000/0,52 = 1.763.461,538

Step 3 – Calculate the number of permits issues in 2012

1.763.461,358/1,056 = 1.669.944,639

## Pregunta 12

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDp3bG0zNy1ZRmpFcXR3d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=2

### Enunciado

In 2010 GDP in Estonia was 2,5% up on 2009. How much more did Estonia need to spend on R & D to meet the target of 3% of GDP in 2009?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0012-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 154,00 million Euros
- **B.** 170,97 million Euros
- **C.** 170,86 million Euros
- **D.** 150,28 million Euros
- **E.** 187,31 million Euros

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **B**: 170,97 million Euros

Step 1 – Calculate GDP in Estonia in 2009

Since GDP grew by 2.5% from 2009 to 2010, we divide the 2010 value by 1.025 to reverse the increase and find the original value. This is the correct method when working backwards from a percentage increase.

11.162,25/1,025 = 10.890 million (inverted percentages)

Step 2 – Calculate how much Estonia spent on R & D in 2009

10.890 x 0,0143 = 155,727 million

Step 3 – Calculate how much the 3% target would have been in 2009

10.890 x 0,03 = 326,70 million

Step 4 – Work out how much Estonia needed to spend to meet 3% target

326,7 – 155,727 million = 170,973 million

## Pregunta 13

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDp3bG0zNy1ZRmpFcXR3d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=3

### Enunciado

In 2008, if 20.000 more people had been employed in fish and crustaceans and 40.000 more people had been employed in bakery products, what turnover would these two activities have made assuming that the same level of productivity could be maintained?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0013-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 140.863 million Euros
- **B.** 676.282 million Euros
- **C.** 308.056 million Euros
- **D.** 78.332 million Euros
- **E.** 105.350 million Euros

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 140.863 million Euros

Step 1 – Work out turnover per person in fish & crustaceans and thus turnover with 20.000 more people

22.440/120 = 187

187 x 140 = 26.180 (120 + 20 = 140)

Step 2 – Work out turnover per person in bakery products and thus turnover with 40.000 more people

111.763/1531 = 73

73 x 1571 = 114.683 (1531 + 40 = 1571)

Step 3 – Calculate the turnover for both activities

26.180 + 114.683 = 140.863 million

## Pregunta 14

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDp3bG0zNy1ZRmpFcXR3d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=4

### Enunciado

Between 2006 and 2011, the trade balance (the difference between exports and imports) decreased by 25% while total imports increased by 25%. By what percentage did exports change between 2006 and 2011?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0014-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 25,0%
- **B.** 74,9%
- **C.** 25,1%
- **D.** 58,8%
- **E.** 33,7%

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **E**: 33,7%

Step 1 – Calculate total imports in 2011

1.363,2 x 1,25 = 1.704,0 thousand million

Step 2 – Calculate the trade balance in 2011

In 2011, Trade Balance decreased by 25%, so we need to multiply it with 0,75 (marking a 25% decrease)

-202,0 x 0,75 = -151,5 thousand million

Step 3 – Calculate total exports in 2011

1704,0 – 151,5 = 1.552,5 thousand million

Step 4 – Calculate the % change in exports between 2006 and 2011

1.552,5/1.161,2 = 1,336978987 (33,698%)

## Pregunta 15

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDp3bG0zNy1ZRmpFcXR3d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=5

### Enunciado

In the EU in social sciences, the ratio of male students was 0,80 for each female student. What percentage of female graduates studied social science?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0015-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 21,0%
- **B.** 34,9%
- **C.** 25,8%
- **D.** 28,6%
- **E.** 32,3%

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **E**: 32,3%

Step 1 – Work out the number of students who graduated in social science in the EU in 2010

4.400.000 x 0,349 = 1.535.600

Step 2 – Work out the number of females who graduated in social science

Ratio is 0,8 to 1,0 so total parts is 1,8

1.535.600 x 1,0/1,8 =853.111,1111 (females 1 part out of 1,8)

Step 3 – Work out total number of female graduates

4.400.000 x 0,601 = 2.644.400

Step 4 – Work out share of female graduates who studied social science

853.111,1111/2.644.400 x 100 = 0,322610464 (32,3%)

## Pregunta 16

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDp3bG0zNy1ZRmpFcXR3d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=6

### Enunciado

In 2007, Poland and Romania accounted for 40% of the EU’s farming workforce. Poland and Romania’s labour force were in the ratio of 10:13. What country had 30% of Romania’s workforce?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0016-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** France
- **B.** Greece
- **C.** Hungary
- **D.** UK
- **E.** Spain

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **B**: Greece

Step 1 – Work out 40% of the total EU workforce

28.750 x 0,4 = 11.500

Step 2 – Work out the workforce in Romania

10:13 means 23 parts in total and Romania has 13 parts out of 23

11.500 x 13/23 = 6.500

Step 3 – Calculate 30% of Romania’s workforce and identify the country

6.500 x 0,3 = 1.950 Greece

## Pregunta 17

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDp3bG0zNy1ZRmpFcXR3d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=7

### Enunciado

In 2009, pig meat production was 2,8 times as high as beef production and 35 times higher than the production of sheep meat. How much higher was cereal production than meat production in the EU in 2009?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0017-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 6 times
- **B.** 3 times
- **C.** 12 times
- **D.** 9 times
- **E.** ¹⁄₉ times

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **D**: 9 times

Step 1 – Work out how much beef was produced in 2009

21.980/2,8 = 7.850

Step 2 – Work out how much sheep meat was produced in 2009

21.980/35 = 628

Step 3 – Calculate the total meat production in 2009

21.980 + 7.850 + 628 = 30.458

Step 4 – Work out how much more cereal was produced than meat

274.122/30.458 = 9

## Pregunta 18

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDp3bG0zNy1ZRmpFcXR3d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=8

### Enunciado

In 2007, 70% of the total turnover of postal operators in the EU was created by Germany, France, the UK and Italy. Given that the postal turnover for the EU as a whole was 0,5% of the EU GDP, what was the EU GDP in 2007?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0018-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 7.792.000 million Euro
- **B.** 1.113.142 million Euro
- **C.** 111.314.286 million Euro
- **D.** 11.131.429 million Euro
- **E.** 1.113.142.857 million Euro

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **D**: 11.131.429 million Euro

Step 1 – Work out the turnover in Germany, France, Italy and UK in 2007

Germany: 2.300.000 x 0,0054 = 12.420 million (be careful with the percentage!)

France: 1.800.000 x 0,0068 = 12.240 million

Italy: 1.500.000 x 0,0032 = 4.800 million

UK: 1.900.000 x 0,005 = 9.500 million

Step 2 – Work out the total postal turnover in the EU in 2007

12.240 + 12.240 + 4.800 + 9.500 = 38.960 is 70% of total EU turnover

38.960/0,7 = 55.657,14286

Step 3 – Work out the EU GDP in 2007

55.657,14286/0,005 = 11.131.428,57 million

## Pregunta 19

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDp3bG0zNy1ZRmpFcXR3d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=9

### Enunciado

Between 1990 and 2011, the total electricity generated from renewable sources increased by 119%. How much electricity was generated from renewable sources in 1990?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0019-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 11.987
- **B.** 11.124
- **C.** 20.472
- **D.** 22.061
- **E.** 10.642

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **B**: 11.124

Step 1 – Work out how much electricity was generated in 2011

Hydropower: 1.600 x 0,83 = 1.328 (100 – 17 = 83%)

Wind power: 950 x 1,2 = 1.140

Solar power: 360 x 1,90 = 684

Other: 21.000 x 1,01 = 21.210

Step 2 – Total the electricity generated in 2011

1.328 + 1.140 + 684 + 21.210 = 24.362

Step 3 – Work out the electricity generated in 1990

24.362/2,19 = 11.124,20091

## Pregunta 20

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDp3bG0zNy1ZRmpFcXR3d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=10

### Enunciado

In 2011, how many households in Turkey had children as a percentage of households in Europe with children?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0020-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 19,9%
- **B.** 17,8%
- **C.** 17,0%
- **D.** 30,2%
- **E.** 9,3%

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **B**: 17,8%

Step 1 – Work out the percentage of households have children in Turkey and from this the number of households

2,3 + 38,5 + 19,5 = 60,3%

So 19.600 x 0,603 = 11.818,8 thousand households

Step 2 – Work out the percentage of households have children in Europe and from this the number of households

4,4 + 21,0 + 6,0 = 31,4%

So 211.000 x 0,314 = 66.254 thousand households

Step 3 – Work out Turkey’s share as a percentage of Europe

11.818,8/66.254 x 100 = 17,83862106%

## Pregunta 21

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpQZnQ2SjU0Q09VVmZvQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=1

### Enunciado

EU imports of natural gas grew by 46% between 2001 and 2011. In 2011, Russia was the principal supplier with a 30% share of total natural gas, which compared to its 2001 share, is down by 15 percentage points. How much natural gas was imported from Russia in 2001?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0021-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 3.353 PJ
- **B.** 6.242 PJ
- **C.** 3.370 PJ
- **D.** 5.673 PJ
- **E.** 4.275 PJ

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **E**: 4.275 PJ

Step 1 – Work out the total imports in 2001

13.870/1,46 = 9.500

Step 2 – Work out Russia’s share in 2001

30 + 15 = 45%

Step 3 – Calculate amount imported from Russia in 2001

9.500 x 0,45 = 4.275

## Pregunta 22

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpQZnQ2SjU0Q09VVmZvQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=2

### Enunciado

In 2012, in Poland, the average number of days training for persons aged 25 to 64 years was 20 days. Females in that age group had an average 15 days training. How many days of training on average did males aged 25 to 64 years have in Poland?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0022-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 23,3 days
- **B.** 25 days
- **C.** 14 days
- **D.** 26,5 days
- **E.** 23⅓ days

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **E**: 23⅓ days

Step 1 – Calculate the total number of training days.

22.000 x 20 = 440.000

Step 2 – Calculate the number of females and males aged 25 to 64 years in Poland.

22.000 x 0,4 = 8.800 females

So there are 22.000 – 8,800 = 13.200 males

Step 3 – Work out total training days for females.

8.800 x 15 = 132.000

Step 4 – Work out average training days for males aged 25 to 64 years.

(440.000 – 132.000)/13.200 = 23 ⅓ days

Note: 23,3 is not the same as 23⅓ which is 23,333333 recurring.

Answer 23⅓ is more accurate.

## Pregunta 23

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpQZnQ2SjU0Q09VVmZvQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=3

### Enunciado

Between 2001 and 2008, EU net imports of solid fuels grew by 27%, followed by an 18% decrease between 2008 and 2009. From 2009 to 2010, they presented a further decline of 2 percentage points while in 2011 they marked an 8% increase compared to 2010. What were the net imports of solid fuels in 2001?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0023-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 235 million tonnes
- **B.** 171 million tonnes
- **C.** 172 million tonnes
- **D.** 798 million tonnes
- **E.** 169 million tonnes

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **C**: 172 million tonnes

Step 1 – Work out the net imports for 2010

190/1,08 = 175,9259259

Step 2 – Calculate the net imports for 2009

175,9259259/0,98 = 179,5162509

Step 3 – Calculate the net imports for 2008

179,5162509/0,82 = 218,9222572

Step 4 – Calculate the net imports for 2001

218,9222572/1,27 = 172,3797301 (million tonnes)

Note: Students should not round up or down between calculations

## Pregunta 24

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpQZnQ2SjU0Q09VVmZvQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=4

### Enunciado

In Sweden, the number of nights spent in hotels in 2013 was 10% of Spain’s. Between 2012 and 2013 the number of nights spent in hotels in Sweden grew by 1,5 percentage points more than the percentage change experienced by Spain in the same period. How many nights were spent in hotels in Sweden in 2012?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0024-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 2.777
- **B.** 2.557
- **C.** 2.797
- **D.** 2.762
- **E.** 2.443

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **D**: 2.762

Step 1 – Work out the number of nights spent in hotels in Sweden in 2013

30.240 x 0,1 = 3.024

Step 2 – Work out percentage change in the number of hotel nights in Spain between 2012 and 2013 and thus the percentage change for Sweden

30.240/28.000 = 1,08 (or 8%)

So 8 + 1,5 = 9,5% is Sweden’s percentage change

Step 3 – Work out the number of nights spent in hotels in Sweden in 2012

3.024/1,095 = 2.761,643836

## Pregunta 25

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpQZnQ2SjU0Q09VVmZvQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=5

### Enunciado

What was the highest public expenditure on education as a percentage of GDP in 2009?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0025-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 5%
- **B.** 6%
- **C.** 7%
- **D.** 8%
- **E.** 9%

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **D**: 8%

This can be worked out quickly using mental estimation and comparing across countries:

Cyprus: 75/1500 = 5%

Finland: 9/150 = 6%

Sweden: 21/300 = 7%

Denmark: 16/200 = 8%

Belgium: 18/300 = 6%

It is possible to exclude many countries before performing the calculations: Belgium can be quickly dismissed as it spends less than Sweden with the same GDP.

Cyprus can also be dismissed as its GDP is 1⁄10 of that of Finland so Cyprus’s expenditure is going to be a smaller percentage than Finland's.

Also, Finland has a GDP half of Sweden’s and yet its expenditure is less than half of Sweden’s

This leaves Sweden and Denmark.

## Pregunta 26

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpQZnQ2SjU0Q09VVmZvQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=6

### Enunciado

Between 1991 and 2011, the share of the population aged less than 15 years in Estonia decreased by 6,9 percentage points, while the share of the older population (65 years and above) increased by 5,4 percentage points. At the same time the population in Estonia decreased by 16%. How many people were aged between 15 and 64 years in 1991 in Estonia?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0026-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 1.071.600
- **B.** 1.024.391
- **C.** 761.290
- **D.** 741.800
- **E.** 1.051.305

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **E**: 1.051.305

Step 1 – Work out the share of the population aged between 15 and 64 in Estonia in 1991

100 – (15,3 + 6,9) – (17,1 – 5,4) = 66,1%

Or -6,9 + 5,4 = -1,5 so 67,6 – 1,5 = 66,1%

Step 2 – Work out the population in Estonia in 1991

1.336.000/0,84 = 1.590.476,19

Step 3 – Work out the number of people aged between 15 and 64 in Estonia in 1991

1.590.476,19 x 0,661 = 1.051.304,762

## Pregunta 27

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpQZnQ2SjU0Q09VVmZvQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=7

### Enunciado

In Cyprus, in 2009 the ratio of total milk production to total meat production was 18:7. Within meat production, the ratio of cattle to pigs to sheep was in the same ratio as in 2011. Total agricultural production in Cyprus in 2009 was 225 thousand tonnes. How much pig and sheep meat was produced in 2009?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0027-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 54 thousand tonnes
- **B.** 372 thousand tonnes
- **C.** 58 thousand tonnes
- **D.** 162 thousand tonnes
- **E.** 51 thousand tonnes

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 54 thousand tonnes

Step 1 – Calculate total meat production.

Total parts across milk and meat production: 18 + 7 = 25

So meat production is 7 parts of 25 of the total: 7/25 x 225 = 63

Step 2 – Work out the ratio of cattle to pigs to sheep in 2009

9 : 51 : 3 = 3 : 17 : 1

So the total parts is 3 + 17 + 1 = 21

Step 3 - Work out the share of pig and sheep meat production.

Pig meat is 17 parts of 21 = 17/21 x 63 = 51

Sheep meat is 1 part of 21 = 1/21 x 63 = 3

Step 4 – The total of pig and sheep meat production in 2009.

51 + 3 = 54 (thousand tonnes)

## Pregunta 28

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpQZnQ2SjU0Q09VVmZvQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=8

### Enunciado

What was the total percentage growth rate in the transport of goods in Belgium between 2011 and 2012?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0028-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** +12,6%
- **B.** -16,8%
- **C.** -10,0%
- **D.** +10,0%
- **E.** +16,8%

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **D**: +10,0%

Step 1 - Work out the national, international and transit totals in 2011

National: 48.675/0,977 = 49.820,88025 (100 – 2,3 = 97,7%)

International: 129.323/1,131 = 114.343,9434

Transit: 12.290/1,397 = 8.797,423049

Step 2 – Work out the total transport in 2011 and 2012

2011 49.820,88025 + 114.343,9434 + 8.797,423049 = 172.962,2467

2012 48.675 + 129.323 + 12.290 = 190.288

Step 3 – Calculate the percentage change

190.288/172.962,2467 = 1,100170723 (+10%)

## Pregunta 29

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpQZnQ2SjU0Q09VVmZvQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=9

### Enunciado

In 2010, it cost the Netherlands €100 to process each asylum applicant. The cost per applicant has reduced by 4% every year since then. How much did the Netherlands spend on asylum applicants in 2013?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0029-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** €326.468
- **B.** €326.454
- **C.** €324.720
- **D.** €354.240
- **E.** €340.070

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: €326.468

Steps 1 to 3 – Find the cost per applicant in 2013.

2011: 100 x 0,96 = 96

2012: 96 x 0,96 = 92,16

2013: 92,16 x 0,96 = 88,4736

Step 4 –

Find the total amount spent per applicant in 2013

3.690 x 88,4736 = €326.467,58

Tip 1:

Steps 1 to 3 can be completed in one step by using the following formula to work out the compound percentage over three years.

Any student who knows this can save time. 100 x (0,96)3 = €88,47

(To calculate (0,96)3 on a calucalator simply do 0,96*0,96*0,96)

Tip 2:

If the student rounds down the final percentage in Step 3 to 88,47, he/she will arrive at the answer €326.454.

You should not round intermediate answers.

## Pregunta 30

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpQZnQ2SjU0Q09VVmZvQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=10

### Enunciado

In France, there was a 24% decline in transport volumes between 2008 and 2009 followed by a 32% recovery in 2010. What was the percentage change in transport volumes in France in between 2008 and 2012?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0030-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** -4,5%
- **B.** +53,9%
- **C.** +4,5%
- **D.** +12,3%
- **E.** +1,8%

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **C**: +4,5%

Step 1 - Work out the transport volumes in France in 2009

72.647/1,32 = 55.035,60606

Step 2 – Work out transport volumes in France in 2008

55.035,60606/0,76 = 72.415,27113 (100 – 24 = 76%)

Step 3 – Calculate the percentage change between 2008 and 2012

75.701/72.415,27113 = 1,045373425 (4,5%)

## Pregunta 31

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDo3ZHlWMVA0QlNFTkI4d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=1

### Enunciado

In 2011, total greenhouse gas emissions in the EU had dropped by 20% from 1990. Transport was the only sector to increase from 1990, rising by 20%. Given that agriculture and industry reduced by 30%, and waste and solvent decreased by 50% in the same period, by what percentage has the energy sector increased or decreased between 1990 and 2011?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0031-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 75% decrease
- **B.** 67% decrease
- **C.** 25% decrease
- **D.** 75% increase
- **E.** 25% increase

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **C**: 25% decrease

Step 1 – Work out the values for 2011

Total: 5.000 x 0,8 = 4.000

Transport: 800 x 1,2 = 960 (10% is 80 so 800 + (2 x 80) = 960)

Agriculture: 600 x 0,7 = 420 (10% is 60 so 600 – (3 x 60) = 420)

Industry: 500 x 0,7 = 350 (10% is 50 so 500 – (3 x 50) = 350)

Waste: 200 x 0,5 = 100

Solvent: 20 x 0,5 = 10

Step 2 – Work out the energy figure for 2011

4.000 – (960 + 420 + 350 + 100 + 10) = 2.160

Step 3 – Work out the percentage increase/decrease

2.160/2.880 = 75% so it has decreased by 25%

Note: no calculator is required. Answers (d) and (e) can be discounted as they are increases.

## Pregunta 32

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDo3ZHlWMVA0QlNFTkI4d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=3

### Enunciado

In 2010, Germany, the UK, Italy and the Czech Republic accounted for 55% of businesses in the IT sector in the EU but accounted for 15 percentage points less of total turnover in the EU. What was the average turnover per IT business in the EU in 2010?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0033-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** €27,7 million
- **B.** €10,1 million
- **C.** €10,4 million
- **D.** €8,9 million
- **E.** €6,4 million

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **C**: €10,4 million

Step 1 – Work out the total number of IT businesses in the EU in 2010

(4.250 + 9.500 + 7.800 + 5.950)/0,55 = 50.000

Step 2 – Calculate the turnover in the EU in 2010

Percentage of total is 55 – 15 = 40%

(Remember, 15 here represents 15 percentage points less of total turnover in the EU)

(20.000 + 104.000 + 34.000 + 50.000)/0,4 = 520.000 million

Step 3 – Calculate average turnover per IT business

520.000/50.000 = 10,4 million

## Pregunta 33

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDo3ZHlWMVA0QlNFTkI4d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=7

### Enunciado

In 2011, the volume of fish catches in the EU fell by 2% from 2010. This represented a decline of 40% since 1995. What was the difference in the weight of catches between 1995 and 2011?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0037-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 3.266.667 tonnes
- **B.** 3.401.361 tonnes
- **C.** 3.400.000 tonnes
- **D.** 7.350.000 tonnes
- **E.** 3.166.667 tonnes

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 3.266.667 tonnes

Step 1 – Work out the total number of catches in the EU in 2011

5.000.000 x 0,98 = 4.900.000

Step 2 – Work out the total number of catches in the EU in 1995

4.900.000/0,6 = 8.166.666,667 (a 40% reduction since 1995)

Step 3 – Work out the difference between 1995 and 2011

8.166.666,667 – 4.900.000 = 3.266.666,667

## Pregunta 34

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDo3ZHlWMVA0QlNFTkI4d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=8

### Enunciado

In 2011, total EU milk production was 240 million tonnes. The amount of cheese produced in the EU was in the same proportion as that of Germany’s cheese production to the proportion of Germany’s total milk products in 2011. The amount of butter produced in the EU was in the same proportion to that of France’s production of butter as a proportion of France’s total milk products in 2011. And the amount of cream produced as in the same proportion to Italy’s production of cream as a proportion of Italy’s total milk products. The remaining was used as drinking milk. How much drinking milk was produced in the EU in 2011?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0038-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 51 million tonnes
- **B.** 48 million tonnes
- **C.** 40 million tonnes
- **D.** 76 million tonnes
- **E.** 76 thousand tonnes

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **D**: 76 million tonnes

Step 1 – Calculate how much cheese was made in the EU.

10.000/30.000 x 240 = 80 million tonnes

Step 2 – Calculate how much butter was produced in the EU.

6.250/25.000 x 240 = 60 million tonnes

Step 3 – Work out how much cream was produced in the EU.

1.000/10.000 x 240 = 24 million tonnes

Step 4 – From this, work out how much drinking milk was produced.

240 – (80 + 60 + 24) = 76 million tonnes

Tip: You could work out the percentages for each and take away from 100%

100% - (33⅓ % + 25% + 10%) = 31⅔ %

31⅔ x 240 = 76 million tonnes

## Pregunta 35

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDo3ZHlWMVA0QlNFTkI4d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=10

### Enunciado

In 2009, of Finland’s catch, 65% was fish with the remainder being crustaceans. Fish was priced at €2500 per 1 tonne and crustaceans were priced at €3500 per 1 tonne. How much did Finland turnover in 2009?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0040-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 444.600.000
- **B.** 444.600
- **C.** 253.500
- **D.** 191.100
- **E.** 491.400.000

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 444.600.000

Step 1 – Calculate the number of fish caught in 2009.

156 x 0,65 = 101,4

So the number of crustaceans caught = 156 – 101,4 = 54,6

Step 2 – Calculate the value of fish caught.

101,4 x 2500 x 1.000 = 253.500.000

Step 3 – Calculate the value of crustaceans caught.

54,6 x 3500 x 1.000 = 191.100.000

Step 4 – Work out the total turnover for Finland

253.500.000 + 191.100.000 = 444.600.000

## Pregunta 36

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDo3ZHlWMVA0QlNFTkI4d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=12

### Enunciado

Compared with 2010, Slovenia recorded the greatest increase in inland freight transport relative to GDP, with its index ¹⁰⁄₉ higher in 2011. In Denmark, the ratio of inland freight transport to GDP fell between 2010 and 2011 by 10%. By what percentage was Slovenia’s index larger than Denmark’s?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0042-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 50,1%
- **B.** 200%
- **C.** 60,7%
- **D.** 97,8%
- **E.** 97,5%

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **E**: 97,5%

Step 1 – Calculate the index in Slovenia in 2011

144 x  ¹⁰⁄₉ = 160

Step 2 – Calculate the index in Denmark

90 x 0,9 = 81

Step 3 – Work out the percentage difference

160/81 = 1,975308642

In percent: 1,975308642-1 = 97,5%

## Pregunta 37

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDo3ZHlWMVA0QlNFTkI4d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=16

### Enunciado

In 2009, how many more births would be required to meet the ideal fertility rate of 2,1 births per woman?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0046-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 107.466
- **B.** 107.466.079
- **C.** 105.657
- **D.** 105.657.000
- **E.** 151.800.000

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **D**: 105.657.000

Step 1 – Work out the number of births in 2009

1,69 x 257.700 = 435.513 thousand

Step 2 – Work out the ideal number of births in 2009

2,1 x 257.700 = 541.170 thousand

Step 3 – Work out the difference

541.170 – 435.513 = 105.657 thousand

## Pregunta 38

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDo3ZHlWMVA0QlNFTkI4d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=17

### Enunciado

The forest that Cyprus planted in 2011 has been planted as a square. In 2012, Cyprus decided to increase the sides of this square plantation by 10%. By how much did the area of this forest increase in 2012?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0047-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 10%
- **B.** 100%
- **C.** 21%
- **D.** 17%
- **E.** 45%

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **C**: 21%

Step 1 – Calculate the size of one side of the square Cyprus forest in 2011.

√16 = 4 km

Step 2 – Calculate how much the side of the forest increased in 2012.

4 x 1,10 = 4,4 km

Step 3 – Calculate the area of the forest in 2012.

4,4 x 4,4 = 19,36 km2

Step 4 – Work out how much the area has increased from 2011 to 2012.

(19,36 – 16)/16 x 100 = 21% or

19,36/16 x 100 = 121% (or 21% increase)

Tip: You could work out the linear scale factor and from this the area scale factor

Ratio of sides of squares: 1,1/1

Ratio of areas = (1,1/1)2 = 1,21/100 or 21% increase

## Pregunta 39

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDo3ZHlWMVA0QlNFTkI4d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=18

### Enunciado

In 2013 compared to 2012, youth unemployment in the EU decreased by 120.000. How many more young people were unemployed in 2013 compared to 2010?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0048-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 552.000
- **B.** 240.000
- **C.** 52.174
- **D.** 168.000
- **E.** 672.000

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 552.000

Step 1 – Work out how much 120.000 represents as a percentage

120.000 = 0,5% (23,6 – 23,1 = 0,5%)

So 120.000/0,5 = 1%

Step 2 – Work out the percentage points difference in youth unemployment between 2013 and 2010

23,1 – 20,8 = 2,3%

Step 3 – Work out how many people this represents

120.000/0,5 x 2,3 = 552.000

Note: you can also work this out by finding the total and then working out the percentage unemployed, eg

120.000/0,5 x 100 = 24.000.000 is 100%

24.000.000 x 0,231 = 5.544.000 in 2010

24.000.000 x 0,208 = 4.992.000 in 2013

5.544.000 – 4.992.000 = 552.000

But the approach above is quicker.

## Pregunta 40

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDo3ZHlWMVA0QlNFTkI4d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=19

### Enunciado

In 2010, UK had ⅕ of the field crops of Spain, twice as many horticulture holdings as Sweden and 25% of permanent crops as Austria. Given UK total holdings were 186.800 in 2010, how much was grazing livestock?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0049-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 40.800
- **B.** 46.075
- **C.** 140.725
- **D.** 78.200
- **E.** 146.000

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **C**: 140.725

Step 1 – Find the UK field crops.

204.000/5 = 40.800

Step 2 – Calculate the UK horticulture.

1.000 x 2 = 2.000

Step 3 – Calculate the UK permanent crops.

13.100 x 0,25 = 3.275

Step 4 – Work out the UK grazing livestock.

186.800 – (40.800 + 2.000 + 3.275) = 140.725

## Pregunta 41

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDo3ZHlWMVA0QlNFTkI4d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=20

### Enunciado

In 2012, if a male earns an average of €25 per hour in Slovenia and Spain, how much would a female earn on average for 10 hours of work in each country (20 hours total), based on the gender pay gap? (negative % represents females earning more than males)?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0050-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 435 Euros
- **B.** 565 Euros
- **C.** 43,5 Euros
- **D.** 56,5 Euros
- **E.** 510 Euros

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 435 Euros

Step 1 – Work out how much a female would earn in Slovenia for 10 hours work

25 x 1,06 = €26,5 per hour

So 26,5 x 10 = 265 Euros for 10 hours work

Step 2 – Work out how much a female would earn in Spain for 10 hours work

25 x 0,68 = €17 per hour

So 17 x 10 = 170 Euros for 10 hour works

Step 3 – Add the two together

265 + 170 = 435 Euros

## Pregunta 42

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDo3ZHlWMVA0QlNFTkI4d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=21

### Enunciado

What was the difference in birth rate (number of births per 1.000 inhabitants) between 1960 and 2011 in the EU?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0051-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 42,8
- **B.** 0,4
- **C.** 400
- **D.** 0,00835
- **E.** 8,35

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **E**: 8,35

Step 1 – Work out the birth rate in 2011

500,00/1.000 = 0,5 as birth rate is per 1.000 inhabitants

5,2/0,5 = 10,4 number of births per 1.000 inhabitants

Step 2 – Work out the birth rate in 1960

400,0/1.000 = 0,4 as birth rate is per 1.000 inhabitants

7,5/0,4 = 18,75 number of births per 1.000 inhabitants

Step 3 – Work out the difference

18,75 – 10,40 = 8,35

## Pregunta 43

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDo3ZHlWMVA0QlNFTkI4d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=23

### Enunciado

In 2011, fishery production in Norway was 3,6% higher than its level in 2007. In contrast, fishery production in Iceland in 2011 was a fifth below its level of 2007. What was the % increase or decrease in total fishery production in Norway together with Iceland between 2007 and 2011?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0053-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 3,96% increase
- **B.** 51,0% decrease
- **C.** 4,12% decrease
- **D.** 3,96% decrease
- **E.** 51,0% increase

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **D**: 3,96% decrease

Step 1 – Work out fishery production in Norway in 2007

3.300.000/1,036 = 3.185.328,185

Step 2 – Work out fishery production in Iceland in 2007

1.200.000/0,8 = 1.500.000 (⅕ = 20% below)

Step 3 – Work out total production in Norway and Iceland in 2007 and 2011

2007: 3.185.328,185 + 1.500.000 = 4.685.328,185

2011: 1.200.000 + 3.300.000 = 4.500.000

Step 4 – Work out % difference between 2011 and 2007

4.500.000/4.685.328,185 x 100= 96,04449939

So 100 – 96,04449939 = 3,95550061% decrease

## Pregunta 44

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDo3ZHlWMVA0QlNFTkI4d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=24

### Enunciado

In 2011, UK invested in an initiative to help those unemployed, allocating a fixed amount per unemployed person, depending on the age group. The UK decided to continue this investment into 2012 but unemployment in 15 – 24 years is expected to reduce by 500 thousand, unemployment in 25 – 54 years is expected to increase by 300 thousand and the unemployment in 55 – 64 years is expected to increase by 100 thousand. How much did the UK invest across the three age groups in 2012?

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** €881.000
- **B.** €890.000
- **C.** €874.000
- **D.** €882.000
- **E.** €883.000

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **C**: €874.000

Step 1 – Work out how much was invested per age group in 2011.

360.000/4.000 = €90 (15 – 24 years)

520.000/6.500 = €80 (25 – 54 years)

10.000/200 = €50 (55 – 64 years)

Step 2 – Calculate the amount of unemployed in each category in 2012.

4.000 – 500 = 3.500 (15 – 24 years)

6.500 + 300 = 6.800 (25 – 54 years)

200 + 100 = 300 (55 – 64 years)

Step 3 – Calculate the amount invested in 2012.

(3.500 x 90) + (6.800 x 80) + (300 x 50) = 874.000

## Pregunta 45

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDo3ZHlWMVA0QlNFTkI4d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=25

### Enunciado

During the period, 2002 to 2006, the total number of EU patent applications increased by a constant 2,8% per annum, while during the period 2006 to 2010, the reduction in number of patent applications was a constant 1,3% per annum. How many patent applications were there in 2010?

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 60.417
- **B.** 32.998
- **C.** 54.449
- **D.** 30.682
- **E.** 72.896

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **C**: 54.449

Step 1 – Calculate the growth in EU patent applications between 2002 and 2006

51.375 x 1,0284 = 57.375,21071

(2,8% x 4 years = 1,0284)

Step 2 – Calculate the reduction in EU applications between 2006 and 2010

57.375,21071 x 0,9874 = 54.449,37564

(reduction of 1,3% for 4 years = (100%-1,3%)4 = 0,9874

Note: The student can calculate each year separately, but the use of compound percentages is far quicker

Also observe that the technology patent applications are only a subgroup of all applications and therefore not relevant to the questions as they are already included among "patent applications"

## Pregunta 46

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDo3ZHlWMVA0QlNFTkI4d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=26

### Enunciado

In 2006, Spain missed its target recycling rate by 4 percentage points but improved its recycling rate by 15% in 2007. In 2007, the number of end-of-life vehicles in Spain was 90% of the 2006 figure. What was the difference in number of recycled vehicles between 2006 and 2007 in Spain?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0056-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 25.270 decrease
- **B.** 25.536 increase
- **C.** 66.500 decrease
- **D.** 25.536 decrease
- **E.** 25.270 increase

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **E**: 25.270 increase

Step 1 – Work out how many end-of-life vehicles were recycled in Spain in 2006

80 – 4 = 76% (missed target by 4 percentage points)

950.000 x 0,76 = 722.000

Step 2 – Work out how many end-of-life vehicles were in Spain in 2007

950.000 x 0,9 = 855.000

Step 3 – Work out recycling rate in Spain and number of end-of-life vehicles recycled in 2007

76 x 1,15 = 87,4

So 855.000 x 0,874 = 747.270

Step 4 – Work out difference in number of end-of-life vehicles recycled between 2006 and 2007

747.270 – 722.000 = 25.270

## Pregunta 47

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDo3ZHlWMVA0QlNFTkI4d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=27

### Enunciado

In 2007, 730 out of 1.000 people questioned in a survey about obstacles to participation in training cited lack of time or conflict with work schedule, 180 cited expense and remainder cited distance as the main issue. In a 2009 survey, the number who cited expense as the main obstacle had gone up by 84 but expense’s relative percentage share of the total obstacles had gone up by a third. How many people took part in the 2009 survey?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0057-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 1.100
- **B.** 1.240
- **C.** 1.250
- **D.** 1.467
- **E.** 792

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 1.100

Step 1 – Work out the share of obstacles as a percentage of the total in 2007.

180/1.000 = 18%

Step 2 – Work out what percentage share expenses were of the total in 2009.

18 + (⅓ x 18) = 24%

Step 3 – Work out how many people cited expense as the main obstacle in 2009.

180 + 84 = 264

Step 4 – Calculate how many people were in the 2009 survey.

264/0,24 = 1.100

## Pregunta 48

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDo3ZHlWMVA0QlNFTkI4d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=28

### Enunciado

In 2010, one in every 12 holdings in the EU maintained hedges. What percentage of total EU holdings maintained their stone walls in 2010?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0058-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 8,3%
- **B.** 9,3%
- **C.** 6,9%
- **D.** 12,2%
- **E.** 11,8%

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **C**: 6,9%

Step 1 – Work out the total number of hedges maintained in the EU in 2010

279.000/0,30 = 930.000 (using the % of total EU for France)

Step 2 – Work out the total number of holdings in the EU in 2010

930.000 x 12 = 11.160.000

Step 3 – Work out the total number of stone walls maintained in the EU in 2010

46.500/0,06 = 775.000 (using the % of total EU for France)

Step 4 – Calculate the % of stone walls maintained in the EU as a share of the total holdings

775.000/11.160.000 x 100 = 6,9444444

## Pregunta 49

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDo3ZHlWMVA0QlNFTkI4d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=29

### Enunciado

What has been the overall percentage decline in the absolute number of marriages in the EU between 1970 and 2010?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0059-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 63,9%
- **B.** 17,8%
- **C.** 36,1%
- **D.** 105,9%
- **E.** 41,3%

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **C**: 36,1%

Step 1 – Work out the number of marriages in the EU in 2010

504.400.000 x 4,4/1.000 = 2.219.360 (marriage rate is per 1.000 inhabitants)

Step 2 – Work out the number of marriages in the EU in 1970

439.900.000 x 7,9/1.000 = 3.475.210

Step 3 – Work out the percentage decline in marriages

2.219.360/3.475.210 x 100 = 63,8626155%

So 100 – 63,8626155 = 36,1373845% decrease in marriages

## Pregunta 50

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDo3ZHlWMVA0QlNFTkI4d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=30

### Enunciado

How much bigger was Germany’s population than Sweden’s in 2010?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0060-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 49,5 million
- **B.** 81,7 million
- **C.** 56,8 million
- **D.** 72,5 million
- **E.** 62,9 million

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **D**: 72,5 million

Step 1 – Calculate Germany’s population in 2010

Number of patents/population (in millions) = patents per million inhabitants

So Number of patents/patents per million inhabitants = population in millions

21.724/265,6 = 81,79216867 million

Step 2 – Calculate Sweden’s population in 2010

2.865/306,7 = 9,341375937 million

Step 3 – Calculate the difference in populations

81,79216867 - 9,341375937 = 72,45079273 million

## Pregunta 51

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDo3ZHlWMVA0QlNFTkI4d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=32

### Enunciado

There was a 50% increase in organic farming in the EU between 2005 and 2009 and organic farming grew by a further 1,9 percentage points between 2009 and 2011. In 2011, the percentage of organic farming in the EU was 500% greater than that of Canada. What percentage of organic farming did Canada have in 2011?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0062-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 0,86%
- **B.** 27,5%
- **C.** 0,62%
- **D.** 0,92 %
- **E.** 1,86%

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **D**: 0,92 %

Step 1 – Work out the percentage increase for the EU between 2005 and 2009

2,4 x 1,5 = 3,6%

Step 2 – Work out the percentage increase for EU between 2009 and 2011

3,6 + 1,9 = 5,5%

Step 3 – Work out the percentage of organic farming in Canada in 2011

5,5 ÷ 6 = 0,9167 %

Note: We are told that in 2011, the percentage of organic farming in the EU was 500% greater than in Canada. This does not mean that the EU was simply 5 times greater — but 500% greater, which means 6 times as much. This is why we need to divide 5,5 with 6.

## Pregunta 52

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDo3ZHlWMVA0QlNFTkI4d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=34

### Enunciado

With 360.000 permits, Italy issued 45% of the permits issued for employment reasons in 2010. Total residence permits issued in 2010 was 12% less then it was 2008. How many residence permits were issued for family reasons in 2008?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0064-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 880.682
- **B.** 767.045
- **C.** 511.364
- **D.** 681.818
- **E.** 859.091

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **B**: 767.045

Step 1 – Work out the total permits issued for employment reasons in 2010

360.000/0,45 = 800.000 permits for employment reasons (as Italy issued 45% in 2010)

Step 2 – Work out the total number of permits issued in 2010

800.000/0,32 = 2.500.000 (as employment was 32% of total in 2010)

Step 3 – Calculate the total number of permits in 2008

2.500.000/0,88 = 2.840.909,091

Step 4 – Calculate the number of permits issued for family reasons in 2008

2.840.909,091 x 0,27 = 767.045,4545

## Pregunta 53

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDo3ZHlWMVA0QlNFTkI4d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=35

### Enunciado

In 1980, the EU measured the gap between the highest rate of live births and the lowest rate of live births. In 1990, this difference had decreased to 65% and by 2011 it had narrowed by a further ⅕. What was the difference between the highest and lowest rate of live births in 2011?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0065-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 1,70
- **B.** 1,105
- **C.** 0,884
- **D.** 0,221
- **E.** 0,7748

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **C**: 0,884

Step 1 – Identify the highest and lowest rates for 1980 and calculate the difference.

3,20 (Ireland) – 1,50 (Luxembourg) = 1,70

Step 2 – Calculate the difference in live birth rates in 1990.

1,70 x 0,65 = 1,105 (65% of 1,70)

Step 3 – Calculate the difference in live birth rates in 2011.

⅘ x 1,105 = 0,884

## Pregunta 54

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDo3ZHlWMVA0QlNFTkI4d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=36

### Enunciado

In 2011, social protection expenditure per capita in Portugal was a third of that spent in the Netherlands and the Netherlands’ expenditure per capita was nine times higher than Bulgaria’s. What was the ratio of social expenditure per capita between Bulgaria and Romania?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0066-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 1 : 63
- **B.** 7 : 9
- **C.** 3 : 7
- **D.** 1 : 7
- **E.** 1 : 3

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **B**: 7 : 9

Step 1 – Calculate the spending per capita in the Netherlands.

63 x 3 = 189

Step 2 – Work out the spending per capita in Bulgaria.

189/9 = 21

Step 3 – Work out the ratio of per capita spending between Bulgaria and Romania. We have already in Step 2 calculated tha the ratio per capita spending for Bulgaria was 21.

We can also in the table see that the per capita spending for Romania is 27 thus the ratio is 21 : 27.

To simplify this ratio, divide both by 3:

21 ÷ 3 = 7

27 ÷ 3 = 9

So the simplified ratio is:

7 : 9

## Pregunta 55

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDo3ZHlWMVA0QlNFTkI4d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=37

### Enunciado

The young age dependency ratio is calculated by dividing the percentage of young people (0 to 14 years old) by the percentage of people of working age (15 to 64 years old), and the old age dependency ratio is calculated by dividing the percentage of older people (65 or more years old) by the percentage of people of working age (15 to 64 years old). In 2011, young people made up 15,6% of the EU total population. What percentage share did older people account for of the EU total population in 2011?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0067-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 1,5%
- **B.** 39,3%
- **C.** 26,2%
- **D.** 17,5%
- **E.** 2,5%

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **D**: 17,5%

Step 1 – Identify the percentage share of the working-age population. We know that

15,6/(% working-age population) = 23,4

Step 2 – So rearranging the equation to find the percentage of the working-age population.

15,6/23,4 = 0,6666667 (66,7%)

Step 3 – Using the percentage of the working population, find the percentage of older people.

(% older people)/0,6666667 = 26,2

Step 4 - So rearranging the equation to find the percentage of older people

% older people = 26,2 x 0,6666667 = 17,466%

## Pregunta 56

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDo3ZHlWMVA0QlNFTkI4d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=38

### Enunciado

In 2011, the UK recorded a net surplus in service transactions which represented 70% of the total net surplus registered by the EU as a whole. Germany recorded debits which were 45% of the total debits for the EU. How much did the EU record as total credits in 2011?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0068-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 579 thousand million
- **B.** 523 thousand million
- **C.** 361 thousand million
- **D.** 204 thousand million
- **E.** 149 thousand million

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 579 thousand million

Step 1 – Work out the total net surplus for the EU

76,3/0,7 = 109 thousand million

Step 2 – Work out the total debits for the EU

211,5/0,45 = 470 thousand million

Step 3 – Calculate the total credits for the EU

470 + 109 = 579 thousand million

## Pregunta 57

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDo3ZHlWMVA0QlNFTkI4d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=39

### Enunciado

In 2008, food and beverages manufacturing contributed 26,0% of profit in the EU. What was the average profit per food and beverages manufacturing enterprise in the EU in 2008?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0069-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 7.277.205
- **B.** 7.277
- **C.** 72,77
- **D.** 727,72
- **E.** 727.721

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **E**: 727.721

Step 1 – Work out % profit for food and beverages manufacturing in 2008

750.000 x 0,26 = 195.000 million

Step 2 – Work out the number of enterprises in food and beverages manufacturing 2008

3.045 x 0,088 = 267,96 thousand

Step 3 – Calculate the average profit per enterprise

195.000.000.000/267.960 = 727.720,553

## Pregunta 58

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDo3ZHlWMVA0QlNFTkI4d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=40

### Enunciado

In 2010, France and Germany together accounted for ⅖ of the EU’s passenger rail travel. The number of passenger-kilometers travelled by France rail passengers was twice the level for Germany which in turn recorded a figure that was 2,5 times as high as that for the UK? How many passenger-kilometres were travelled by UK rail passengers in 2010?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0070-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 1.600
- **B.** 10.000
- **C.** 2.400
- **D.** 3.200
- **E.** 4.000

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 1.600

Step 1 – Calculate France and Germany’s share of EU passenger rail travel

⅖ x 30.000 = 12.000

Step 2 – Calculate Germany’s share of 12.000

12.000 x ⅓ = 4.000

Step 3 – Calculate UK’s passenger-kilometres

4.000/2,5 = 1.600

Note: there is no need for a calculator.

## Pregunta 59

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpBc3lDNmdMSGRVTXdfZ2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=1

### Enunciado

In 2011, total EU foreign direct investment (FDI) flows showed signs of recovery. Outward flows of FDI increased for the first time in four years, rising by 154% when compared with 2010. At the same time, inward flows of FDI more than doubled compared with the previous year – up 117%. What was the net difference between outward and inward FDI in 2011?

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 3.008 thousand million
- **B.** 2.188 thousand million
- **C.** 5.508 thousand million
- **D.** 2.188 million
- **E.** 3.008 million

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 3.008 thousand million

Step 1 – Calculate outward FDI for 2011

3.320 x 1,54 = 5.112,80 (54% increase)

3.320 + 5.112,80 = 8.432,80 (154% increase)

Or simply 3.320 x 2,54 (154% increase) = 8.432,80

Step 2 – Calculate inward FDI for 2011

2.500 x 1,17 = 2.925 (17% increase)

2.500 + 2.925 = 5.425 (117% increase)

Step 3 – Calculate difference

8.432,80 – 5.425 = 3.007,80 thousand million

Tip: You can complete steps 1 and 2 in one stage by using a multiplier:

For example: 3.320 x 2,54 = 8.432,80

## Pregunta 60

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpBc3lDNmdMSGRVTXdfZ2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=2

### Enunciado

Among partner countries and regions in 2011, the EU’s current account deficit was largest with China, which was twice as large as the deficit with Russia and five times the deficit with Japan What percentage of the total EU current account deficit of 242.800 million did these three countries represent?

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 50%
- **B.** 4%
- **C.** 400%
- **D.** 75%
- **E.** 85%

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **E**: 85%

Step 1 – Find the deficit with Russia.

121.400/2 = 60.700

Step 2 – Find the deficit with Japan.

121.400/5 = 24.280

Step 3 – Find the total deficit across the three countries.

121.400 + 60.700 + 24.280 = 206.380

Step 4 – Find the percentage of the total deficit this represents

206.380/242.800 x 100 = 85%

## Pregunta 61

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpBc3lDNmdMSGRVTXdfZ2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=4

### Enunciado

In 2012, the majority of EU countries recorded a higher gender pay gap in the private sector than in the public sector, apart from four countries as seen in the table below. For these four countries, what was the difference between the average public gender pay gap and the average private gender pay gap?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0074-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 1,975%
- **B.** 20,9%
- **C.** 5,225%
- **D.** 7,85%
- **E.** 15,4%

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **C**: 5,225%

Step 1 – Identify the four countries with a higher gender gap in the public sector than the private sector

Bulgaria, Latvia, Hungary and Sweden

Step 2 – For these four countries, calculate the average gender pay gap in the public sector

(20,9 + 17,5 + 24,4 + 13,6)/4 = 19,1 %

Step 3 – For these four countries, calculate the average gender pay gap in the private sector

(12,6 + 13,2 + 16,3 + 13,4)/4 = 13,875 %

Step 4 – Calculate the difference between the averages

19,1 – 13,875 = 5,225 %

## Pregunta 62

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpBc3lDNmdMSGRVTXdfZ2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=6

### Enunciado

In 2009, ⅓ of all maths, science & technology (MST) graduates were female and MST graduates made up 10% of all graduates. How many graduates did not study MST in 2009?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0076-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 4.050.000
- **B.** 1.350.000
- **C.** 450.000
- **D.** 4.500.000
- **E.** 135.000

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 4.050.000

Step 1 – Work out the total number of MST graduates in 2009.

150.000 x 3 = 450.000

Step 2 – Work out the total number of graduates in 2009.

450.000/10 x 100 = 4.500.000

Step 3 – Work out the number of graduates who did not study MST.

4.500.000 x 0,9 = 4.050.000

Tip: Students who have good mental arithmetic may be able to get to step 3 quicker by recognising that the total number of graduates is 10 x 450.000

## Pregunta 63

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpBc3lDNmdMSGRVTXdfZ2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=7

### Enunciado

Government spending on health in 2010 across Denmark, France, Netherlands and Norway had fallen by 25% compared with 2005. France spent 224.000 million in 2005. What percentage of the four countries' health spending did France account for in 2005?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0077-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 10%
- **B.** 15%
- **C.** 20%
- **D.** 40%
- **E.** 50%

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **D**: 40%

Step 1 – Calculate the total spending on health across the four countries

30.000 + 260.000 + 90.000 + 40.000 = 420.000 million

Step 2 – Work out the total spending on health in 2005.

420.000/75 x 100 = 560.000 million

Step 3 – Work out the percentage share of health spending by France in 2005.

224.000/560.000 = 40%

## Pregunta 64

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpBc3lDNmdMSGRVTXdfZ2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=8

### Enunciado

In 2012, Netherland’s loaded container transport grew by 12% from 2011 and represented 50% of total EU loaded transport. In 2012, loaded container movements were 2.5 times the number of empty container movements. How much empty container transport was there in the EU in 2012?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0078-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 157.696 thousand tonnes
- **B.** 298.240 thousand tonnes
- **C.** 575.232 thousand tonnes
- **D.** 372.800 thousand tonnes
- **E.** 2.609.600 thousand tonnes

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **B**: 298.240 thousand tonnes

Step 1 - Work out the loaded container volume in Netherlands in 2012

466.000 x 1,12 = 521.920 (thousand tonnes)

Step 2 – Work out total EU loaded container volumes in 2012

521.920 x 2 = 1.043.840 (thousand tonnes)

Step 3 – Calculate the total EU empty container volumes in 2012

1.043.840/3,5 = 298.240 (thousand tonnes)

-----

It’s important to note that we need to divide by 3,5, not 2,5.

This is because the number of loaded container movements was 2.5 times greater than the number of empty container movements.

Therefore, the total number of container movements (loaded + empty) is equal to:

1 empty movement + 2.5 loaded movements = 3.5 total movements per unit:

298.240 +(298.240*2,5)= 1.043.840 thousand tonnes

## Pregunta 65

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpBc3lDNmdMSGRVTXdfZ2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=10

### Enunciado

The ratio between the highest and lowest selling prices for chickens in 2012 is the same as the ratio between the selling prices of eggs in two other countries.  For these two countries, what is the ratio between the higher and lower selling prices for cows?

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 6:1
- **B.** 3:1
- **C.** 4:1
- **D.** 8:1
- **E.** 7:1

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **B**: 3:1

Step 1

Calculate the ratio between the highest and lowest selling prices for chickens.

Ratio is 375,00 : 62.50 (Luxembourg : Portugal) = 6 : 1

Step2

Find which pair of countries has the same 6:1 ratio.

To quickly find the values, multiply the lower values by 6 to find the pair of countries.

We can see that the only two countries that have the ratio 6:1 are UK and Greece as 21,00 : 3.50 = 6 : 1

Step 3

Identify the values for the higher and lower selling prices for Greece and UK and calculate the ratio

150 : 50 = 3:1 (Greece: UK)

## Pregunta 66

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpBc3lDNmdMSGRVTXdfZ2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=11

### Enunciado

In 2010, France, Spain, UK, and Germany represented one half of all land used in agriculture across the EU. What percentage was used for agricultural use in Poland, Romania and Italy?

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 55%
- **B.** 48%
- **C.** 6%
- **D.** 12%
- **E.** 24%

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **E**: 24%

Step 1 – Find the total utilised agricultural area across France, Spain, UK and Germany.

28.000 + 23.700 + 16.900 + 16.700 = 85.300

Step 2 – Calculate the total agricultural land in the EU.

85.300 x 2 = 170.600

Step 3 – Find the total utilised agricultural area across Poland, Romania and Italy.

14.500 + 13.300 + 12.800 = 40.600

Step 4 – Find the agricultural land in Poland, Romania and Italy as a percentage of the total EU agricultural land.

40.600/170.600 x 100 = 23,8%

## Pregunta 67

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpBc3lDNmdMSGRVTXdfZ2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=12

### Enunciado

The world population doubled between 1960 and 2000 and increased by a further 20% from 2000 to 2012. The world population is projected to grow by 35% by 2060 from 2012 figures. What is the predicted growth in world population between 1960 and 2060?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0082-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 2.333 million
- **B.** 5.400 million
- **C.** 3.720 million
- **D.** 6.720 million
- **E.** 9.830 million

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **D**: 6.720 million

Step 1 – Work out the world population in 1960 from 2012 figures

7.200/1,20 = 6.000 million in 2000

So 6.000/2 = 3.000 million in 1960

Step 2 – Calculate the predicted world population in 2060 from 2012 figures

7.200 x 1,35 = 9.720 million in 2060

Step 3 – Work out growth between 1960 and 2060

9.720 – 3.000 = 6.720 million

## Pregunta 68

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpBc3lDNmdMSGRVTXdfZ2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=13

### Enunciado

The table shows energy savings in the EU from 2010 to 2012, along with the target for 2020. What would be the difference in energy consumed between 2012 and 2020 if the energy savings were not achieved in either year?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0083-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 94,0
- **B.** 9,3
- **C.** 100,4
- **D.** 15,7
- **E.** 84,7

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **E**: 84,7

Step 1 - Work out energy consumed in 2012 if savings not achieved

100 – 10,49 = 89,51%

1.583,5/0,8951 = 1.769,076081

Step 2 – Work out the energy consumed in 2020 if savings not achieved

100 – 20 = 80%

1.483/0,8 = 1.853,75

Step 3 – Calculate the difference between the two

1.853,75 – 1769,076081 = 84,673919

## Pregunta 69

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpBc3lDNmdMSGRVTXdfZ2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=14

### Enunciado

In 2000, on average one (per 100.000) Italian physician was able to handle 28 hospital discharges. In 1990 there 20% fewer physicians but ⅛ more discharges in Italy. How many discharges would one physician (per 100.000) be able to handle on average in Italy in 1990?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0084-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 26
- **B.** 31
- **C.** 20
- **D.** 39
- **E.** 28

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **D**: 39

Step 1 – Calculate the number of physicians in 1990.

420 x 0,80 = 336

Step 2 – Calculate the number of hospital discharges in 1990

11.760 + (⅛ x 11.760) = 13.230

Step 3 – Calculate how many discharges on average one physician would handle in

13.230/336 = 39,375

## Pregunta 70

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpBc3lDNmdMSGRVTXdfZ2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=15

### Enunciado

The total bovine population has grown by 10% between 2004 and 2011. In the same period, female cattle aged 2 years and over have grown by 7,5%. What percentage of the total bovine population was female aged over 2 years in 2004?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0085-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 49%
- **B.** 63%
- **C.** 54%
- **D.** 45%
- **E.** 50%

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **E**: 50%

Step 1 - Work out the bovine population in 2004

(250 + 150 + 69.700 +17.900) = 88.000

88.000/1,1 = 80.000

Step 2 – Work out the female population aged 2 years and over in 2004

43.000/1,075 = 40.000

Step 3 – Calculate the share of females aged over 2 years as a percentage of the total bovine population

40.000/80.000 x 100 = 50%

## Pregunta 71

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpBc3lDNmdMSGRVTXdfZ2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=17

### Enunciado

In 2010, the number of healthy life years for men and women in the EU as a whole represented 80% and 75% of total life expectancy for men and women respectively. What is the difference between the total life expectancy between men and women?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0087-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 7,5 years
- **B.** 1,8 years
- **C.** 1,71 years
- **D.** 2,85 years
- **E.** 4,65 years

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 7,5 years

Step 1 – Work out what life expectancy for men

61,2/0,8 = 76,5 (80% of 76,5 = 61,2)

Step 2 – Work out the life expectancy for women.

63/0,75 = 84 (75% of 84 = 63)

Step 3 – Calculate the difference between women’s and men’s life expectancy.

84 – 76,5 = 7,5

## Pregunta 72

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpBc3lDNmdMSGRVTXdfZ2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=18

### Enunciado

The combined average length of education across France and Sweden was 18 years in 2005. In 2005, France had a population 6,5 times larger than Sweden’s population of 10 million. What was the average length of education in Sweden in 2005?

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 20,5 years
- **B.** 24,5 years
- **C.** 22,5 years
- **D.** 23 years
- **E.** 21 years

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **B**: 24,5 years

Step 1 – Work out France’s population

10 x 6,5 = 65 million

Step 2 – Work out the total number of education years across Sweden and France.

The total population in France and Sweden: 65 +10 = 75 million, so total education years is

(75 x 18) = 1.350 million

Step 3 – Calculate the total number of education years in France.

(65 x 17) = 1.105 million

So the total number of education years in Sweden is:

1.350 – 1.105 = 245 million

Step 4 – Work out the average number of years of education in Sweden.

245/10 = 24,5 years

## Pregunta 73

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpBc3lDNmdMSGRVTXdfZ2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=19

### Enunciado

Euro member states pledge to keep government debt below 60% of GDP. However, in 2011 many member states exceeded this threshold. Greece’s GDP in 2011 was €215.000 million. By how much did Greece need to reduce government debt (as an amount) to meet its target?

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** €237.790.000
- **B.** €129.000.000
- **C.** €366.790.000.000
- **D.** €237.790
- **E.** €237.790.000.000

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **E**: €237.790.000.000

Step 1 – Calculate Greece’s government debt in 2011.

1,706 x 215.000 = 366.790 million

Step 2 – Calculate 60% of GDP (government debt threshold).

215.000 x 0,6 = 129.000 million

Step 3 – Calculate by how much Greece needed to reduce government debt to meet its threshold.

366.790 – 129.000 = 237.790 million

## Pregunta 74

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpBc3lDNmdMSGRVTXdfZ2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=20

### Enunciado

In 2012, Belgium represented 2,5% of the total EU output value of the agricultural industry. In 2013, the total EU output value is expected to grow by 15%. Given France is expected to increase its total share by 5 percentage points, what is France’s expected output value in 2013?

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 102.000
- **B.** 97.750
- **C.** 117.300
- **D.** 391.000
- **E.** 102

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **C**: 117.300

Step 1 – Find the total EU output value for 2012. If 8.500 is 2,5%, then the total EU output value is 340.000 (8.500/2,5 x 100 = 340.000).

Step 2 – Calculate what percentage of the EU total France represents. 85.000/340.000 x 100 = 25%

Step 3 – Calculate the expected increase in total EU output value in 2013: 340.000 x 1,15 = 391.000

Remember, 1,15 represents the 15% expected growth.
