Step 4 – Calculate France’s expected share of the EU total: 25% + 5% = 30%

30% of 391.000 = 117.300

Tip: You can calculate France’s percentage share (in step 2) directly as it is ten times bigger than Belgium's (85.000/8.500 )

## Pregunta 75

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpBc3lDNmdMSGRVTXdfZ2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=21

### Enunciado

How much bigger is the area of China than the area of the EU?

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 132.081 km²
- **B.** 5.350.000 km²
- **C.** 5,35 km²
- **D.** 132.081.000.000 km²
- **E.** 132.081.000 km²

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **B**: 5.350.000 km²

Step 1 – Calculate the area of China

Population/area = population density

So Population/population density = area

1.353,6/141,0 = 9,6

9,6 x 1.000.000 = 9.600.000 km2 (as population is in millions)

Step 2 – Calculate the area of the EU using the method above

499,8/117,6 = 4,25

4,25 x 1.000.000 = 4.250.000 km2

Step 3 – Work out how much bigger China is than the EU

9.600.000 – 4.250.000 = 5.350.000 km2

## Pregunta 76

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpBc3lDNmdMSGRVTXdfZ2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=22

### Enunciado

The table shows the number of dairy cows and the average milk yield per cow. What was the difference in total milk production between the country with the highest yield per cow and the country with the lowest yield per cow in 2011 in tonnes (where 1 tonne is equivalent to 1.000 kilograms)?

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 5.875.200 tonnes
- **B.** 3.145.000 tonnes
- **C.** 3.145 tonnes
- **D.** 5.875 tonnes
- **E.** 4.085 tonnes

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **C**: 3.145 tonnes

Step 1 - Work out the milk production in the country with the highest yield per cow

8.500 x 580 = 4.930.000 kg (Denmark)

Step 2 – Work out the milk production in the country with the lowest yield per cow

5.100 x 350 = 1.785.000 kg (Lithuania)

Step 3 – Work out the difference

4.930.000 – 1.785.000 = 3.145.000 kg

Step 4 – Convert to tonnes

3.145.000/1000 = 3.145 tonnes

## Pregunta 77

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpBc3lDNmdMSGRVTXdfZ2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=23

### Enunciado

The country with the lowest levels of internet access in 2013 would like to grow this figure by 20 percentage points in the next two years. Given that its number of households is ¹⁄₉ of the size of the UK’s, which is 63 million, by how much does it under or over achieve against this target if it has 5,6 million households with internet in 2015 (assuming the population does not change)?

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 0,28 million over
- **B.** 0,42 million under
- **C.** 0,28 million under
- **D.** 1,06 million under
- **E.** 0,42 million over

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **E**: 0,42 million over

Step 1 – Identify the country with the lowest level of internet usage and add on 20 percentage points.

54 (Bulgaria) + 20 = 74%

Step 2 – Calculate the size of Bulgaria’s population.

63/9 = 7 million

Step 3 – Calculate the target internet users.

7 x 0,74 = 5,18 million

Step 4 - Calculate the difference between the target and the actual figure.

5,6 – 5,18 = 0,42 million

This means that if the actual number of households with internet is 5,6 million two years later it has overachieved the target with 0,42 million.

## Pregunta 78

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpBc3lDNmdMSGRVTXdfZ2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=24

### Enunciado

In 2009, GDP contracted considerably as a result of the global financial and economic crisis, but recovered across all countries by 8% in 2010. In Germany, GDP grew by 20% between 2001 and 2012. What was the percentage growth in GDP in Germany between 2009 and 2012?

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 10%
- **B.** 11%
- **C.** 12%
- **D.** 13%
- **E.** 14%

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **E**: 14%

Step 1 – Calculate the GDP in Germany in 2009.

2.538/1,08 = 2.350

Step 2 – Calculate the GDP in Germany in 2012.

2.232,5 x 1,2 = 2.679

Step 3 – Calculate the percentage growth in GDP between 2009 and 2012.

2.679/2350 x 100 = 1,14 (14% growth)

## Pregunta 79

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpBc3lDNmdMSGRVTXdfZ2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=25

### Enunciado

In 2010, the total pupils in upper secondary education in Sweden was ⅙ of the total pupils in the UK and in that year ¼ of UK pupils studied French. How many pupils in Sweden and UK studied French in 2010?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0095-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 825.000
- **B.** 110.000
- **C.** 3.300.000
- **D.** 935.000
- **E.** 247.500

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **D**: 935.000

Step 1 – Find the total pupils in the UK – six times that of Sweden:   550.000 x 6 = 3.300.000.

Step 2 – Find the number of UK pupils who studied French in 2010:   ¼ x 3.300.000 = 825.000

Step 3 – Find the number of Swedish pupils who studied French in 2010:  0,2 x 550.000 = 110.000

Step 4 – Find the total of UK and Swedish pupils who studied French in 2010: 825.000 + 110.000 = 935.000

## Pregunta 80

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpBc3lDNmdMSGRVTXdfZ2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=26

### Enunciado

Of the total business in the tourism industry in the EU in 2010, 56% were located in four countries - Italy, Spain, France and Germany. In terms of numbers employed, these four countries represented 49% of the total. What was the average number of people employed per business in Italy, Spain, France and Germany in 2010?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0096-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 4,7
- **B.** 4,1
- **C.** 4,0
- **D.** 5,4
- **E.** 5,2

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **B**: 4,1

Step 1 - Work out the number of business in France, Spain, Italy and Germany

3.389.000 x 0,56 = 1.897.840

Step 2 – Work out the number of persons employed in these four countries

15.915.500 x 0,49 = 7.798.595

Step 3 – Calculate the average number of people employed per business in these four countries

7.798.595/1.897.840 = 4,10919519

## Pregunta 81

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpBc3lDNmdMSGRVTXdfZ2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=27

### Enunciado

Total exports grew by 1% between 2012 and 2013 while imports shrunk by 3% in the same period. What was the trade balance in 2012?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0097-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 51,5
- **B.** 52,5
- **C.** 107,7
- **D.** 105,6
- **E.** 103,5

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 51,5

Step 1 – Calculate the total exports for 2012.

1.405,9/1,01 = 1391,98

Step 2 – Calculate the total imports for 2012.

1.300,3/0,97 = 1.340,515

Step 3 – From this, work out the trade balance for 2012

1.391,98 – 1.340,515 = 51,465

## Pregunta 82

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpBc3lDNmdMSGRVTXdfZ2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=28

### Enunciado

Female unemployment rates in 2008 were 76% of the rate in 2000 while male unemployment rates were 30% higher in 2012 than 2008. What was the difference in percentage points between male and female unemployment rates in 2008?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0098-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 0,4 percentage points
- **B.** 4,0 percentage points
- **C.** 5,3 percentage points
- **D.** 95,0 percentage points
- **E.** 5,0 percentage points

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 0,4 percentage points

Step 1 - Work out the female unemployment rate in 2008

10,0 x 0,76 = 7,6%

Step 2 – Work out the male unemployment rate in 2008

10,4/1,3 = 8,0%

Step 3 – Work out the difference in percentage points

8,0 – 7,6 = 0,4 percentage points

## Pregunta 83

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpBc3lDNmdMSGRVTXdfZ2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=29

### Enunciado

Milk storage tanks in Sweden have the same shape (that is, the corresponding lengths are in the same ratio) as those in Denmark apart from the fact that the average length of storage in Sweden is 3 meters shorter. What is the average volume of Sweden’s milk storage?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0099-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 80 l³
- **B.** 320l³
- **C.** 1280l³
- **D.** 160
- **E.** 62,5 l³

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 80 l³

Step 1 – Calculate the ratio of lengths of storage for Denmark and Sweden to give the linear scale factor.

6/3 = 2 (the linear scale factor)

Step 2 – Calculate the ratio of volumes based on the linear scale factor.

23 = 8 (volume scale factor) – the linear scale factor is cubed as volume has three dimensions

Step 3 – Calculate the volume of Sweden’s smaller storage facility based on the volume of Denmark’s milk storage.

640/8 = 80 l3

Tip: You need to remember to cube the linear scale factor as volume has three dimensions

## Pregunta 84

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpBc3lDNmdMSGRVTXdfZ2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=30

### Enunciado

In 2012, how many more individuals need to be employed for Norway to meet its target?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0100-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 2.560
- **B.** 160
- **C.** 120.000
- **D.** 2.560.000
- **E.** 160.000

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **E**: 160.000

Step 1 – Work out what 1% of Norway’s employed is

2.400/75 = 32 (thousand)

Step 2 – Work out the total employed if Norway met its target.

32 x 80 = 2.560 (thousand)

Step 3 – Calculate the difference between the target number of employed and the actual number of employed.

2.560 – 2.400 = 160 (thousand)

## Pregunta 85

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpBc3lDNmdMSGRVTXdfZ2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=32

### Enunciado

Total energy consumption increased between 1990 and 2012 by 0,9%. In the same period, the consumption of fossil fuels decreased by 35,4%, oil decreased by 12% and gas increased by 34,4%. By what percentage did renewables change between 1990 and 2012?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0102-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 163,9%
- **B.** 420,2%
- **C.** 59,0%
- **D.** 98,8%
- **E.** 117,3%

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **D**: 98,8%

Step 1 - Work out energy consumed in 2012

Total 1.376,7 x 1,009 = 1.389,0903

Fossil 453,2 x 0,646 = 292,7672 (100 – 35,4 = 64,6%)

Oil 549,9 x 0,88 = 483,912 (100 – 12 = 88%)

Gas 202,4 x 1,344 = 272,0256

Step 2 – Work out renewables consumed in 2012

1.389,0903 – (292,7672 + 483,912 + 272,0256) = 340,3855

Step 3 – Calculate the percentage change in renewable between 1990 and 2012

340,3855/171,2 = 1,988233061 (98,8%)

## Pregunta 86

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpBc3lDNmdMSGRVTXdfZ2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=33

### Enunciado

In 2009, construction and manufacturing accounted for a large percentage of total fatal accidents. By 2011, construction had reduced its share of fatal accidents by 5% and manufacturing had lowered its percentage share of fatal accidents by 10%. In 2011, the number fatal accidents were 5.580. How many were in the construction and manufacturing sectors?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0103-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 162
- **B.** 1.507
- **C.** 3.457
- **D.** 958
- **E.** 2.182

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **E**: 2.182

Step 1

Work out the percentage share of fatal accidents in construction in 2011.

26 x 0,95 = 24,7% (or 26 – (0,05 x 26) = 24,7%)

Step 2

Work out the percentage share of fatal accidents in manufacturing in 2011.

16 x 0,90 = 14,4% (or 16 – (0,10 x 16) = 14,4%)

Step 3

Work out the total share of construction and manufacturing in 2011.

24,7 + 14,4 = 39,1%

Step 4

Calculate the number of fatal accidents in construction and manufacturing in 2011.

5.580 x 0,391 = 2.181,78

## Pregunta 87

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpBc3lDNmdMSGRVTXdfZ2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=34

### Enunciado

Population growth is anticipated to be a constant 2% per annum in Italy and a constant 1% per annum in the UK. In what year is the population of Italy expected to exceed that of the UK?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0104-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 2013
- **B.** 2014
- **C.** 2015
- **D.** 2016
- **E.** 2017

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **D**: 2016

Step 1 – Calculate the % growth in the UK and Italy for year one (2013)

Italy 60.673 x 1,02 = 61.886,46

UK 62.751 x 1,01 = 63.378,51

Step 2, etc – Keep on calculating the percentage growth for both countries

until Italy’s population exceeds the UK’s.

For year two (2014):

Italy 61.886,46 x 1,02 = 63.124,1892

UK 63.378,51 x 1,01 = 64.012,2951

Italy                        UK

2013                61.886,46                   63.378,51

2014                63.124,19                   64.012,30

2015                64.386,67                   64.652,42

2016                65.674,41                   65.298,94

Tip: It is easier to calculate the compound percentages using 1,01n where n is the number of years. So for example, in year two (2014), Italy’s population will be:

60.673 x 1,022 = 63.124,1892

Using this approach, it is easy to use trial and error to find the correct year.

## Pregunta 88

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpBc3lDNmdMSGRVTXdfZ2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=36

### Enunciado

Having peaked in 2001 with 450.000 applications, the number of asylum applications within the EU fell by ⁴⁄₉ by 2006. By how much has the number of asylum applications risen between 2006 and 2011?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0106-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** ⅓
- **B.** ⅙
- **C.** ⅔
- **D.** ⅕
- **E.** ¼

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **D**: ⅕

Step 1 – Work out how many applications there were in 2006.

450.000 - ((⁴⁄₉)*450.000) = 250.000

Step 2 – Work out the difference between applications in 2011 and 2006.

300.000 – 250.000 = 50.000

Step 3 – Work out the growth in applications.

50.000/250.000 = ⅕

## Pregunta 89

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpBc3lDNmdMSGRVTXdfZ2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=38

### Enunciado

In 2010, the average number of months worked by working-age individuals in Bulgaria was 7,3 months, but the average number of months worked by females of working age was 7,8. Of Bulgaria’s working population, 1,4 million were female. What was the average number of months worked by the male working-age population?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0108-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 7,3
- **B.** 6,5
- **C.** 7,4
- **D.** 6,8
- **E.** 6,9

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **D**: 6,8

Step 1 – Find the total number of months worked in Bulgaria across the working-age population.

2,9 x 7,3 = 21,17 million

Step 2 – Find the total number of months worked by the female working-age population.

1,4 x 7,8 = 10,92 million

Step 3 – Calculate the total number of months worked by the male working-age population.

21,17 – 10,92 = 10,25 million

Step 4 – Work out the average number of months worked by the male working-age population.

10,25/(2,9 – 1,4) = 6,83333

## Pregunta 90

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpBc3lDNmdMSGRVTXdfZ2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=39

### Enunciado

In July 2013, the four-month moving average for Germany was 1,4 and in June 2013 it was 1,6. What was the percentage change in production for Germany in April 2013 and March 2013?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0109-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 1,7 and 2,9
- **B.** 1,5 and 2,0
- **C.** 0,9 and 1,1
- **D.** 1,5 and 2,3
- **E.** 1,7 and 2,8

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 1,7 and 2,9

Step 1 – Find the total on which the moving average was based in July 2013

We know that four month moving average was 1,4

1,4 x 4 = 5,6

Step 2 – Subtract from this total the three pieces of data you have for Germany to find April’s figure.

5,6 – (0,8 + 1,0 + 2,1) = 1,7 (Use the May, Jun and Jul figures)

Step 3 – Now using the same method find the total on which the moving average was based in June 2013.

1,6 x 4 = 6,4

Step 4 – Subtract from this total the three pieces of data you have to find March’s figure.

6,4 – (1,7 + 0,8 + 1,0) = 2,9 (Apr, May and Jun)

## Pregunta 91

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpBc3lDNmdMSGRVTXdfZ2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=40

### Enunciado

Germany’s total population has decreased by 2% between 2009 and 2013. By how many people has the total population reduced over this period?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0110-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 80.000.000
- **B.** 12.960.000
- **C.** 160.000
- **D.** 1.600.000
- **E.** 1.440

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **D**: 1.600.000

Step 1 – Calculate the population of Germany in 2009

72.000/90 x 100.000 = 80.000.000

Step 2 – Calculate the population of Germany in 2013

80.000.000 x 0,98 = 78.400.000

Step 3 – Work out the difference between the two populations

80.000.000 – 78.400.000 = 1.600.000

## Pregunta 92

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpIcGV4X3RxaUJFVXdLQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=8

### Enunciado

In 2011, UK invested in an initiative to help those unemployed, allocating a fixed amount per unemployed person, depending on the age group. The UK decided to continue this investment into 2012 but unemployment in 15 – 24 years is expected to reduce by 500 thousand, unemployment in 25 – 54 years is expected to increase by 300 thousand and the unemployment in 55 – 64 years is expected to increase by 100 thousand. How much did the UK invest across the three age groups in 2012?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0118-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** €881.000
- **B.** €890.000
- **C.** €874.000
- **D.** €882.000
- **E.** €883.000

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **C**: €874.000

Step 1 – Work out how much was invested per age group in 2011.

360.000/4.000 = €90 (15 – 24 years)

520.000/6.500 = €80 (25 – 54 years)

10.000/200 = €50 (55 – 64 years)

Step 2 – Calculate the amount of unemployed in each category in 2012.

4.000 – 500 = 3.500 (15 – 24 years)

6.500 + 300 = 6.800 (25 – 54 years)

200 + 100 = 300 (55 – 64 years)

Step 3 – Calculate the amount invested in 2012.

(3.500 x 90) + (6.800 x 80) + (300 x 50) = 874.000

## Pregunta 93

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpIcGV4X3RxaUJFVXdLQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=9

### Enunciado

The range shows the difference between the country with the highest life expectancy and the country with the lowest life expectancy. What is the ratio between the range for men and the range for women in 2011?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0119-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 4 : 5
- **B.** 9:11
- **C.** 11 : 9
- **D.** 5 : 4
- **E.** 3 : 4

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **B**: 9:11

Step 1 – Work out the range for men.

Highest is 65 + 18,5 = 83,5

(Sweden) and lowest is 65 + 14 = 79

(Lithuania) 83,5 – 79 = 4,5

Step 2 – Work out the range for women.

Highest is 65 + 22,8 = 87,8

(Spain) and lowest is 65 + 17,3 = 82,3

(Bulgaria) 87,8 – 82,3 = 5,5

Step 3 – Work out the ratio between men and women.

4,5 : 5,5 = 9:11

## Pregunta 94

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpIcGV4X3RxaUJFVXdLQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=11

### Enunciado

Over the period 2006 to 2013, the annual growth rate of the euro area house price index reached a maximum of 200% of Estonia’s Q2 2013 figure and a minimum of 1,1 percentage points below than Ireland’s Q1 2013 figure. What was the difference between the minimum and maximum growth rate from 2006 to 2013?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0121-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 5,8
- **B.** 10,8
- **C.** 3,6
- **D.** 7,9
- **E.** 1,1

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **B**: 10,8

Step 1 – Work out the house price index maximum

3,6 x 2  = 7,2

Step 2 – Work out the house price index minimum

-2,5 – 1,1 = -3,6

Step 3 – Calculate the difference between the maximum and minimum

7,2 – (-3,6) = 10,8

## Pregunta 95

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpIcGV4X3RxaUJFVXdLQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=14

### Enunciado

In 2010, 34% of the 20 million students in tertiary education were studying social sciences. Of this, 3,9 million were female. What percentage of students in tertiary education were males studying social science?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0124-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 34%
- **B.** 43%
- **C.** 19,5%
- **D.** 14,5%
- **E.** 22,5%

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **D**: 14,5%

Step 1 – Calculate the total number of students studying social sciences.

0,34 x 20 = 6,8 million

Step 2 – Calculate the number of males studying social science.

6,8 – 3,9 = 2,9 million

Step 3 – Work out the percentage of students in tertiary education that are males studying social science.

2,9/20 x 100 = 14,5%

## Pregunta 96

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpIcGV4X3RxaUJFVXdLQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=15

### Enunciado

In 2000, the number of students per teacher in Portugal is half that of France and the number of students per teacher in France is ⅘ of that of the UK. In 2000, the number of students in France was 16.000.000. What was the total number of students across Portugal, France and the UK in 2000?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0125-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 13.920.000
- **B.** 28.320.000
- **C.** 34.080.000
- **D.** 22.400.000
- **E.** 44.100.000

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **C**: 34.080.000

Step 1 – Work out the number of students per teacher in France.

16.000 to 1.000 (or 16.000.000 to 1.000.000) = 16 to 1

Step 2 – Work out the number of students per teacher ratio in Portugal.

16/2 = 8 so the ratio is 8 to 1

Step 3 – Work out the number of students per teacher in the UK.

16 x ⁵⁄₄= 20 (note: it is not ⁴⁄₅) so the ratio is 20 to 1

Step 4 – Calculate the total number of students across France, Portugal, and the UK.

16.000.000 + (260.000 x 8) + (800.000 x 20) = 34.080.000

## Pregunta 97

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpIcGV4X3RxaUJFVXdLQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=18

### Enunciado

An average car in the UK does 14 km per litre and a litre of petrol costs £1,30 per litre. How much does an average family, living 50 km from the sea, spend on petrol on round trips to the seaside each summer in Euros (£1 = 1,20 Euros)?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0128-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** €270
- **B.** €390
- **C.** €326
- **D.** €195
- **E.** €66

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **B**: €390

Step 1 – Work out the number of km travelled.

50 x 35 x 2 = 3.500 km (remember round trips)

Step 2 – Calculate how many liters used to travel this distance.

3.500/14 = 250 litres

Step 3 – Calculate the cost of the petrol used in £s.

250 x 1,30 = £325,00

Step 4 – Calculate the cost in Euros.

325,00 x 1,20 = €390

## Pregunta 98

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpIcGV4X3RxaUJFVXdLQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=29

### Enunciado

The table shows the number of dairy cows and the average milk yield per cow. What was the difference in total milk production between the country with the highest yield per cow and the country with the lowest yield per cow in 2011 in tonnes (where 1 tonne is equivalent to 1.000 kilograms)?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0139-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 5.875.200 tonnes
- **B.** 3.145.000 tonnes
- **C.** 3.145 tonnes
- **D.** 5.875 tonnes
- **E.** 4.085 tonnes

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **C**: 3.145 tonnes

Step 1 - Work out the milk production in the country with the highest yield per cow

8.500 x 580 = 4.930.000 kg (Denmark)

Step 2 – Work out the milk production in the country with the lowest yield per cow

5.100 x 350 = 1.785.000 kg (Lithuania)

Step 3 – Work out the difference

4.930.000 – 1.785.000 = 3.145.000 kg

Step 4 – Convert to tonnes

3.145.000/1000 = 3.145 tonnes

## Pregunta 99

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpIcGV4X3RxaUJFVXdLQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=30

### Enunciado

The combined average length of education across France and Sweden was 18 years in 2005. In 2005, France had a population 6,5 times larger than Sweden’s population of 10 million. What was the average length of education in Sweden in 2005?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0140-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 20,5 years
- **B.** 24,5 years
- **C.** 22,5 years
- **D.** 23 years
- **E.** 21 years

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **B**: 24,5 years

Step 1 – Work out France’s population

10 x 6,5 = 65 million

Step 2 – Work out the total number of education years across Sweden and France.

The total population in France and Sweden: 65 +10 = 75 million, so total education years is

(75 x 18) = 1.350 million

Step 3 – Calculate the total number of education years in France.

(65 x 17) = 1.105 million

So the total number of education years in Sweden is:

1.350 – 1.105 = 245 million

Step 4 – Work out the average number of years of education in Sweden.

245/10 = 24,5 years

## Pregunta 100

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpIcGV4X3RxaUJFVXdLQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=32

### Enunciado

The UK, Italy, the Netherlands and Spain handled 50% of total EU seaborne freight in 2011. The UK handled the largest amount. The ratio of the UK’s seaborne freight to Italy’s was 21:20 and the ratio of the Netherlands ’ seaborne freight to Spain’s was 9:8. How many million tonnes did these four countries handle in 2011?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0142-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 2.032 million tonnes
- **B.** 1.550 million tonnes
- **C.** 1.443 million tonnes
- **D.** 1.875 million tonnes
- **E.** 2.100 million tonnes

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **D**: 1.875 million tonnes

Step 1 – Work out Italy’s seaborne freight

If 525 is 21 parts out of the total, then one part is 525/21 = 25

So Italy’s freight is 20 x 25 = 500 (20 parts x 25)

Step 2 – Work out Spain’s seaborne freight

If 450 is 9 parts out the total, then one part is 450/9 = 50

So Spain’s freight is 8 x 50 = 400 (8 parts x 50)

Step 3 – Calculate the total seaborne freight across the four countries

525 + 500 + 450 + 400 = 1.875 million tonnes

Note: this can be carried out without a calculator by a student with very good mental calculation skills

## Pregunta 101

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpIcGV4X3RxaUJFVXdLQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=35

### Enunciado

In 2012, Europe had double the population density of the United States and a population 3,9 times the size of Brazil. What is the area of Europe?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0145-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 16.380.000 km²
- **B.** 58.500.000.000 km²
- **C.** 10.400.000 km²
- **D.** 10,4 km²
- **E.** 16,380 km²

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **C**: 10.400.000 km²

Step 1 – Work out the population density of Europe.

37,5 x 2 = 75

Step 2 – Work out the population of Europe.

200 x 3,9 = 780 million

Step 3 – Calculate the area of Europe

780.000.000/75 = 10.400.000 km2

## Pregunta 102

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpIcGV4X3RxaUJFVXdLQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=36

### Enunciado

In 2001, the unemployment rate for women in the EU was 9,6%, while the rate for men was 2 percentage points lower. By 2003, this gender gap had narrowed to 70% of the 2001 gap but the male unemployment rate was now higher than the female unemployment rate. The unemployment rate for men in 2003 was the same as the average of the male rate across Belgium, Denmark, Germany and France in 2011. What was the female unemployment rate in 2003?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0146-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 5,9 %
- **B.** 8,9 %
- **C.** 6,9 %
- **D.** 8,1 %
- **E.** 6,1 %

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **E**: 6,1 %

Step 1 – Work out the gender gap in 2003.

2 x 0,7 = 1,4 (70% of 2 percentage points)

Step 2 – Work out the male unemployment rate in 2003.

(7,1 + 7,6 + 6,2 +9,1)/4 = 7,5

Step 3 – Calculate the female unemployment rate.

7,5 – 1,4 = 6,1

## Pregunta 103

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpIcGV4X3RxaUJFVXdLQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=37

### Enunciado

In 2005, Finland, UK, Austria and Sweden had an average of 46% of subsidies across all four countries. In 2005, Austria had 5 percentage points more subsidies than Sweden, and Finland’s subsidies were 100% more than the Czech Republic’s. What percentage share of subsidies did UK have in 2005?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0147-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 54%
- **B.** 19%
- **C.** 45%
- **D.** 90%
- **E.** 9%

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **E**: 9%

Step 1 – Work out the total percentage subsidies across the four countries

46 x 4 = 184

Step 2 – Work out Austria’s and Sweden’s percentages

Sweden is 40% so Austria is 45% (40 + 5 percentage points = 45)

Step 3 – Work out Finland’s percentage

45 x 2 = 90% (Czech Republic is 45% in 2005)

Step 4 – Work out UK’s percentage

184 – (40 + 45 + 90) = 9%

## Pregunta 104

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpIcGV4X3RxaUJFVXdLQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=38

### Enunciado

In Austria, the share of inland freight that was transported by road fell by 8,5 percentage points between 2000 and 2010 while total inland freight transport grew by 18% in the same period. How much road freight did Austria have in 2010?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0148-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 33.217 million tkm
- **B.** 34.982 million tkm
- **C.** 1.769.528 million tkm
- **D.** 1.830.199 million tkm
- **E.** 27.238 million tkm

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 33.217 million tkm

Step 1 – Work out the total inland freight in Austria in 2010

50.000 x 1,18 = 59.000

Step 2 – Calculate the % road freight in Austria in 2010

64,8 – 8,5 = 56,3%

Step 3 – Calculate the amount of road freight in Austria in 2010

59.000 x 0,563 = 33.217 (million tkm)

## Pregunta 105

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpIcGV4X3RxaUJFVXdLQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=39

### Enunciado

In 2011, Italy was the top importer of electricity, with its imports being 3 times above the imports of the second top importer Finland. In 2011, France was the main net exporter, with exports of 2½ times of the second biggest exporter Czech Republic. What was the net difference between Italy’s and France’s imports and exports in 2011?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0149-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 120
- **B.** 15
- **C.** -15
- **D.** 2
- **E.** -2

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **B**: 15

Step 1 – Work out how much Finland imported and thus what Italy imported in 2011

Finland imported: 80 x 0,25 = 20

So Italy imported: 20 x 3 = 60

Step 2 – Work out how much Czech Republic exported and thus what France exported in 2011

Czech Rep exported: -90 x 0,20 = -18

So France exported: -18 x 2,5 = -45

Step 3 – Calculate the net difference

60 – 45 = 15

## Pregunta 106

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpIcGV4X3RxaUJFVXdLQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=40

### Enunciado

In 2009, there were 6.000 enterprises in the EU producing 150 million tonnes of milk. How much on average was produced by medium-sized enterprises (those not small or large)?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0150-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 52.717 tonnes
- **B.** 500 tonnes
- **C.** 625.000 tonnes
- **D.** 1.125 tonnes
- **E.** 1.125.000 tonnes

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 52.717 tonnes

Step 1 – Work out the number of enterprises and how much milk is produced by small enterprises

0,75 x 6.000 = 4.500 enterprises

0,015 x 150.000.000 = 2.250.000 tonnes of milk

Step 2 – Work out the number of enterprises and how much milk is produced by large enterprises

0,02 x 6.000 = 120 enterprises

0,50 x 150.000.000 = 75.000.000

Step 3 – Calculate the number of enterprises and how much milk is produced by medium enterprises

6.000 – (4.500 + 120) = 1.380 enterprises

150.000.000 – (75.000.000 + 2.250.000) = 72.750.000

Step 4 – Work out the average milk produced by a medium sized enterprise

72.750.000/1.380 = 52.717,3913

## Pregunta 107

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDo2NFh1dzBfanVFRXY3UWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=3

### Enunciado

In 2010, France, Spain, UK, and Germany represented one half of all land used in agriculture across the EU. What percentage was used for agricultural use in Poland, Romania and Italy?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0153-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 55%
- **B.** 48%
- **C.** 6%
- **D.** 12%
- **E.** 24%

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **E**: 24%

Step 1 – Find the total utilised agricultural area across France, Spain, UK and Germany.

28.000 + 23.700 + 16.900 + 16.700 = 85.300

Step 2 – Calculate the total agricultural land in the EU.

85.300 x 2 = 170.600

Step 3 – Find the total utilised agricultural area across Poland, Romania and Italy.

14.500 + 13.300 + 12.800 = 40.600

Step 4 – Find the agricultural land in Poland, Romania and Italy as a percentage of the total EU agricultural land.

40.600/170.600 x 100 = 23,8%

## Pregunta 108

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDo2NFh1dzBfanVFRXY3UWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=6

### Enunciado

5% of the total shipment weight for the day is made up of packaging. How many kilograms does the packaging weigh?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0156-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 75
- **B.** 80
- **C.** 78,00
- **D.** 68
- **E.** 74

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 75

Step 1 : Find the total weight by summing the values in the weight column:552+236+232+480=1.500.

Step 2 : Divide this value by 10 to find 10%: 1.500/10=150.

Step 3 : Divide the 10% value by 2 to find 5%: 150/2=75. This calculation can be figured out using a calculator, but will likely be a lot quicker to work out mentally.

## Pregunta 109

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDo2NFh1dzBfanVFRXY3UWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=9

### Enunciado

The ratio between the highest and lowest selling prices for chickens in 2012 is the same as the ratio between the selling prices of eggs in two other countries.  For these two countries, what is the ratio between the higher and lower selling prices for cows?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0159-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 6:1
- **B.** 3:1
- **C.** 4:1
- **D.** 8:1
- **E.** 7:1

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **B**: 3:1

Step 1

Calculate the ratio between the highest and lowest selling prices for chickens.

Ratio is 375,00 : 62.50 (Luxembourg : Portugal) = 6 : 1

Step2

Find which pair of countries has the same 6:1 ratio.

To quickly find the values, multiply the lower values by 6 to find the pair of countries.

We can see that the only two countries that have the ratio 6:1 are UK and Greece as 21,00 : 3.50 = 6 : 1

Step 3

Identify the values for the higher and lower selling prices for Greece and UK and calculate the ratio

150 : 50 = 3:1 (Greece: UK)

## Pregunta 110

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDo2NFh1dzBfanVFRXY3UWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=10

### Enunciado

In Ireland, the inflation rate in Jul 2013 was 0,3%. What was the four-month average rate in Oct 2013?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0160-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 0,4
- **B.** 0
- **C.** 0,1
- **D.** 0,3
- **E.** 0,5

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **C**: 0,1

Step 1 – Calculate the total rate for Ireland for period Aug – Nov 2013

0,1 x 4 = 0,4

Step 2 – Work out monthly inflation rate for Aug 2013

0,4 – (0,3 + 0,1 + 0) = 0 (Total rate - (Jul + Oct + Sep) = Aug)

Step 3 – Calculate the 4 month average rate for Oct 2013 using the figures provided.

(0,1 + 0 + 0 +0,3)/4 = 0,1

## Pregunta 111

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDo2NFh1dzBfanVFRXY3UWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=11

### Enunciado

In 2010, an average household spent €100 per month on each of the items below. In 2011, what was the difference in spending between the item with the highest price increase and the one with the lowest price increase?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0161-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** €10
- **B.** €2
- **C.** €4
- **D.** €12
- **E.** €8

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: €10

Step 1 – Find the item with the highest price rise in 2011 and calculate how much it now costs.

100 x 1,06 (transport) = 106

Step 2 – Find the item with the lowest price rise in 2011 and calculate how much it now costs.

100 x 0,96 (communications) = 96

Step 3 – Calculate the difference between the two items

106 – 96 = 10€

## Pregunta 112

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDo2NFh1dzBfanVFRXY3UWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=13

### Enunciado

In 2011, in Spain the number of physicians dropped by 1⁄16 and the number of hospital beds dropped by 1⁄13 compared to the previous year. In 2012, hospital beds in Spain were expected to increase by 200. How many physicians will be required in 2012 assuming the same ratio of doctors to beds as in 2011.

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0163-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 430
- **B.** 860
- **C.** 800
- **D.** 1280
- **E.** 500

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **E**: 500

Step 1 – Calculate the number of physicians in 2011. 400 x 15⁄16 = 375

Step 2 – Calculate the number of hospital beds in 2011. 650 x12⁄13 = 600

Step 3 – Calculate the proportion of physicians to hospital beds in 2011 in Spain. 375 physicians can support 600 beds, so 1 physician can support 1,6 beds (600/375 = 1,6)

Step 4 – Calculate the expected increase in hospital beds in 2011. 600 + 200 = 800 beds

Step 5 – Apply the proportion calculation to the new hospital bed total 800/1,6 = 500 doctors

## Pregunta 113

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDo2NFh1dzBfanVFRXY3UWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=16

### Enunciado

In Germany between 2009 and 2013, the number of police officers rose by 12% while the number of crimes recorded reduced by 15% in the same time. What was the ratio of police officers to crimes recorded in Germany in 2013?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0166-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 1 : 24
- **B.** 280.000 : 5.100
- **C.** 14 : 255
- **D.** 280 : 510
- **E.** 10 : 240

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **C**: 14 : 255

Step 1 – Work out how many police officers there are in Germany in 2013.

250.000 x 1,12 = 280.000 (this can be completed in two steps)

Step 2 – Work out the number of crimes recorded in Germany in 2013.

6.000.000 x 0,85 = 5.100.000

Step 3 – Work out the ratio of police officers to crimes recorded.

280.000 : 5.100.000 (remember the crimes recorded are in thousands)

Step 4 – Express the ratio in its simplest form 14 : 255

In this case it is also possible to express the value as a decimal 280,000 / 5,100,000 = 0.0549 and see which of the ratio options also has the same decimal value as 14: 255

## Pregunta 114

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDo2NFh1dzBfanVFRXY3UWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=20

### Enunciado

In 1960, Asia had 40% of the population that it had in 2012 and in 2012 its percentage share of the world population had increased by 4 percentage points from 1960. What percentage of the world’s population was Europe in 2012 with 740 million people?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0170-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 18,6%
- **B.** 10,1%
- **C.** 10,4%
- **D.** 12,3%
- **E.** 21,7%

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **C**: 10,4%

Step 1 – Calculate Asia’s population in 2012

1701/40 = 42,525 (1%)

42,525 x 100 = 4.252,5

Step 2 – Calculate percentage share of Asia’s world population

56,0 + 4 = 60

Step 3 – Calculate the world population

4.252,5/60 = 70,875 (1%)

70,875 x 100 = 7.087,5

Step 4 –Calculate Europe’s percentage share of the world’s population

740/7087,5 x 100 = 10,441

## Pregunta 115

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDo2NFh1dzBfanVFRXY3UWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=22

### Enunciado

By what percentage does the interest rate increase on the Boost Plus credit card after 12 months?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0172-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 90
- **B.** 85
- **C.** 67,5
- **D.** 6,75
- **E.** 32,5

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 90

As we are finding the percentage change we need to calculate the increase in percentage between the rate before and after the change at 12 months.

Step 1 : Identify the promotional interest percentage (7,5) and interest percent after the promotional period (14,25).

Step 2 : Use the formula to find the percentage change between these numbers: ((14,25-7,5)/7,5)*100=(6,75/7,5)*100=90.

## Pregunta 116

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDo2NFh1dzBfanVFRXY3UWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=24

### Enunciado

Among partner countries and regions in 2011, the EU’s current account deficit was largest with China, which was twice as large as the deficit with Russia and five times the deficit with Japan What percentage of the total EU current account deficit of 242.800 million did these three countries represent?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0174-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 50%
- **B.** 4%
- **C.** 400%
- **D.** 75%
- **E.** 85%

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **E**: 85%

Step 1 – Find the deficit with Russia.

121.400/2 = 60.700

Step 2 – Find the deficit with Japan.

121.400/5 = 24.280

Step 3 – Find the total deficit across the three countries.

121.400 + 60.700 + 24.280 = 206.380

Step 4 – Find the percentage of the total deficit this represents

206.380/242.800 x 100 = 85%

## Pregunta 117

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDo2NFh1dzBfanVFRXY3UWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=25

### Enunciado

In 2012, Belgium represented 2,5% of the total EU output value of the agricultural industry. In 2013, the total EU output value is expected to grow by 15%. Given France is expected to increase its total share by 5 percentage points, what is France’s expected output value in 2013?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0175-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 102.000
- **B.** 97.750
- **C.** 117.300
- **D.** 391.000
- **E.** 102

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **C**: 117.300

Step 1 – Find the total EU output value for 2012. If 8.500 is 2,5%, then the total EU output value is 340.000 (8.500/2,5 x 100 = 340.000).

Step 2 – Calculate what percentage of the EU total France represents. 85.000/340.000 x 100 = 25%

Step 3 – Calculate the expected increase in total EU output value in 2013: 340.000 x 1,15 = 391.000

Remember, 1,15 represents the 15% expected growth.

Step 4 – Calculate France’s expected share of the EU total: 25% + 5% = 30%

30% of 391.000 = 117.300

Tip: You can calculate France’s percentage share (in step 2) directly as it is ten times bigger than Belgium's (85.000/8.500 )

## Pregunta 118

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDo2NFh1dzBfanVFRXY3UWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=26

### Enunciado

In 2011, total EU foreign direct investment (FDI) flows showed signs of recovery. Outward flows of FDI increased for the first time in four years, rising by 154% when compared with 2010. At the same time, inward flows of FDI more than doubled compared with the previous year – up 117%. What was the net difference between outward and inward FDI in 2011?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0176-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 3.008 thousand million
- **B.** 2.188 thousand million
- **C.** 5.508 thousand million
- **D.** 2.188 million
- **E.** 3.008 million

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 3.008 thousand million

Step 1 – Calculate outward FDI for 2011

3.320 x 1,54 = 5.112,80 (54% increase)

3.320 + 5.112,80 = 8.432,80 (154% increase)

Or simply 3.320 x 2,54 (154% increase) = 8.432,80

Step 2 – Calculate inward FDI for 2011

2.500 x 1,17 = 2.925 (17% increase)

2.500 + 2.925 = 5.425 (117% increase)

Step 3 – Calculate difference

8.432,80 – 5.425 = 3.007,80 thousand million

Tip: You can complete steps 1 and 2 in one stage by using a multiplier:

For example: 3.320 x 2,54 = 8.432,80

## Pregunta 119

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDo2NFh1dzBfanVFRXY3UWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=28

### Enunciado

Euro member states pledge to keep government debt below 60% of GDP. However, in 2011 many member states exceeded this threshold. Greece’s GDP in 2011 was €215.000 million. By how much did Greece need to reduce government debt (as an amount) to meet its target?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0178-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** €237.790.000
- **B.** €129.000.000
- **C.** €366.790.000.000
- **D.** €237.790
- **E.** €237.790.000.000

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **E**: €237.790.000.000

Step 1 – Calculate Greece’s government debt in 2011.

1,706 x 215.000 = 366.790 million

Step 2 – Calculate 60% of GDP (government debt threshold).

215.000 x 0,6 = 129.000 million

Step 3 – Calculate by how much Greece needed to reduce government debt to meet its threshold.

366.790 – 129.000 = 237.790 million

## Pregunta 120

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDo2NFh1dzBfanVFRXY3UWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=29

### Enunciado

In 2009, GDP contracted considerably as a result of the global financial and economic crisis, but recovered across all countries by 8% in 2010. In Germany, GDP grew by 20% between 2001 and 2012. What was the percentage growth in GDP in Germany between 2009 and 2012?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0179-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 10%
- **B.** 11%
- **C.** 12%
- **D.** 13%
- **E.** 14%

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **E**: 14%

Step 1 – Calculate the GDP in Germany in 2009.

2.538/1,08 = 2.350

Step 2 – Calculate the GDP in Germany in 2012.

2.232,5 x 1,2 = 2.679

Step 3 – Calculate the percentage growth in GDP between 2009 and 2012.

2.679/2350 x 100 = 1,14 (14% growth)

## Pregunta 121

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDo2NFh1dzBfanVFRXY3UWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=35

### Enunciado

The country with the lowest levels of internet access in 2013 would like to grow this figure by 20 percentage points in the next two years. Given that its number of households is ¹⁄₉ of the size of the UK’s, which is 63 million, by how much does it under or over achieve against this target if it has 5,6 million households with internet in 2015 (assuming the population does not change)?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0185-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 0,28 million over
- **B.** 0,42 million under
- **C.** 0,28 million under
- **D.** 1,06 million under
- **E.** 0,42 million over

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **E**: 0,42 million over

Step 1 – Identify the country with the lowest level of internet usage and add on 20 percentage points.

54 (Bulgaria) + 20 = 74%

Step 2 – Calculate the size of Bulgaria’s population.

63/9 = 7 million

Step 3 – Calculate the target internet users.

7 x 0,74 = 5,18 million

Step 4 - Calculate the difference between the target and the actual figure.

5,6 – 5,18 = 0,42 million

This means that if the actual number of households with internet is 5,6 million two years later it has overachieved the target with 0,42 million.

## Pregunta 122

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDo2NFh1dzBfanVFRXY3UWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=38

### Enunciado

In 2010, Italy had a third of the number of national immigrants as the UK. In the UK national immigrants represented 16% of total immigrants. Of the remainder, ⅓ were citizens of other EU Member States. How many immigrants to UK were citizens of other non-member countries?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0188-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 357.000
- **B.** 178.500
- **C.** 637.500
- **D.** 212.500
- **E.** 425.000

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 357.000

Step 1 – Find the number of UK national immigrants.

34.000 x 3 = 102.000

Step 2 – Find the total immigrants to UK.

102.000/16 x 100 = 637.500

Step 3 – Find the number of non-national immigrants to UK.

637.500 – 102.000 = 535.500

Step 4 – Calculate how many immigrants were citizens of non-member counties.

⅔ x 535.500 = 357.000

or

535.500 – (⅓ x 535.500) = 357.000

## Pregunta 123

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDo2NFh1dzBfanVFRXY3UWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=40

### Enunciado

The crude marriage rate in the EU declined from 7,9 marriages per thousand inhabitants in 1970 to 4,4 marriages per thousand inhabitants by 2010. What was the percentage overall decline in the absolute number of marriages?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0190-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 36%
- **B.** 46%
- **C.** 42%
- **D.** 53%
- **E.** 56%

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 36%

Step 1 – Work out the number of marriages in 1970.

434.936.709/1000 x 7,9 = 3.436.000

Step 2 – Work out the number of marriages in 2010.

500.909.091/1000 x 4,4 = 2.204.000

Step 3 – Calculate the difference between the number of marriages in 1970 and 2010.

3.436.000 – 2.204.000 = 1.232.000

Step 4 – Work out the % decline in marriages over the period 1970 to 2010.

1.232.000/3.436.000 x 100 = 35,9%

## Pregunta 124

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpfUHRuempKOFNVb3ZjQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=1

### Enunciado

Jaime walks from Bampton to Corbry and then from Corbry to Gorman. What distance in miles does he walk in total?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0191-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 4 3/20
- **B.** 4 1/20
- **C.** 4 1/10
- **D.** 3 5/9
- **E.** 3 9/10

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 4 3/20

Step 1 : Convert both mixed numbers into improper fractions: 1 2/5=7/5, 2 3/4= 11/4.

Step 2 : Multiply the denominators of the fractions to give the denominator of the answer: 4*5=20.

Step 3 : Multiply the numerators of the fractions by the opposite denominators: 7*4=28, 11*5=55.

Step 4 : Add the two numbers from

Step 3 to give the numerator of the fraction: 28+55=83.

Step 5 : Write the fraction: 83/20.

Step 6 : Rewrite the fraction as a mixed number: 83/20=4 3/20.

## Pregunta 125

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpfUHRuempKOFNVb3ZjQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=2

### Enunciado

By how much must the average number of books read by 18-24 year olds increase between 2010 and 2020 in order for the target from 2000 to 2020 to be met?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0192-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 4,2
- **B.** 5,04
- **C.** 7,56
- **D.** 3,53
- **E.** 10,08

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 4,2

Step 1 : Find the multiplier associated with the 20% increase in the average number of books read by 18-24 year-olds: 1+(20/100)=1.2.

Step 2 : Divide the average number of books read by this age group in 2010 by this multiplier to find the average number read in 2000: 12,6/1,2=10.5.

Step 3 : find the multiplier for the targeted 60% increase between 2000 and 2020: 1+(60/100)=1,6.

Step 4 : Multiply the average number for 2000 by this multiplier to get the target average for 2020: 1,6*10,5=16,8.

Step 5 : Subtract this number from the average number read in 2010: 16,8-12,6=4,2.

## Pregunta 126

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpfUHRuempKOFNVb3ZjQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=3

### Enunciado

40% of the "ingredients" spending in January was used spent on meat products. What was the total spending on meat products?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0193-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** $2 800
- **B.** $3 400
- **C.** $3 800
- **D.** $4 900
- **E.** $5 250

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: $2 800

Step 1 : Divide the ingredient costs for January by 10 to find 10%: 7.000/10=700.

Step 2 : multiply the 10% figure by 4 to find 40%: 700*4=280. These calculations can be done with a calculator, but you may be able to do them quicker mentally.

## Pregunta 127

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpfUHRuempKOFNVb3ZjQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=4

### Enunciado

What fraction of the total seats at the Broadwell Theatre are located in the balcony or stalls sections?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0194-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 13/20
- **B.** 1/3
- **C.** 3/20
- **D.** 7/10
- **E.** 9/20

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 13/20

Step 1 : Identify the two fractions to be added as 2/5 and 1/4.

Step 2 : Multiply the denominators of the fractions to give the denominator of the answer: 5*4=20.

Step 3 : multiply the numerators of the fractions by the opposite denominators: 2*4=8, 1*5=5.

Step 4 : Add the two numbers from

Step 3 to give the numerator of the fraction: 8+5=13.

Step 5 : Write the fraction: 13/20.

## Pregunta 128

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpfUHRuempKOFNVb3ZjQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=5

### Enunciado

Assuming sample A continued to grow by the same factor, which of the following is the best estimate of the number of days required for the weight of sample A to double?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0195-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 30
- **B.** 35
- **C.** 40
- **D.** 25
- **E.** 20

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 30

We can estimate this value using the rule of 72.

Step 1 : Find the rate of increase and compounding period for the weight of sample A: The rate of increase is (112-100)=12% and its compounding period is 5 days.

Step 2 : Divide 72 by the rate of increase to find an estimate of the number of compounding periods needed to double the amount: 72/12=6.

Step 3 : Multiply this answer by the length of the compounding period in days: 6*5=30.

## Pregunta 129

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpfUHRuempKOFNVb3ZjQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=6

### Enunciado

Marcus has a bank account with a balance of €1.341. What is the total amount in euros left in Marcus's account after he buys a ticket from Paris to Los Angeles including all taxes and fees?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0196-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 430
- **B.** 420
- **C.** 442
- **D.** 426
- **E.** 450

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 430

Step 1 : Identify the base price (562) and taxes and fees price (349) in euros of the Paris to Los Angeles Flight.

Step 2 : Add these two prices: 562+349=911.

Step 3 : subtract this value from the total account balance in euros: 1341-911=430. Some calculations can be done faster mentally than on a calculator. For example 562+349 can be calculated by splitting up the numbers 562 and 349 to (500+60+2) and (300+40+9). Then500+300=800, 800+60+40=900 and then 900+9+2=911.

## Pregunta 130

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpfUHRuempKOFNVb3ZjQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=7

### Enunciado

What fraction of the instruments sold are guitars or bass guitars?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0197-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 23/37
- **B.** 6/37
- **C.** 17/37
- **D.** 1/2
- **E.** 19/74

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 23/37

Step 1 : Identify the number of guitars sold (68) and the number of bass guitars sold (24).

Step 2 : Add the numbers of guitars and bass guitars sold: 68+24=92.

Step 3 : Write this as a fraction over the total number of instruments sold: 92/148.

Step 3 : Simplify this fraction by dividing the numerator and denominator by 4: (92/4)/(148/4)=23/37. The addition in this question can be done faster mentally than on a calculator by splitting up the numbers 68 and 24 to (60+8) and (20+4). Then 60+20=80, 80+8=88 and 88+4=92.

## Pregunta 131

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpfUHRuempKOFNVb3ZjQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=8

### Enunciado

What fraction of the total time spent on the project was spent developing sketches?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0198-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 3/8
- **B.** 1/25
- **C.** 5/37
- **D.** 2/5
- **E.** 3/7

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 3/8

Step 1 : Multiply the decimal representing the time spent developing sketches by 100: 0,375*100=37,5.

Step 2  : Write a fraction with this number as the numerator, and 100 as the denominator: 37,5/1.00.

Step 3 : Simplify this fraction by dividing both the numerator and denominator by 12,5: 37,5/100=3/8.

## Pregunta 132

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpfUHRuempKOFNVb3ZjQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=9

### Enunciado

If the value of KG investment's group gold investment grows by a fixed percentage each year, what is the approximate value of this percentage?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0199-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 4,5
- **B.** 4,1
- **C.** 4,2
- **D.** 4,8
- **E.** 5,0

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 4,5

The rule of 72 tells us that an investment which gains interest at p% anually would take approximately t=72/p years to double.

Step 1 :Rearrange the rule of 72 by multiplying both sides by p and then dividing both sides by t. p=72/t.

Step 2 : Interpret this statement: To find the percentage increase each year we can divide 72 by the number of years it takes the investment to double.

Step 3 : Complete this calculation; divide 72 by the years it will take the gold investment to double (16): 72/16=4,5.

## Pregunta 133

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpfUHRuempKOFNVb3ZjQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=10

### Enunciado

What was the percentage point decrease in the proportion of passengers choosing to upgrade on the Barcelona route between 2012 and 2014?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0200-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 4
- **B.** 30
- **C.** 33
- **D.** 8
- **E.** 12

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 4

As we are finding the "percentage point decrease" we just need the difference in the percentages between 2012 and 2014.

Step 1 : Find the percentage of passengers on the Barcelona route who upgraded in 2012: (13.488/112.400)*100=12.

Step 2 : Find the percentage of passengers on the Barcelona route who upgraded in 2014: (9.464/118.300)*100=8.

Step 3 : Subtract the 2014 percentage from the 2012 percentage: 12-8=4.

## Pregunta 134

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpfUHRuempKOFNVb3ZjQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=11

### Enunciado

In which group is the ratio of boys to girls in the same proportion as the ratio in year 3?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0201-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** Year 1
- **B.** Year 2
- **C.** Year 4
- **D.** Year 5
- **E.** Year 6

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: Year 1

First exclude all of the years in which there are more boys then boys than girls (years 2 and 5) as these obviously cannot be in the same ratio. Now for each remaining year in the table the following trick can be used to test if the ratios are the same. These are the steps for year 1:

Step 1 : Write the ratios in year 1 and year 3 as fractions: 8/12 and 10/15.

Step 2 : Multiply the numerator of the first fraction by the denominator of the second: 8*15= 120.

Step 3 : Multiply the denominator of the first fraction by the numerator of the second: 12*10= 120.

Step 4 : Compare the values from Steps 2 and 3: As these multiplications gave the same values, the ratios are in the same proportion.

## Pregunta 135

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpfUHRuempKOFNVb3ZjQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=12

### Enunciado

By what percentage has the proportion of the population who are classed as obese increased by between 1990 and 2000?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0202-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 75
- **B.** 6
- **C.** 12
- **D.** 60
- **E.** 67

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 75

As we are finding the percentage change we need to calculate the increase in percentage between 1990 and 2000 as a percentage of the 1990 percentage.

Step 1 : Identify the percentage of the population which was obese in 1990 (8) and 2000 (14).

Step 2 : Use the formula to find the percentage change between these numbers: ((14-8)/8)*100=(6/8)*100=75.

## Pregunta 136

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpfUHRuempKOFNVb3ZjQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=13

### Enunciado

3/5 of the staff members in the marketing department at Technix work part time. What fraction of the total staff are part timestaff members in the marketing department?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0203-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 2/15
- **B.** 1/15
- **C.** 1/9
- **D.** 5/9
- **E.** 37/45

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 2/15

Step 1 : Write the multiplication required: 3/5 of 2/9 = (3/5)*(2/9).

Step 2 : Multiply the numerators to find the numerator of the answer: 3*2=6.

Step 3 : Multiply the denominators to find the denominator of the answer: 5*9=45.

Step 4 : Write the answer as a fraction: 6/45.

Step 5 : simplify this fraction by dividing the numerator and denominator by any common factors: 6/45=(6/3)/(45/3)=2/15.

## Pregunta 137

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpfUHRuempKOFNVb3ZjQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=14

### Enunciado

In May, approximately 2/3 of the income from taxes was from a property tax. What fraction of the total income was from the property tax?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0204-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 4/15
- **B.** 8/19
- **C.** 15/16
- **D.** 3/5
- **E.** 2/5

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 4/15

Step 1 : Find and simplify the fraction of income which was from taxes: 14/35=2/5.

Step 2 : Write the multiplication required: 2/3 of 2/5 = (2/3)*(2/5).

Step 3 : Multiply the numerators to find the numerator of the answer: 2*2=4.

Step 4 : Multiply the denominators to find the denominator of the answer: 5*3=15.

Step 5 : Write the answer as a fraction: 4/15.

## Pregunta 138

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpfUHRuempKOFNVb3ZjQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=15

### Enunciado

What amount in euros did the council spend on education in 2016?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0205-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 2.450
- **B.** 2.415
- **C.** 2.168
- **D.** 1.897
- **E.** 2.515

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 2.450

Step 1 : Find the multiplier associated with a 12% decrease: 1-(12/100)=0,88.

Step 2 : Divide the amount spent on education in 2017 by this multiplier: 2.156/0,88=2.450.

## Pregunta 139

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpfUHRuempKOFNVb3ZjQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=16

### Enunciado

The government has a target to increase the total area of protected land (both nationally and locally protected) by 50% between 2009 and 2029. What area of land, in square kilometers, will need to be protected in 2029 to meet this target?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0206-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 216.000
- **B.** 218.000
- **C.** 212.000
- **D.** 208.000
- **E.** 210.000

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 216.000

Step 1 : Identify the number of thousand square kilometers of locally protected land (86) and nationally protected land (58) in 2009.

Step 2 : Add these two numbers: 58+86=144.

Step 3 : Increase this number by 50% by finding half of the number and adding this on: 144+(144/2)=144+72=216.

Step 4 : Multiply this number by 1 000: 216*1 000=216 000. Some calculations in this problem can be done faster mentally than on a calculator. For example we can calculate 86+58 by splitting up the numbers 86 and 58 to (80+6) and (50+8). Then 80+50=130, 130+6=136 and 136+8=144.

## Pregunta 140

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpfUHRuempKOFNVb3ZjQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=17

### Enunciado

What is the ratio of hours of Mathematics lessons to hours of History lessons at Widecombe Academy?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0207-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 9:4
- **B.** 3:1
- **C.** 2:1
- **D.** 5:2
- **E.** 7:3

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 9:4

Step 1 : Write the hours of mathematics lessons to the hours of history lessons at Widecombe High as a ratio: 171:76.

Step 2 : Find the factors of the numbers on each side of the ratio: 171 has factors 1, 3, 9, 19, 57, 171 while 76 has factors 1, 2, 4, 19, 38, 76.

Step 3 : Reduce the ratio to its simplest form by dividing both sides by the highest common factor of 171 and 76 (19): (171/19):(76/19)=9:4. Remember that a factor is any number which your number can be divided by and they usually come in pairs, e.g. 4 and 6 are both factors of 24 because 4*6=24. A common factor of two numbers is a factor of both numbers.

## Pregunta 141

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpfUHRuempKOFNVb3ZjQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=18

Between 2010 and 2015 the number of full time junior lecturers in the Humanities faculty increased by 10%. How many full time Junior lecturers were there at the faculty in 2010?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0208-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 30
- **B.** 29
- **C.** 31
- **D.** 23
- **E.** 43

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 30

Step 1 : Find the multiplier associated with a 10% decrease: 1+(10/100)=1,1.

Step 2 : Divide the number of Junior Lecturers in 2015 by this multiplier:33/1,1=30.

## Pregunta 142

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpfUHRuempKOFNVb3ZjQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=19

### Enunciado

What is the ratio of revenue generated from rentals to income generated from admissions in May?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0209-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 6:7
- **B.** 6:13
- **C.** 1:17
- **D.** 9:25
- **E.** 1:1

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 6:7

Step 1 : Write the income from admissions to the income from concessions in may as a ratio: 96:112.

Step 2 : Find the factors of the numbers on each side of the ratio: 96 has factors 1, 2, 3, 4, 6, 8, 12, 16, 24, 32, 48, 96 while 112 has factors 1, 2, 4, 7, 8, 14, 16, 28, 56, 112.

Step 3 : Reduce the ratio to its simplest form by dividing both sides by the highest common factor of 96 and 112 (16): (96/16):(112/16)=6:7. Remember that a factor is any number which your number can be divided by and they usually come in pairs, e.g. 4 and 6 are both factors of 24 because 4*6=24. ## Pregunta 143

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpfUHRuempKOFNVb3ZjQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=20

### Enunciado

After driving his shift, Zbigniew must rest for 3 hours to ensure that he meets regulations. How many hours will Hans have to rest so that his ratio of driving to rest is the same as Zbigniew's?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0210-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 1 7/8
- **B.** 4 4/5
- **C.** 1
- **D.** 1 2/3
- **E.** 1 5/8

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 1 7/8

Step 1 : Let the number of hours Hans must rest be x, and then write Zbigniew's and Hans's ratios of driving to rest: Zbigniew - 8:3, Hans - 5:x.

Step 2 : Write each of these ratios as a fraction and set them equal to one another: 8/3=5/x.

Step 3 : multiply both sides of the ratio by x, cancelling the x in the denominator of the right hand side: (8/3)*x=5.

Step 4 : multiply both sides of the equation by 3/8, cancelling the factor of 8/3 on the left hand side: x=5*(3/8).

Step 5 : Calculate the value of the multiplication on the right-hand side as a fraction: x=5*(3/8)=15/8.

Step 6 : Convert this to a mixed number: x=15/8=1 7/8.

## Pregunta 144

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpfUHRuempKOFNVb3ZjQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=21

### Enunciado

What is the mean average amount in pounds invested in the 3 transport projects?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0211-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 450.000
- **B.** 451.000
- **C.** 449.000
- **D.** 452.000
- **E.** 453.000

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 450.000

Step 1 : Identify the amounts spent in thousands of pounds on the three transport projects: 437, 279 and 634.

Step 2 : Add these three numbers: 437+279+634=1.350.

Step 3 : Divide this total by 3: 1.350/3=450.

Step 3 : Multiply this total by 1.000: 450*1.000=450.000. The addition can be done faster mentally than on a calculator by splitting up the numbers 437, 279 and 634 to (400+30+7), (200+70+9) and (600+30+4). Then 400+600+200=1.200, 1.200+70+30=1.300, 1.300+30=1.330 and 1.320+7+4+9=1.350.

## Pregunta 145

What is the ratio of the hours Angus worked shelf stocking to the hours he worked on the deli counter?

## Pregunta 146

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpfUHRuempKOFNVb3ZjQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=23

## Pregunta 147

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpfUHRuempKOFNVb3ZjQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=24

What is the probability, expressed as a fraction, of a random treasure chest in the game containing gold (either 10 or 20)?

- **A.** 3/4
- **B.** 1/75

- **E.** 2/3

---

### Respuesta y explicacion
