## Pregunta 148

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpfUHRuempKOFNVb3ZjQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=25

### Enunciado

How much greater is the total floor space in square metres of the three flats at 128 Burgreen Gardens than the total floor space in square metres of the two flats at 14 Marten Close?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0215-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 101
- **B.** 98
- **C.** 103
- **D.** 100
- **E.** 96

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 101

Step 1 : Add the floor space figures for the three flats at 128 Burgreen Gardens: 64+65+86=215.

Step 2 : Add the floor space figures for the two flats at 14 Marten Close: 43+71=114.

Step 3 : subtract the total floorspace at 14 Marten Close from the total floorspace at 128 Burgreen Gardens: 215-114=101. These calculations may be done faster mentally than on a calculator. For example we can calculate 64+65+86 by splitting up the numbers 64, 65 and 86 to (60+4), (60+5) and (80+6). Then 60+60+80=200, 200+4+6=210 and 210+5=215.

## Pregunta 149

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpfUHRuempKOFNVb3ZjQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=26

### Enunciado

Approximately how many years would it take for the population of Vernon to double assuming the predicted growth rates continue?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0216-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 36
- **B.** 50
- **C.** 40
- **D.** 24
- **E.** 48

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 36

We can estimate this value using the rule of 72.

Step 1 : Find the rate of increase and compounding period for the population of Vernon: The rate of increase is 2% and its compounding period is 1 year (as it is an annual increase).

Step 2 : Divide 72 by the rate of increase to find an estimate of the number of compounding periods needed to double the amount: 72/2=36.

## Pregunta 150

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpfUHRuempKOFNVb3ZjQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=27

### Enunciado

Approximately how many years would it take a deposit of £1.000 to be doubled if placed in the Gold account?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0217-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 9
- **B.** 11
- **C.** 7
- **D.** 12
- **E.** 14

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 9

We can estimate this value using the rule of 72.

Step 1 : Find the interest rate and compounding period for the Gold Account: The interest is 8% and its compounding period is 1 year.

Step 2 : Divide 72 by the interest rate to find an estimate of the number of compounding periods needed to double the amount: 72/8=9.

## Pregunta 151

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpfUHRuempKOFNVb3ZjQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=28

### Enunciado

What is the total number of students enrolled in schools other than Chesham Primary and Chesham Comprehensive?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0218-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 1.483
- **B.** 1.477
- **C.** 1.476
- **D.** 1.450
- **E.** 1.469

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 1.483

Step 1 : Add the number of students enrolled at Chesham Primary (274) and Chesham Comprehensive (963): 274+963=1.237.

Step 2 : Find the total number of students enrolled by adding the nubers enrolled at Primary (1.330) and Secondary (1.389) level: 1.330+1.390=2.720.

Step 3 : Subtract the number of students enrolled at the two Chesham schools from the total number of students: 2.720-1.237=1.483.

This calculation can be done faster mentally than on a calculator by splitting up the numbers 274 and 963 to (200+70+4) and (900+60+3). Then 200+900=1.100, 1.100+70=1.170, 1 170+60=1.230 and 1.230+4+3=1.237.

## Pregunta 152

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpfUHRuempKOFNVb3ZjQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=29

### Enunciado

15% of the products (excluding items in the groceries department) are to be placed on sale. Approximately how many products will be placed on sale?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0219-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 39
- **B.** 30
- **C.** 32
- **D.** 18
- **E.** 28

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 39

Step 1 : Subtract the number of products in groceries from the total: 992-732=260.

Step 2 : Divide this total by 10 to find 10%: 260/10=26.

Step 3 : Divide the 10% figure by 2 to find 5%: 26/2=13.

Step 4 : add the 10% and 5% figures to find 15%: 26+13=39. The percentage calculations can be done with a calculator, but may be done a lot quicker mentally.

## Pregunta 153

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpfUHRuempKOFNVb3ZjQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=30

### Enunciado

In 2012, 80% by weight of the potatoes and sweet potatoes grown were exported. What was the total weight of potatoes and sweet potatoes grown on the farm which were exported in 2012?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0220-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 888
- **B.** 896
- **C.** 892
- **D.** 884
- **E.** 880

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 888

Step 1 : Identify the weight in kilograms of potatoes (787) and sweet potatoes (323) grown in 2012.

Step 2 : Add these two weights: 787+323=1 110.

Step 3 : divide this number by 10 and then multiply by 8 to find 80%:(1.110/10)*8=111*8=888. Some calculations can be done faster mentally than on a calculator. For example we can calculate 787+323 by splitting up the numbers 787 and 323 to (700+80+7) and (300+20+3). Then 700+300=1.000, 1.000+80+20=1.100 and 1.100+3+7=1.110.

## Pregunta 154

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpfUHRuempKOFNVb3ZjQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=31

### Enunciado

By how many minutes must Helen reduce her average time between June and December to meet the target she set from January to December?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0221-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 4,0
- **B.** 6,6
- **C.** 4,2
- **D.** 2,8
- **E.** 9,4

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 4,0

Step 1 : Find the multiplier associated with the 6% decrease in Helen's time between January and June: 1-(6/100)=0,94.

Step 2 : Divide Helen's June time by this multiplier to find her January time: 47/0,94=50.

Step 3 : find the multiplier for her targeted 14% decrease between January and December: 1-(14/100)=0,86.

Step 4 : Multiply her January time by this multiplier to get her target time for December: 50*0,86=43. Step5: Subtract this time from her June time to find the amount she still has to decrease her time by: 47-43=4.

## Pregunta 155

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpfUHRuempKOFNVb3ZjQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=32

### Enunciado

In 2015, 3/4 of the hardware spending was on new equipment. What fraction of the total spending was on new equipment?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0222-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 3/20
- **B.** 19/20
- **C.** 4/15
- **D.** 1/3
- **E.** 4/9

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 3/20

Step 1 : Find and simplify the fraction of spending which was on hardware: 5/25=1/5.

Step 2 : Write the multiplication required: 3/4 of 1/5 = (3/4)*(1/5).

Step 3 : Multiply the numerators to find the numerator of the answer: 3*1=3.

Step 4 : Multiply the denominators to find the denominator of the answer: 5*4=20.

Step 5 : Write the answer as a fraction: 3/20.

## Pregunta 156

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpfUHRuempKOFNVb3ZjQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=33

### Enunciado

What is the total number of cups of the liquid ingredients (milk and water) required to make 16 Yorkshire puddings?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0223-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 2 5/6
- **B.** 2 2/5
- **C.** 2 1/3
- **D.** 2 1/6
- **E.** 3 1/3

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 2 5/6

Step 1 : Convert both mixed numbers into improper fractions: 1 1/3=4/3, 1 1/2= 3/2.

Step 2 : Multiply the denominators of the fractions to give the denominator of the answer: 3*2=6.

Step 3 : Multiply the numerators of the fractions by the opposite denominators: 4*2=8, 3*3=9.

Step 4 : Add the two numbers from

Step 3 to give the numerator of the fraction: 8+9=17.

Step 5 : Write the fraction: 17/6.

Step 6 : Rewrite the fraction as a mixed number: 17/6=2 5/6.

## Pregunta 157

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpfUHRuempKOFNVb3ZjQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=34

### Enunciado

In which room is the ratio of the width to the length of the room in the same proportion as in the bathroom?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0224-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** Bedroom 3
- **B.** Bedroom 2
- **C.** Bedroom 1
- **D.** Kitchen
- **E.** Living Room

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: Bedroom 3

For each room in the table the following trick can be used to test if the ratios are the same. These are the steps for Bedroom 3:

Step 1 : Write the ratios of the width to height Bathroom and Bedroom 3 as fractions: 6/8 and 9/12.

Step 2 : Multiply the numerator of the first fraction by the denominator of the second: 6*12= 72.

Step 3 : Multiply the denominator of the first fraction by the numerator of the second: 8*9=72.

Step 4 : Compare the values from Steps 2 and 3: As these multiplications gave the same values, the ratios are in the same proportion.

## Pregunta 158

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpfUHRuempKOFNVb3ZjQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=35

### Enunciado

What fraction of the last 7 days of snow fell between 24 hours and 3 days before the time of the snow report?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0225-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 7/20
- **B.** 4/5
- **C.** 9/20
- **D.** 1/35
- **E.** 1/45

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 7/20

As the total snow is 1 meter, we may find the amount of snow in meters during this fraction and convert this to a fraction.

Step 1 : Subtract the depth of snow in the last 24 hours from the depth in the last 3 days: 0,8-0,45=0,35.

Step 2 : Multiply this decimal by 100: 0,35*100=35.

Step 3 : Write a fraction with this number as the numerator, and 100 as the denominator: 35/100.

Step 4 : Simplify this fraction by dividing both the numerator and denominator by 5: 35/100=7/20.

## Pregunta 159

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpfUHRuempKOFNVb3ZjQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=36

### Enunciado

There were a total of 180 tickets available for the 14:20 tour departure. How many of these tickets were NOT sold?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0226-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 21
- **B.** 11
- **C.** 18
- **D.** 23
- **E.** 25

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 21

Step 1 : Identify the number of adult (72), child (48) and OAP/student (39) tickets sold for the 14:20 departure.

Step 2 : Add these three numbers: 72+48+39=159. Some of these calculations can be done faster mentally than on a calculator. For example 72+48+39 can be calculated by splitting up the numbers 72, 48 and 39 to (70+2), (40+8) and (30+9). Then 70+30=100, 100+40=140, 140+2+8=150 and 150+9=159.

## Pregunta 160

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpfUHRuempKOFNVb3ZjQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=37

### Enunciado

What is the ratio of the total number of trains travelling north to the total number of trains travelling south in one day from Queensford station?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0227-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 4:5
- **B.** 5:4
- **C.** 12:7
- **D.** 3:5
- **E.** 1:5

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 4:5

Step 1 : Add the number of trains per day for each of the journeys which have a direction of north: 12+4=16.

Step 2 : Add the number of trains per day for each of the journeys which have a direction of south: 13+7=20.

Step 3 : Find the factors of the numbers on each side of the ratio: 16 has factors 1, 2, 4, 8, 16 while 20 has factors 1, 2, 4, 5, 10, 20.

Step 4 : Reduce the ratio to its simplest form by dividing both sides by the highest common factor of 16 and 20 (4): (16/4:20/4)=4:5. Remember that a factor is any number which your number can be divided by and they usually come in pairs, e.g. 4 and 6 are both factors of 24 because 4*6=24. A common factor of two numbers is a factor of both numbers.

## Pregunta 161

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpfUHRuempKOFNVb3ZjQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=38

### Enunciado

Excluding the invertebrates, what is the mean average number of species present in each family of animals?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0228-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 255
- **B.** 219
- **C.** 16.922
- **D.** 14.504
- **E.** 306

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 255

Step 1 : Sum the numbers of species in each group excluding the invertebrates:219+41+546+488+85+151=.

Step 2 : divide this answer by the number of different animal families: 1.530/6=255.

## Pregunta 162

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpfUHRuempKOFNVb3ZjQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=39

### Enunciado

The ratio of the weight in tonnes to the volume of the container in cubic meters for the shipments on Tuesday and Wednesday were the same. If the volume of the container on Wednesday was 22 cubic metres, what was its weight in tonnes?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0229-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 8 4/5
- **B.** 13
- **C.** 8 3/5
- **D.** 9 2/5
- **E.** 8 1/5

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 8 4/5

Step 1 : Let the weight of the shipment on Wednesday be x, and then write Tuesday's and Wednesday's ratios of weight to volume: Tuesday - 6:15, Wednesday - x:22.

Step 2 : Write each of these ratios as a fraction and set them equal to one another: 6/15=x/22.

Step 3 : simplify the fraction 6/15 on the left-hand side to 2/5 by dividing the numerator and the denominator by 3: 2/5=x/22.

Step 4 : multiply both sides of the equation by 22, cancelling the factor of 22 on the right hand side: (2/5)*22=x.

Step 5 : Calculate the value of the multiplication on the left-hand side as a fraction: x=(2/5)*22=44/5.

Step 6 : Convert this to a mixed number: x=44/5=8 4/5.

## Pregunta 163

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpfUHRuempKOFNVb3ZjQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=40

### Enunciado

What fraction of the charity's spending is not spent on admin costs?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0230-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 17/20
- **B.** 3/20
- **C.** 1/85
- **D.** 3/17
- **E.** 1/5

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 17/20

Step 1 : Subtract the decimal representing the proportion of spending on admin from 1: 1-0,15=0,85.

Step 2 : Multiply this decimal by 100: 0,85*100=85.

Step 3 : Write a fraction with this number as the numerator, and 100 as the denominator: 85/100.

Step 4 : Simplify this fraction by dividing both the numerator and denominator by 5: 85/100=17/20.

## Pregunta 164

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDozMHM2TXdTR0RVd3ZLQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=1

### Enunciado

Given that there are 4 automatic vehicles for every manual vehicle, what is the average mileage of all vehicles on the lot?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0231-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 100.740
- **B.** 159.060
- **C.** 129.900
- **D.** 81.300
- **E.** 178.500

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 100.740

Step 1 : Find the percentage of automatic vehicles which are cars by subtracting 45 from 100: 100-45=55.

Step 2 : Find the percentage of manual vehicles which are cars by subtracting 70 from 100: 100-70=30.

Step 3 : Find the average mileage of the automatic vehicles by multiplying the average mileage of the two types of automatic vehicles by the percentage (as a decimal) of each type and summing the results: 0,55*84.000+0,45*78.000=81.300.

Step 4 : Find the average mileage of the manual vehicles by multiplying the average mileage of the two types of manual vehicles by the percentage (as a decimal) of each type and summing the results: 0,3*140.000+0,7*195.000=178.500.

Step 5 : Work out the fractions of manual and automatic cars from the given ratio of 1:4: 1/(1+4)=1/5 are manual and 4/(1+4)=4/5 are automatic.

Step 6 : Find these fractions of the relevant average mileages of both types and add the results: (4/5)*81.300+(1/5)*178.500=100.740.

## Pregunta 165

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDozMHM2TXdTR0RVd3ZLQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=2

### Enunciado

Approximately how much greater was the revenue generated per customer at Trattoria Verde than at Spaghetteria?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0232-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 5,74 €
- **B.** 3,59 €
- **C.** 2,86 €
- **D.** 6,03 €
- **E.** 2,14 €

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 5,74 €

Step 1 : Divide the total revenue of Trattoria Verde by the number of customers at Trattoria Verde: 586.080/78.553=7,4610.

Step 2 : Divide the total revenue of Spaghetteria by the number of customers at Spaghetteria:310.500/180.076=1,7243 .

Step 3 : Subtract the revenue per customer figure for Spaghetteria from the same figure for Trattoria Verde: 7,4610-1,7243=5,7367.

Step 4 : Round this to €5,74.

## Pregunta 166

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDozMHM2TXdTR0RVd3ZLQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=3

### Enunciado

Which free-range farm has the most square meters of open-air range per hen?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0233-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** Chicken Coop
- **B.** Fowl Play
- **C.** Qluck Farm
- **D.** Green Gales Farm
- **E.** Hatching Plan

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: Chicken Coop

Step 1 : For each farm, divide the number of square meters of open-air range by the total number of hens on that farm: Fowl Play: 19.200/16.000=1,2, Chicken Coop:39.875/15.950=2,5, Qluck Farm: 21.780/14.520=1,5, Green Galse Farm: 15.283/8.990=1,7, Hatching Plan: 12.694/11.540=1,1.

Step 2 : Compare these values to see which is the highest: the value for Chicken Coop is highest at 2.5.

## Pregunta 167

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDozMHM2TXdTR0RVd3ZLQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=4

### Enunciado

The metro system of the City of Toulouse carried a total of 122 million passengers in 2015. How many more passengers did the Toulouse metro carry than the Marseille metro?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0234-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 42.000.000
- **B.** 12.480.000
- **C.** 3.000.000
- **D.** 22.000.000
- **E.** 114.000.000

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 42.000.000

Step 1 : Divide the total number of passenger complaints by the number who complained per 10 000 passengers in Marseille: 12.800/1,6=8.000.

Step 2 : Multiply this number by 10.000 to find the total number of passengers for the Marseille metro: 8.000*10.000=80.000.000.

Step 3 : Subtract this number from the 122 million Toulouse metro passengers: 122.000.000-80.000.000=42.000.000.

## Pregunta 168

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDozMHM2TXdTR0RVd3ZLQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=5

### Enunciado

Approximately how much greater was the average number of books borrowed per visitor at Fulbourn Library than at Central Library?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0235-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 3,29
- **B.** 4,67
- **C.** 2,17
- **D.** 2,39
- **E.** 0,74

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 3,29

Step 1 : Divide the total number of books borrowed at Central Library by the total number of visitors: 87.635/27.056=3,2390.

Step 2 : Divide the total number of books borrowed at Fulbourn Library by the total number of visitors: 65.112/9.968=6,5321.

Step 3 : Subtract the smaller number from the larger: 6.5321-3,2390=3,2931.

Step 4 : Round this answer to 3,29.

## Pregunta 169

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDozMHM2TXdTR0RVd3ZLQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=6

### Enunciado

Approximately how much greater is the GDP per person of Bavaria than the GDP per person of Hesse?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0236-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 82 €
- **B.** 285 €
- **C.** 6.028 €
- **D.** 308 €
- **E.** 226 €

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 82 €

Step 1 : Divide the GDP of Bavaria (in millions of €) by the population of Bavaria (in millions) to find the GDP per person: 549.190/12,9=42.572,87.

Step 2 : Divide the GDP of Hesse (in millions of €) by the population of Hesse (in millions) to find the GDP per person: 263.444/6,2=42.490,97.

Step 3 : Subtract the GDP per peson of Hesse from the GDP per person of Bavaria: 42.572,87-42.490,97=81,90.

Step 4 : Round this to €82.

## Pregunta 170

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDozMHM2TXdTR0RVd3ZLQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=7

### Enunciado

Which country has the highest number of mobile phones per person?

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0237-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** Russia
- **B.** Japan
- **C.** Italy
- **D.** New Zealand
- **E.** Zimbabwe

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: Russia

Step 1 : For each country, divide the number of mobile phones (in thousands) by the total population (also in thousands): Japan: 146.649/127.300=1,152, Italy:88.580/60.790=1,457, New Zealand: 4.500/4.761=0,945, Russia: 256.116/142.905=1,792, Zimbabwe: 13.060/13.518=0,966.

Step 2 : Compare these values to see which is the highest: the value for Russia is highest at 1,792.

## Pregunta 171

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDozMHM2TXdTR0RVd3ZLQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=8

### Enunciado

How many more participants were there in the study looking for gene B than for gene D?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0238-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 9.000
- **B.** 13.000
- **C.** 15.000
- **D.** 900
- **E.** 1.500

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 9.000

Step 1 : Divide the total number of participants with gene B by the number of participants per 1.000 with this gene in the relevant study: 12/0,5=24.

Step 2 : Multiply this number by 1.000 to find the total number of participants in the study: 24*1.000=24.000.

Step 3 : Divide the total number of participants with gene D by the number of participants per 1.000 with this gene in the relevant study: 27/1,8=15.

Step 4 : Multiply this number by 1.000 to find the total number of participants in the study: 15*1.000=15.000.

Step 5 : Subtract the number of participants in the study of gene D from the number in the study of gene B: 24.000-15.000=9.000.

## Pregunta 172

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDozMHM2TXdTR0RVd3ZLQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=9

### Enunciado

Approximately how many more trees per square kilometer are there in National Park areas than in Provincial Forest areas?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0239-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 875
- **B.** 485
- **C.** 342
- **D.** 4.656
- **E.** 87

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 875

Step 1 : Find the number of trees per kilometers in millions in the national park and provincial forest areas by multiplying both figures by 1.000.000:1.162* 1.000.000=1.162.000.000 and 677*1.000.000=667.000.000.

Step 2 : Divide the total number of trees in each region by the total area of that region: National park: 1.162.000.000/70.690=16.437,97, Provincial Forest: 677.000/43.500=15.563,22.

Step 3 : Subtract the smaller number from the larger: 16.437,97-15.563,22=874,75.

Step 4 : Round this answer to 875.

## Pregunta 173

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDozMHM2TXdTR0RVd3ZLQWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=10

### Enunciado

All the schools except Elizabeth College and Rose Meadows are participating in an interschool competition. What is the mean average number of students at the schools taking part in the competition?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0240-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 1.500
- **B.** 1.429
- **C.** 2.000
- **D.** 1.071
- **E.** 2.500

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 1.500

Step 1 : Subtract the numbers of students in the schools not taking part in the competition from the total number of students: 10.000-1.447-1.053=7.500.

Step 2 : divide this answer by the number of schools in the competition: 7.500/5=1.500.

## Pregunta 174

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpaU09YaF8zd25rQXZGZ2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=1

### Enunciado

The table shows passenger numbers for the 5 most popular trains from Neuss to Dusseldorf on two days in one week. On Tuesday the 07:43 carried 15% of the passengers who took a train from Neuss to Dusseldorf that day. On Wednesday there were 4% fewer passengers who took a train from Neuss to Dusseldorf. What percentage of passengers taking a train from Neuss to Dusseldorf on Wednesday took the 07:43 and travelled first class?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0241-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 0,40%
- **B.** 3,20%
- **C.** 12,50%
- **D.** 0,65%
- **E.** 0,48%

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 0,40%

Step 1 : Find 15% as a decimal: 15/100=0,15.

Step 2 : Divide the number of passengers on the Tuesday 07:43 by this decimal to find the total number of passengers on Tuesday: 1.335/0,15=8.900.

Step 3 : Find the multiplier for a 4% decrease: 1-(4/100)=0,96.

Step 4 : Multiply the total passengers on Tuesday by this figure to find the total passengers on Wednesday: 8.900*0,96=8.544.

Note that the question asks for the total number of passangers on Wednesday and not only for the 5 most popular trains which is why we need to find the total number of passangers.

Step 5 : Find the number of passengers on the 07:43 as a percentage of this figure: (1.068/8.544)*100=12,5. Find 3,2 percent of 12,5 percent by multiplying 12,5 by the decimal 0,032: 12,5*0,032=0,4.

## Pregunta 175

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpaU09YaF8zd25rQXZGZ2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=2

### Enunciado

How many tickets were sold for Saturday performances of The Lion King?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0242-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 1.431
- **B.** 2.629
- **C.** 767
- **D.** 719
- **E.** 712

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 1.431

Step 1 : Identify the 4 cells which correspond to tickets sold for Lin King Saturday performances (Adult matinee, Child Matinee, Adult Evening, Child Evening).

Step 2 : Add the 4 values in these cells: 365+354+402+310=1.431.

## Pregunta 176

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpaU09YaF8zd25rQXZGZ2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=3

### Enunciado

How much did Jorge's average earnings per hour increase by from 2016 to 2017?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0243-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** $3,61
- **B.** $4,03
- **C.** $4,19
- **D.** $4,34
- **E.** $1,96

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: $3,61

Step 1 : Find Jorge's average earnings per hour in 2016 by dividing his total earning by the total number of hours he worked: 67.280,34/2.341=28,74.

Step 2 : Find Jorge's average earnings per hour in 2016 by dividing his total earning by the total number of hours he worked: 77.057,70/2.382=32,35.

Step 3 : Subtract the 2016 average from the 2017 average to find the differene: 32,35-28,74=3,61.

## Pregunta 177

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpaU09YaF8zd25rQXZGZ2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=4

### Enunciado

On Wednesday how much greater was the average donation collected by Brian than the average donation collected by Meghna?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0244-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 2,16 £
- **B.** 1,11 £
- **C.** 0,88 £
- **D.** 0,89 £
- **E.** 2,04 £

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 2,16 £

Step 1 : Find the average donation collected by Meghna on Wednesday by dividing the total amount donated by the total number of donors: 251,94/19=13,26.

Step 1 : Find the average donation collected by Brian on Wednesday by dividing the total amount donated by the total number of donors: 339,24/22=15,42.

Step 3 : Subtract the Meghna's average from Brian's average to find the differene: 15,42-13,26=2,16

## Pregunta 178

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpaU09YaF8zd25rQXZGZ2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=5

### Enunciado

How many students were enrolled in sixth form colleges in Carford in 2017?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0245-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 3.485
- **B.** 1.886
- **C.** 1.599
- **D.** 3.572
- **E.** 3.554

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 3.485

Step 1 : Add the numbers in the "2017 Lower 6th" column: 871+740+275=1.886.

Step 2 : Add the numbers in the "2017 Upper 6th" column: 782+619+198=1.599.

Step 3 : Add the total number of students in lower and upper 6th in 2017: 1.886+1.599= 3.485.

## Pregunta 179

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpaU09YaF8zd25rQXZGZ2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=6

### Enunciado

Gigatech's wage bill in 2017 was $22.446.000. If the average employee wage at Gigatech was the same in 2016 and 2017, how many employee's were there at Gigatech in 2017?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0246-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 387
- **B.** 351
- **C.** 424
- **D.** 468
- **E.** 362

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 387

Step 1 : Find the average employee wage (in thousands of dollars) at Gigatech in 2016 by dividing the total wage bill by the number of employees: 20.532/354=58.

Step 2 : multiply by 1.000 to get the actual average employee wage: 58*1.000=58.000.

Step 3 : Divide the total wage bill in 2017 by this average wage: 22.446.000/58.000=387

## Pregunta 180

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpaU09YaF8zd25rQXZGZ2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=7

### Enunciado

At UGS combined school, there are 3 teachers for every 2 teaching assistants employed. What is the average pay in euros of all teaching staff at UGS combined school?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0247-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 25.710
- **B.** 24.000
- **C.** 24.325
- **D.** 22.940
- **E.** 20.313

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 25.710

Step 1 : Find the percentage of teachers at the lower school by subtracting 75 from 100: 100-75=25.

Step 2 : Find the percentage of teaching assistants at the lower school by subtracting 40 from 100: 100-40=60.

Step 3 : Find the average pay of the teachers by multiplying the average pay of teachers at the two schools by the percentage (as a decimal) of teachers at each and summing the results: 0,25*29.000+0,75*32.000=31.250.

Step 4 : Find the average pay of the teaching assistants by multiplying the average pay of teaching assistants at the two schools by the percentage (as a decimal) of teaching assistants at each and summing the results: 0,6*17.000+0,4*18.000=17.400.

Step 5 : Work out the fractions of teachers and teaching assistants from the given ratio of 3:2: 3/(2+3)=3/5 are teachers and 2/(2+3)=2/5 are teaching asistants.

Step 6 : Find these fracations of the relevant average pay of both groups and add the results: (3/5)*31.250+(2/5)*17.400=25.710.

## Pregunta 181

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpaU09YaF8zd25rQXZGZ2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=8

### Enunciado

What is the average height of an adult in Colombia to the nearest 0,1cm?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0248-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 163
- **B.** 162,8
- **C.** 170,6
- **D.** 155
- **E.** 162,6

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 163

Step 1 : Find the percentage of women in the country by subtracting the percentage of men from 100: 100-51,5=48,5.

Step 2 : Find 51,5% of the average men's height: 0,515*170,6=87,859.

Step 3 : Find 48,5% of the average women's height: 0,485*155=75,175.

Step 4 : Add the two results: 87,859+75,175=163,034.

Step 5 : Round this to the nearest 0,1cm: 163.

## Pregunta 182

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpaU09YaF8zd25rQXZGZ2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=9

### Enunciado

The table above shows the budget allotted to selected departments in a large company. A report shows that the company budget increased by 15% between 2012 and 2013, and that spending on marketing made up 20% of all spending at the company in 2012. What percentage of the total budget for 2013 was allocated for staff costs in marketing?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0249-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 3,50%
- **B.** 3,75%
- **C.** 3%
- **D.** 3,25%
- **E.** 4%

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 3,50%

Step 1 : Find 20% as a decimal: 20/100=0,2.

Step 2 : Divide the 2012 Marketing budget by this to find the total budget for 2012 (in millions of Yen): 3,2/0,2=16.

Step 3 : Find the multiplier for a 15% increase: 1+(15/100)=1,15.

Step 4 : Multiply the 2012 budget by this multiplier to find the 2013 budget: 16*1,15=18,4.

Step 5 : Find the marketing budget as a percentage of this figure: (4,6/18,4)*100=25. Find 14 percent of 25 percent by multiplying 25 by the decimal 0,14%: 25*0,14%=3,5%

## Pregunta 183

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpaU09YaF8zd25rQXZGZ2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=10

### Enunciado

Each year Mr Jones's class sits a final exam on which they choose 1 of 3 questions to answer. What was the average score by students in Mr Jones's class in 2015?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0250-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 71,38
- **B.** 70
- **C.** 72,85
- **D.** 73,08
- **E.** 33,33

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 71,38

Step 1 : Find 32% of the average score of those who answered question 1 in 2015: 0,32*76=24,32.

Step 2 : Find 19% of the average score of those who answered question 2 in 2015: 0,19*62=11,78.

Step 3 : Find 49% of the average score of those who answered question 3 in 2015: 0,49*72=35,28.

Step 4 : Add the three results: 24,32+11,78+35,28=71,38.

## Pregunta 184

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpZRVZtdXJFOUlVY3U4Z2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=1

### Enunciado

How many of the companies in the table were net importers in 2017 (the value of their imports exceeded the value of their exports)?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0251-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 1
- **B.** 0
- **C.** 2
- **D.** 3
- **E.** 4

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 1

Step 1 : Sum the exports for each company in 2017: Commodities Ltd: 567+643=1.210, Imp-Exp Corp: 852+498=1.350, Retailor: 389+651=1.040, Silk Journey: 185+980=1.165.

Step 2 : Compare the value of the exports and the imports to see for how many countries the imports value was higher than the exports value:  imports 1.040< exports 1.116, so Realtailor is a net importer.

All the other companies are net exporters, so there is just one net importer.

## Pregunta 185

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpZRVZtdXJFOUlVY3U4Z2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=2

### Enunciado

In 2011, what was the approximate ratio of the total spending at Sovereign Science to the total spending at Medic Basics?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0252-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 4:5
- **B.** 3:5
- **C.** 1:2
- **D.** 5:6
- **E.** 3:4

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 4:5

Step 1 : Write 28% as a decimal:28/100=0,28.

Step 2 : find the total spending (in millions of euros) at Sovereign Science in 2011 by dividing the marketing spending by 0,28: 12.535/0,28=44.767,86 (to 2dp).

Step 3 : Write 35% as a decimal:35/100=0,35.

Step 4 : find the total spending (in millions of euros) at Medic Basics in 2011 by dividing the marketing spending by 0,35: 19.586/0,35=55.960.

Step 5 : Find the ratio between the two amounts as a decimal: 44.767,86/55.960=0,8 (to 2dp).

Step 6 : Write the ratio in the form 0,8:1

Step 7 : multiply both sides of the ratio by 5: 0,8:1=4:5.

## Pregunta 186

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpZRVZtdXJFOUlVY3U4Z2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=3

### Enunciado

The total number of admissions at Greenwood college increased by 176 each year between 2014 and 2016. In 2016 Greenwood had a target of 25% of their admissions being from disadvantaged backgrounds. How many more of the admissions at Greenwood would have needed to be from disadvantaged backgrounds in order for them to have met their target?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0253-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 363
- **B.** 352
- **C.** 358
- **D.** 382
- **E.** 376

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 363

Step 1 : Find the total number of admissions at Greenwood college in 2016 by adding twice the yearly increase to the 2014 admissions: 11.748+2*176=12.100.

Step 2 : Calculate the increase in percentage points needed to reach the target: 25-22=3.

Step 3 : find 3% of the total admissions in 2016: 0,03*12.100=363.

## Pregunta 187

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpZRVZtdXJFOUlVY3U4Z2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=4

### Enunciado

For which type of cookies is the ratio of the sales price to the total cost the highest?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0254-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** Peanut Butter
- **B.** Chocolate Chip
- **C.** Ginger Snap
- **D.** Triple Chocolate
- **E.** Nut & Raisin

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: Peanut Butter

Step 1 : Add the ingredient costs and packaging costs to find the total cost for each type: Chocolate chip: 5,25+0,25=5,5, Peanut Butter: 2,5+2=4,5, Ginger Snap: 3,75+1,25=5, Triple Chocolate: 5,25+0,75=6, Butterscotch: 5,5+1=6,5, Nut and Raisin: 3,5+0,5=4.

Step 2 : calculate the sale price to cost ratio by dividing the sale price by the costs for each type of cookies: Chocolate chip: 11/5,5=2, Peanut Butter: 13,5/4,5=3, Ginger Snap: 8/5=1,6, Triple Chocolate: 8/6=1,333..., Butterscotch: 9,75/6,5=1,5, Nut and Raisin: 10/4=2,5.

Step 3 : Compare these values to find the cookies with the highest value (peanut butter).

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpZRVZtdXJFOUlVY3U4Z2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=5

## Pregunta 189

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpZRVZtdXJFOUlVY3U4Z2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=6

### Enunciado

At which school is the ratio of students to teachers the lowest?

- **A.** Academy
- **B.** Primary School
- **C.** Secondary School
- **D.** College
- **E.** University

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: Academy

Step 2 : calculate the student to teacher ratio by dividing the number of students by the number of teachers at each school: Primary School: 96/3=32, Middle School: 180/6=30..., Secondary School: 130/4=32,5, College:110/11=10, University: 253/11=23, Academy: 38/4=9,5.

## Pregunta 190

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpZRVZtdXJFOUlVY3U4Z2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=7

### Enunciado

The total amount of energy used by Jenysys increased by 153.000 kWh each year between 2013 and 2015. In 2015 the company had a target of 40% of their energy used being from renewable sources. How many more kWh of the energy Jenysys used would have needed to be generated from renewable sources in order for them to have met their target?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0257-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 41.544
- **B.** 29.304
- **C.** 35.424
- **D.** 566.784
- **E.** 664.704

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 41.544

Step 1 : Find the total amount of energy by Jenysys in by adding twice the yearly increase to the 2013 figure: 732.600+(2*153.000)=1.038.600.

Step 2 : Calculate the increase in percentage points needed to reach the target: 40%-36%=4%.

Step 3 : find 4% of the total energy used in 2016: 0,04*1.038.600=41.544.

## Pregunta 191

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpZRVZtdXJFOUlVY3U4Z2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=8

### Enunciado

In 2015, what was the ratio of the total revenue of Monies Bank to the total revenue of DSB?

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0258-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 2:3
- **B.** 1:3
- **C.** 1:2
- **D.** 2:5
- **E.** 1:4

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 2:3

Step 1 : Write 1,5% as a decimal:1,5/100=0,015.

Step 2 : find the total revenue (in millions of euros) at Monies Bank in 2011 by dividing the revenue from fees by 0,015: 15,6/0,015=1.040.

Step 3 : Write 3,5% as a decimal:3,5/100=0,035.

Step 4 : find the total revenue (in millions of euros) at DSB in 2011 by dividing the revenue from fees by 0,035: 54,6/0,035=1.560.

Step 5 : write the two revenues as a ratio: 1.040:1.560.

Step 7 : Simplify the ratio by dividing both sides of the ratio by 520: (1.040/520):(1.560/520)=2:3

According to the table, how many megatonnes of the world's plastic waste was not recycled?

- **A.** 204,218
- **B.** 133,124
- **C.** 159,206
- **D.** 96,876
- **E.** 70,794

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 204,218
