Step 3 : Subtract the amount which was recycled from the total amount: 230,3-26,082=204,218

## Pregunta 194

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDoxTzM4MWpURWZVOHV0d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=1

### Enunciado

By how many euros must Agnieszka Zielinksa increase her sales from 2016-2017 to meet the target she has been set?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0261-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 24.000
- **B.** 16.000
- **C.** 8.000
- **D.** 10.666
- **E.** 24.0000

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 24.000

Step 1 : Convert 15% (Agnieszka's target) to a decimal: 15/100=0.15.

Step 2 : Multiply Agnieszka's 2016 total by this number to find 15% of this total: 0,15*160.000=24.000.

## Pregunta 195

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDoxTzM4MWpURWZVOHV0d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=2

### Enunciado

The percentage of Panjabi speakers who are first-generation immigrants is the same in British Columbia and Alberta. If there are 64.022 Panjabi speakers in British Columbia who are first-generation immigrants, how many Panjabi speakers in Alberta are first-generation immigrants?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0262-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 17.479
- **B.** 64.022
- **C.** 38.768
- **D.** 32.461
- **E.** 118.898

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 17.479

Step 1 : Find the percentage of Panjabi speakers who are first-generation immigrants: (64.022/182.920)*100=35%.

Step 2 : Find 35% of the number of Panjabi speakers in Alberta: 0,35*49.940=17.479.

## Pregunta 196

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDoxTzM4MWpURWZVOHV0d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=3

### Enunciado

A survey is conducted of the number of books a group of people read during the year 2016. In the 45-54 age group, 165 of the participants were men and the rest were women. The men aged 45-54 read an average of 9 books in the year 2016. What was the average number of books read by women in the survey aged 45-54?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0263-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 11,1
- **B.** 10,2
- **C.** 11,4
- **D.** 10,7
- **E.** 10,5

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 11,1

Step 1 : Find the number of women aged 45-54: 385-165=220.

Step 2 : Calculate the total number of books read by those aged 45-54 by multiplying the average number they read by the number in the sample: 10,2*385=3.927.

Step 3 : Calculate the total number of books read by the men aged 45-54 by multiplying the average number read by them by the number of men aged 45-54: 165*9=1.485.

Step 4 : subtract this from the total number read by those aged 45-54 to give the total number of books read by the women aged 45-54: 3.927-1.485=2.442.

Step 5 : Divide the total number of books read by the women aged 45-54 by the number of women aged 45-54 to give the average: 2.442/220=11,1.

## Pregunta 197

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDoxTzM4MWpURWZVOHV0d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=4

### Enunciado

Of those who booked a rafting holiday between 2012 and 2015, 65% booked a 7-day rafting package and 35% booked a 10-day rafting package. How many individual days of rafting were booked by customers between 2012 and 2015?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0264-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 2.511,6
- **B.** 1.729
- **C.** 1.330
- **D.** 3.800
- **E.** 2.660

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 2.511,6

Step 1 : Add the numbers of people who booked rafting holidays between 2012 and 2015 to find the total number: 83+93+57+79=312.

Step 2 : Find the number who booked 7-day rafting holidays by finding 65% of 312: 0,65*312=202,8.

Step 3 : Find the total number of days on the 7-day holidays by multiplying 202,8 by 7: 247*7=1.419,6

Step 4 : Find the number who booked 10-day rafting holidays by finding 35% of 312: 0,35*312=109,2.

Step 5 : Find the total number of days on the 10-day holidays by multiplying 109,2 by 10: 109,2*10= 1.092.

Step 6 : Add the number of days from the 7-day and 10-day holidays: 1.419,6 +1.092=2 511,6.

## Pregunta 198

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDoxTzM4MWpURWZVOHV0d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=5

### Enunciado

In 2014 The publisher released 10 fewer fiction books than in 2015. In 2014 the number of non-fiction books released by the publisher was 3 times higher than the number of fiction books. How many more non-fiction books were released in 2014 than in 2015.

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0265-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 17
- **B.** 27
- **C.** 34
- **D.** 37
- **E.** 22

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 17

Step 1 : Find the number of fiction books released in 2014 by subtracting 10 from the number released in 2015: 32-10=22.

Step 2 : Find the number of non-fiction books released in 2014 by multiplying the number of fiction books by 3: 22*3=66.

Step 3 : Find the difference between the number of non-fiction books released in 2014 and 2015: 66-49=17.

## Pregunta 199

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDoxTzM4MWpURWZVOHV0d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=6

### Enunciado

What was the mean average spending of business members (both associate and full) in 2016?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0266-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 20.400 €
- **B.** 8.700 €
- **C.** 60.384 €
- **D.** 30.808 €
- **E.** 23.600 €

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 20.400 €

Step 1 : Find the total number of business members in 2016: 98+50=148.

Step 2 : Find the total spent by business members in 2016: 3.019*1.000=3.019.000.

Step 3 : Divide the total spent by the total number of members: 3.019 000/148=20.400.

## Pregunta 200

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDoxTzM4MWpURWZVOHV0d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=7

### Enunciado

Between 1995 and 2015 the total number of civil servants employed at the administrative level across the 5 countries in the table decreased by 50%. If in 1995 there were 32.000 civil servants employed at the administrative level in Ireland, what percentage of the total number of administrative level civil servants in 1995 were employed in Ireland?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0267-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 16%
- **B.** 18%
- **C.** 15%
- **D.** 11%
- **E.** 9,50%

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 16%

Step 1 : Find the total number of administrative level civil servants, in thousands, employed in 2015: 13+41+11+21+14=100.

Step 2 : Find the multiplier associated with a 50% decrease: 1-(50/100)=0.5.

Step 3 : Divide the 2015 total (in thousands) by the multiplier to find the number of administrative civil servants in 2015: 100/0,5=200.

Step 4 : Write the fraction of these which were employed in Ireland: 32/200.

Step 5 : Convert this value into a percentage: 32/200=16/100=16%.

## Pregunta 201

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDoxTzM4MWpURWZVOHV0d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=8

### Enunciado

In 2015, how many Penguins were there at Aqualand?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0268-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 110
- **B.** 120
- **C.** 100
- **D.** 130
- **E.** 140

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 110

Step 1 : Divide the total amount of fish consumed at Aqualand by the amount of fish needed to feed 100 penguins: 605/550=1,1.

Step 2 : Multiply this number by 100: 1,1*100=110.

## Pregunta 202

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDoxTzM4MWpURWZVOHV0d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=9

### Enunciado

One quarter of all of the songs played on Today's music in the month were in the top 20 chart at the time of being played. Given that a total of 8.370 songs were not in the top 20 chart at the time of being played, how long on average did each song played on Today's Music last?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0269-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 3 minutes
- **B.** 2 minutes
- **C.** 4 minutes
- **D.** 5 minutes
- **E.** 6 minutes

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 3 minutes

Step 1 : Find the fraction of songs which were not in the top 20 when played: 1-(1/4)=3/4.

Step 2 : Find the total number of songs played in the month: (8.370/3)*4=11.160.

Step 3 : Find the total number of minutes for which music was played: 558*60=33.480.

Step 4 : Divide the total time in minutes by the number of songs: 33.480/11.160=3.

## Pregunta 203

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDoxTzM4MWpURWZVOHV0d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=10

### Enunciado

The average price of an opera ticket in 2000 was €120. This price increased by 10% each year between 2000 and 2003 (three years). By how much did the average price of an opera ticket increase between 2000 and 2003?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0270-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** €39,72
- **B.** 36,00 €
- **C.** 24,00 €
- **D.** 25,20 €
- **E.** 44,61 €

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: €39,72

Step 1 : Find the percentage multiplier associated with a 10% increase: 1+(10/100)=1,1.

Step 2 : Multiply the price in 2000 by this multiplier cubed to give the price for 2003: 120*1,1^3=159,72.

Step 3 : Subtract the 2000 ticket price from the 2003 price to find the increase in ticket price: 159,72-120=39,72

## Pregunta 204

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDoxTzM4MWpURWZVOHV0d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=11

### Enunciado

The average income tax paid by someone with a university degree in South Africa in 2011 was equivalent to 5.000 USD. This figure increased by 10% in each of the years between 2011 and 2014. In 2014 in South Africa there were 9.100.000 25-34 year-olds, 6.400.000 35-44 year-olds, 4.800.000 45-54 year-olds and 3.200.000 55-64 year-olds. What was the total of the income tax paid by all people with a university degree aged 25-64 in South Africa in 2014, measured in USD?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0271-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 10.055.705.000
- **B.** 9.821.500.000
- **C.** 7.819.625.000
- **D.** 10.947.475.000
- **E.** 12.511.400.000

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 10.055.705.000

Step 1 : Find the percentage multiplier for a 10% increase: 1+(10/100)=1.1. Setp 2: multiply the 2011 average tax by this value cubed to give the average 2014 tax: 5.000*1,1^3=6.655.

Step 3 : Find the number of people with degrees in each age group: 0,05*9.100.000=455.000 25-34 year-olds, 0,07*6.400.000=448.000 35-44 year-olds, 0,08*4.800.000=384.000 45-54 year-olds, 0,07*3.200.000=224.000 55-64 year-olds.

Step 4 : Add these figures to find the total number of people with a degree in South Africa in 2014: 455.000+448.000+384.000+224.000=1.511.000.

Step 5 : Multiply the total number of degree holders by the average tax paid by each: 1.511.000*6.655=10.055.705.000.

## Pregunta 205

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDoxTzM4MWpURWZVOHV0d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=12

### Enunciado

Between 2016 and 2017, the total spending by "Inspire Food" increased by 4%. By what percentage did the marketing spending funded by income from adverts at "Inspire Food" increase by between 2016 and 2017?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0272-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 30%
- **B.** 5%
- **C.** 7,50%
- **D.** 21%
- **E.** 29,70%

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 30%

Step 1 : Write 8% as a decimal:8/100=0,08.

Step 2 : find the total 2016 spending by dividing the marketing spending by 0,08:12.600/0,08=157.500.

Step 3 : Find the 2017 spending by increasing the 2016 spending by 4%: 1,04*157.500=163.800.

Step 4 : Find the marketing spending in 2017 by finding 9% of the total spending: 0,09*163.800=14.742.

Step 5 : Find the amount funded by advert income in 2016 by finding 45% of the spending on marketing: 0,45*12.600=5.670.

Step 6 : Find the amount funded by advert income in 2017 by finding 50% of the spending on marketing: 0,5*14.742=7.371.

Step 7 : Calculate the percentage difference between the 2016 and 2017 figure: ((7.371-5.670)/5.670)*100=30.

## Pregunta 206

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDoxTzM4MWpURWZVOHV0d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=13

### Enunciado

In 2014 the average house floor area in New Zealand was 60 square meters. Between 2014 and 2016 this value decreased by 5% each year. In 2016 there were 1,2 million people aged 18-34 in New Zealand, 1,8 million aged 35-64, and 0,9 million aged 65+. What was the total floor space in square meters of all the houses owned in New Zealand in 2016? (You may assume that each homeowner owns exactly one house)

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0273-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 131.909.400
- **B.** 131.544.000
- **C.** 121.512.600
- **D.** 91.296.900
- **E.** 158.388.750

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 131.909.400

Step 1 : Find the percentage multiplier for a 5% decrease: 1-(5/100)=0.95.

Step 2 : multiply the 2014 average floor area by this value squared to give the average 2016 floor area: 60*0,95^2=54,15.

Step 3 : Find the number of homeowners in each age group: 0,32*1.200.000=384.000 18-34 year-olds, 0,75*1.800.000=1.350.000 35-64 year-olds, 0,78*900.000=702.000 65+ year-olds.

Step 4 : Add these figures to find the total number of homeowners/houses owned in New Zealand: 384.000+1.350.000+702.000=2.436.000.

Step 5 : Multiply the total number of houses by the average floorspace: 2.436.000*54,15=131.909.400.

## Pregunta 207

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDoxTzM4MWpURWZVOHV0d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=14

### Enunciado

The Humanities department of a university is given a budget of €500.000 euros in 2013. This is cut by 3% each year between 2013 and 2015 (two years). What was the Humanities department's budget in 2015?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0274-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 470.450 €
- **B.** 470.000 €
- **C.** 8.561 €
- **D.** 467.350 €
- **E.** 8.764 €

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 470.450 €

Step 1 : Find the percentage multiplier associated with a 3% decrease: 1-(3/100)=0.97.

Step 2 : Multiply the budget from 2013 by this multiplier squared to give the budget for 2015: 500.000*(0,97)^2=470.450.

## Pregunta 208

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDoxTzM4MWpURWZVOHV0d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=15

### Enunciado

From 2016 onwards, beet sugar production in Poland is expected to decrease by 27% each year, while in the Netherlands it is expected to increase by 12% each year. In what year will the Netherlands first produce more sugar beet than Poland?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0275-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 2019
- **B.** 2018
- **C.** 2020
- **D.** 2021
- **E.** 2022

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 2019

Step 1 : Calculate the percentage multiplier for a 27% decrease: 1-(27/100)=0,73.

Step 2 : Calculate the percentage multiplier for a 12% increase: 1+(12/100)=1,12.

Step 3 : Write an expression for the total sugar beet produced in Poland n years after 2016: 13.524*0,73^n.

Step 4 : Write an expression for the total sugar beet produced in the Netherlands n years after 2016: 5.502*1,12^n.

Step 5 : substitute increasing values of n into the expression until one gives a higher total for the Netherlands than for Poland: 13.524*0,73^3=5.261 while 5.502*1,12^3=7.730.

Step 6 : Add this value of n to 2016 to find the year: 2016+3=2019.

## Pregunta 209

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDoxTzM4MWpURWZVOHV0d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=16

### Enunciado

The increase in full-time students between the years 2012 and 2016 was half the increase between 2008 and 2016. If the number of female full-time students remained constant between 2008 and 2012, how many male full-time students were there in 2012?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0276-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 46.995
- **B.** 92.025
- **C.** 34.425
- **D.** 33.970
- **E.** 7.920

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 46.995

Step 1 : Find the increase in full-time students between 2008 and 2016: 105.050-79.000=26.050.

Step 2 : Divide this by 2 to find the increase between 2012 and 2016: 26.050/2=13.025.

Step 3 : Find the number of full-time students in 2012 by subtracting this number from the figure for 2016: 105.050-13.025 = 92.025.

Step 4 : subtract the number of full-time female students in 2012 (the same as the number in 2008): 92.025-45.030=46.995.

## Pregunta 210

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDoxTzM4MWpURWZVOHV0d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=17

### Enunciado

In April, the number of visits to the rugby page of the website "maxsports" was 15% lower than the number of visits in March and the average length of each visit to the rugby page was 12% higher. What was the total browsing time spent by users on the rugby page in April?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0277-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 47.600
- **B.** 48.700
- **C.** 56.000
- **D.** 42.500
- **E.** 506.000

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 47.600

Step 1 : Find the average length of a visit in March to the rugby page by dividing the total browsing time by the total number of visits (both are measured in hundreds):500/800=0,625 hours.

Step 2 : Find the average length of a visit in April by increasing the average length of a visit in March by 12%: 1,12*0,625=0,7 hours.

Step 3 : Find the total number of visits in April by decreasing the number of visits in March by 15%: 800*0,85=680.

Step 4 : multiply these figures together to get the total browsing time in hundreds of hours: 0,7*680=476.

Step 5 : Multiply by 100 to find the total browsing time in hours: 476*100=47.600.

## Pregunta 211

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDoxTzM4MWpURWZVOHV0d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=18

### Enunciado

The table above shows the presence of contaminants in 4 water sources measured in parts per million. This is the number of grams of the substance that would be present in 1 million grams of water. How many grams of sulphate would be expected to be present in Spinney Lake if the total mass of the water in the lake is 20.000.000 grams?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0278-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 5.800
- **B.** 580
- **C.** 58
- **D.** 2.900
- **E.** 290

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 5.800

Step 1 : Find the factor by which 20.000.000 (20 million) is larger than 1.000.000 (1 million): 20.000.000/1.000.000=20.

Step 3 : Multiply the contaminatnt level by this factor: 290*20=5.800.

## Pregunta 212

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDoxTzM4MWpURWZVOHV0d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=19

### Enunciado

One fifth of the small mammals owned in Belgium in 2010 were rabbits and the rest were rodents. If one eighth of the rodents were rats, how many rodents which were not rats were owned in Belgium in 2010?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0279-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 1.652
- **B.** 1.888
- **C.** 472
- **D.** 236
- **E.** 413

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 1.652

Step 1 : Find the number of rabbits: 2.360/5=472.

Step 2 : Find the number of rodents: 2.360-472=1.888.

Step 3 : Find the number of rats: 1.888/8=236.

Step 4 : Find the number of rodents which are not rats: 1.888-236=1.652.

## Pregunta 213

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDoxTzM4MWpURWZVOHV0d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=20

### Enunciado

The total number of tickets sold across the three theme parks was 25% higher in 2017 than in 2015. Given that 16 Million tickets were sold at Culture City in 2015, what percentage of the tickets sold in 2015 were at Culture City?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0280-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 25%
- **B.** 27%
- **C.** 16%
- **D.** 38%
- **E.** 20%

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 25%

Step 1 : Find the total number of tickets, in millions, sold in 2017: 31+19+30=80.

Step 2 : Find the multiplier associated with a 25% increase: 1+(25/100)=1,25.

Step 3 : Divide the 2017 total (in millions) by the multiplier to find the number of tickets in 2015: 80/1,25=64.

Step 4 : Write the fraction of these which were sold at Culture City: 16/64=1/4.

Step 5 : Convert this value into a percentage: 1/4=25/100=25%.

## Pregunta 214

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDoxTzM4MWpURWZVOHV0d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=21

### Enunciado

In Tiegansville the number of victims of serious crimes per 10.000 inhabitants decreased by 0,3 victims between 2005 and 2015. What was the population of Tiegansville in 2005

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0281-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 50.000
- **B.** 64.000
- **C.** 60.000
- **D.** 56.000
- **E.** 54.000

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 50.000

Step 1 : Find the number of victims per 10.000 inhabitants in Tiegansville in 2005 by adding 0,3 to the figure for 2015: 2,5+0,3=2,8.

(Remember that the number of victims decreased from 2005 to 2015 which is why the number must be higher in 2005)

Step 2 : Divide the total number of victims in 2005 by the number of victims per 10.000: 14/2,8=5.

Step 3 : Multiply this answer by 10.000: 5*10.000=50.000.

## Pregunta 215

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDoxTzM4MWpURWZVOHV0d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=22

### Enunciado

The table shows average times and attendances for a weekly 5km run. Of the runners on the 18th February, 60 belonged to a running club, and these runners recorded an average time of 23,8 minutes. What was the average time of those who did not belong to a running club on the 18th February?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0282-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 25
- **B.** 25,8
- **C.** 25,2
- **D.** 25,4
- **E.** 25,6

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 25

Step 1 : Find the number of runners who were not in a club: 360-60=300.

Step 2 : Calculate the total of all times on this day by multiplying the average time by the number of runners: 24,8*360=8.928 minutes.

Step 3 : Calculate the total of the times recorded by running club members by multiplying the number of these members by their average time: 23,8*60=1.428.

Step 4 : subtract this from the total of all times to give the total of times recorded by those not in a running club: 8.928-1.428=7.500.

Step 5 : Divide the total time of non-members by the number of non-members to get their average time: 7.500/300=25.

## Pregunta 216

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDoxTzM4MWpURWZVOHV0d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=23

### Enunciado

The farm grew 5 tons more of peas in 2017 than in 2016. The farm also grew fruit in 2016 and 2017. They grew 10 tons more fruit in 2017 than in 2016. If the total weight of fruit grown in 2017 was 4 times the weight of the peas grown in 2017, how many tons of fruit were grown in 2016?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0283-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 38
- **B.** 48
- **C.** 28
- **D.** 18
- **E.** 58

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 38

Step 1 : Find the weight of peas grown in 2017 by adding 5 to the weight grown in 2016: 7+5=12 tons.

Step 2 : Find the weight of fruit grown in 2017 by multiplying the weight of the peas in tons by 4: 12*4=48.

Step 3 : Find the weight of fruit grown in 2016 by subtracting 10 from the weight of fruit grown in 2017: 48-10=38

## Pregunta 217

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDoxTzM4MWpURWZVOHV0d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=24

### Enunciado

What will be the decrease in the value of UK pharmaceutical exports between 2017 and 2018 if the value changes as predicted by the table?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0284-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 1.000.000.000 £
- **B.** 1.000.000 £
- **C.** 2.000.000.000 £
- **D.** 500.000.000 £
- **E.** 100.000.000 £

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 1.000.000.000 £

Step 1 : Convert 5% (the projected percentage decrease in pharmaceutical exports) to a decimal: 5/100=0,05.

Step 2 : Multiply the 2018 pharmaceutical exports value by this number to find 5% of this total: 0,05*20=1.

Step 3 : As this represents billions of pounds, multiply by 1 billion: 1 billion=1.000.000.000.

## Pregunta 218

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDoxTzM4MWpURWZVOHV0d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=25

### Enunciado

In 2013, 60% of the photos of puffins contained a single puffin, 25% contained 2 puffins and 15% contained 3 or more puffins. What is the minimum number of puffins which appear in photographs in year 2013?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0285-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 1.364
- **B.** 880
- **C.** 968
- **D.** 2.640
- **E.** 1.760

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 1.364

Step 1 : Find the number of photos with one puffin in by finding 60% of 880: 0,6*880=528. There are 528 puffins in these photos.

Step 2 : Find the number of photos with two puffins in by finding 25% of 880: 0,25*880=220.

Step 3 : Find the total number of puffins appearing in photos with 2 puffins in by multiplying 220 by 2: 2*220=440.

Step 2 : Find the number of photos with three of more puffins in by finding 15% of 880: 0,15*880=132.

Step 5 : Find the minimum number of puffins appearing in photos with 3 or more puffins in by multiplying 132 by 3: 3*132=396.

Step 6 : Add the number of puffins appearing in photos with 1 and 2 puffins to the minimum number in photos with 3 or more puffins: 528+440+396=1.364. Note: We do not know how many puffins there are in total in the photos in the group "3 or more" which is why we have to say minimum.

## Pregunta 219

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDoxTzM4MWpURWZVOHV0d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=26

### Enunciado

One quarter of the "technical" staff in Szczecin hospitals in 2016 worked at the East General Hospital Of these, one third were MRI technicians. How many Pharmaceutical Staff worked at the East General Hospital in 2016?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0286-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 14
- **B.** 7
- **C.** 21
- **D.** 13
- **E.** 8

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 14

Step 1 : Work out how many technical staff worked at the East General Hospital in 2016 by finding one quarter of 84: 84/4=21.

Step 2 : work out how many of these were MRI technicians by finding one third of 21: 21/3=7.

Step 3 : Work out how many of the East General technical staff were pharmaceutical staff: 21-7=14.

## Pregunta 220

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDoxTzM4MWpURWZVOHV0d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=27

### Enunciado

Between 2016 and 2017, the spending at Urban Food increased by 3%. By what percentage did the total inventory spending being funded by savings at Urban Food decrease by between 2016 and 2017?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0287-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 15,54%
- **B.** 20%
- **C.** 2,50%
- **D.** 12,20%
- **E.** 5,60%

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 15,54%

Step 1 : Write 80% as a decimal:80/100=0,8.

Step 2 : find the total spending (in euros) in 2016 by dividing the inventory spending by 0,8: 39.000/0,8=48.750.

Step 3 : Find the total spending (in euros) in 2017 by increasing the total 2016 spending by 3%: 1,03*48.750=50.212,5.

Step 4 : Find the total inventory spending (in euros) in 2017 by finding 82% of the total spending: 0,82*50.212,5=41.174,25.

Step 5 : Find the total inventory spending (in euros) funded by savings in 2016 by finding 5% of the inventory spending in 2016: 0,05*39.000=1.950.

Step 6 : Find the total inventory spending (in euros) funded by savings in 2017 by finding 4% of the inventory spending in 2017: 0,04*41.174,25=1.647.

Step 7 : Calculate the percentage difference between the 2016 and 2017 figure: ((1.950-1.647)/1.950)*100=15,54.

## Pregunta 221

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDoxTzM4MWpURWZVOHV0d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=28

### Enunciado

Participants in several UK cities take part in a competition to track their steps for charity. The total number of participants doubled, while the total number of steps increased by 25% between week 5 and week 6. By how much did the average number of steps per participant decrease between week 5 and week 6?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0288-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 36.000
- **B.** 10.000
- **C.** 22.500
- **D.** 30.000
- **E.** 48.000

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 36.000

Step 1 : Calculate the average number of steps per participant in week 6: (480/80)*10 000=60 000.

Step 2 : calculate the number of participants in week 5: 80/2=40.

Step 3 : Calculate the percentage multiplier for a 25% increase: 1+(25/100)=1.25.

Step 4 : Calculate the number of steps (in tens of thousands) in week 5: 480/1.25=384.

Step 5 : Calculate the average number of steps per participant in week 5: (384/40)*10 000=96 000.

Step 6 : Calculate the decrease in the number of steps per participant: 96 000-60 000=36 000.

## Pregunta 222

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDoxTzM4MWpURWZVOHV0d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=29

### Enunciado

Based on the data in the table, what would be the predicted turnover in pounds of a retailer who sold 10.000 CDs and 5000 Vinyls in 2015?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0289-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 205.000
- **B.** 100.000
- **C.** 250.000
- **D.** 246.881
- **E.** 150.000

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 205.000

Step 1 : Find the average turnover per CD sold by dividing the turnover from CDs by the number of CDs sold: 225.000/22.500=10.

Step 2 : Use this to work out the estimated turnover from selling 10.000 CDs: 10*10.000=100.000.

Step 3 : Find the average turnover per vinyl sold by dividing the turnover from vinyls by the number of vinyls sold: 672.000/32.000=21.

Step 4 : Use this to work out the estimated turnover from selling 5.000 vinyls: 21*5 000=105.000.

Step 5 : Add the estimated turnover from the CDs and vinyls to find the total estimated turnover: 100.000+105.000=205.000

## Pregunta 223

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDoxTzM4MWpURWZVOHV0d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=30

### Enunciado

Between 2010 and 2015, the spending at Emporium Science increased by 2%. By what percentage did the total research spending being funded by investors at Emporium Science increase by between 2010 and 2015?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0290-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 32,60%
- **B.** 27,50%
- **C.** 4,20%
- **D.** 25%
- **E.** 30%

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 32,60%

Step 1 : Write 16% as a decimal:16/100=0,16.

Step 2 : find the total spending (in millions of euros) in 2010 by dividing the research spending by 0,16:96.850/0,16=605.312,5.

Step 3 : Find the total spending (in millions of euros) in 2015 by increasing the total 2010 spending by 2%: 1,02*605.312,5=617.418,75.

Step 4 : Find the total research spending (in millions of euros) in 2015 by finding 20% of the total spending: 0,2*617.418,75=123.483,75.

Step 5 : Find the total research spending (in millions of euros) funded by investors in 2010 by finding 75% of the research spending in 2010: 0,75*96.850=72.637,5.

Step 6 : Find the total research spending (in millions of euros) funded by investors in 2015 by finding 78% of the research spending in 2015: 0,78*123.483,75=96.317,325.

Step 7 : Calculate the percentage difference between the 2010 and 2015 figure: ((96.317,325-72.637,5)/72.637,5)*100=32,6.

## Pregunta 224

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDoxTzM4MWpURWZVOHV0d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=31

### Enunciado

From 2012 onwards the growth in renewable energy in Poland each year was 10%. From 2012 onwards the growth in renewable energy in the UK was 16% each year. In what year was the amount of renewable energy generated in the UK first greater than the amount of renewable energy generated in Poland?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0291-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 2016
- **B.** 2017
- **C.** 2015
- **D.** 2014
- **E.** 2013

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 2016

Step 1 : Calculate the percentage multiplier for a 10% increase: 1+(10/100)=1,1.

Step 2 : Calculate the percentage multiplier for a 16% increase: 1+(16/100)=1,16.

Step 3 : Write an expression for the total renewable energy generated in Poland n years after 2012: 8.478*1,1^n.

Step 4 : Write an expression for the total renewable energy generated in the UK n years after 2012: 7.095*1,16^n.

Step 5 : substitute increasing values of n into the expression until one gives a higher total for the UK than for Poland: 8.478*1,1^4=12.412,6 while 7.095*1,16^4=12.846,5

Step 6 : Add this value of n to 2012 to find the year: 2012+4=2016.

## Pregunta 225

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDoxTzM4MWpURWZVOHV0d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=32

### Enunciado

On Monday Melissa spent 30 minutes of her weights time completing bench presses and the rest of the time using free weights. She split her cardio time between using a treadmill and using an elliptical trainer. She was using the treadmill for the same amount of time as she was using the free weights. How long was she using the elliptical trainer for?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0292-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 1 hour 45 minutes
- **B.** 45 minutes
- **C.** 1 hour 55 minutes
- **D.** 2 hours
- **E.** 1 hour 30 minutes

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 1 hour 45 minutes

Step 1 : Convert 30 minutes to a decimal hour form: 30/60=0,5.

Step 2 : Find the time spent on free weights by subtracting the time spent completing bench presses from the total weights time: 1,25-0,5=0,75.

Step 3 : Find the time spent on the elliptical machine by subtracting the time spent on the treadmill (0,75 hours) from the cardio time: 2.5-0.75=1.75.

Step 4 : convert this time into hours and minutes: 1,75hours= 1 hour +0,75*60 minutes=1 hour 45 minutes.

## Pregunta 226

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDoxTzM4MWpURWZVOHV0d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=33

### Enunciado

Between 2014 and 2015, the total number of cases taken by Jenkins and Associates increased by 5%. By what percentage did the total number of appeals cases which resulted in a win increase at Jenkins and Associates between 2014 and 2015?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0293-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 35%
- **B.** 30%
- **C.** 32%
- **D.** 36%
- **E.** 37%

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 35%

Note that the question asks for appeal cases won:

From the table we are able to find the number of appeal cases which resulted in a win at Jenkins and Associates for year 2014. We do that by:

Step 1:  First finding the number of total cases in 2014 which is 700 and multiplying it with the percentage of the appeal cases: 700 * 0,07 = 49

Step 2: Now we need to calculate how many of those appeal cases were won: 49*0,6= 29,4

Now we need to do the same for 2015.

Step 4: We do not know how many total cases there were in 2015 but we do know there were 5% more than in 2015. We can therefore conclude that 700*1,05=735

step 5: Calculate how many appeal cases there were in 2015 by multiplying the total number of cases with the Appeals cases percent for 2014:  735 * 0,12= 88,2

step 6: Calculate number of appeal cases won in 2015 by multiplying the number of appeals cases for 2014 (above) by the percentage of appeals cases won  88,2* 0,45=39,7

Step 7: Calculate percentage by which the total number of appeals cases which resulted in a win increased at Jenkins and Associates between 2014 and 2015:  39,7/29,4=1,35 which is equal to 35% more appeal cases won in 2015

## Pregunta 227

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDoxTzM4MWpURWZVOHV0d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=34

### Enunciado

Based on the data in the table, what was the total number of inhabitants in India in 2015?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0294-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 1.350.000.000
- **B.** 337.600.000
- **C.** 150.000.000
- **D.** 1.332.000.000
- **E.** 1.688.000.000

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 1.350.000.000

Step 1 : Write out the total number of bicycles in India by multiplying the figure in the table by 1 million: 337,5*1.000.000=337.500.000.

Step 2 : Divide the total amount of bicycles in India by the number of bikes per 100 inhabitants: 337.500 000/25=13.500.000.

Step 3 : Multiply this number by 100: 13.500.000*100=1.350.000.000. Another way of solving the question is to simply multiply the number of bicycles by 4 as we can see that every fourth person in India owns a bicycle (25 of 100) hence 4*337.500.000 = 1.350.000.000 (rounded down)

## Pregunta 228

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDoxTzM4MWpURWZVOHV0d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=35

### Enunciado

The percentage of France's gold medals which were in Judo was the same at the 2016 and 1996 Olympics. If France won 3 gold medals in Judo at the 1996 Olympics, how many gold medals in Judo did they win at the 2016 Olympics?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0295-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 2
- **B.** 3
- **C.** 1
- **D.** 4
- **E.** 5

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 2

Step 1 : Find the percentage of France's gold medals which were in Judo at the 1996 Olympics: (3/15)*100=20%.

Step 2 : Find 20% of the total number of gold medals won by Frane at the 2016 Olympics: 0,2*10=2.

## Pregunta 229

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDoxTzM4MWpURWZVOHV0d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=36

### Enunciado

The volume of diesel sold at V11 Garage in 2017 is 20% higher than the volume sold in 2016. The average cost of diesel at V11 Garage in 2017 is 25% higher than the average cost sold in 2016. What is the turnover from diesel sales at V11 Garage in 2017?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0296-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 2.700.000
- **B.** 2.160.000
- **C.** 2.250.000
- **D.** 1.728.000
- **E.** 1.875.000

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 2.700.000

Step 1 : Find the average cost of diesel sold in 2016 by dividing the 2016 turnover from diesel by the 2016 volume for diesel (both are measured in thousands):18/15=1,2. 18.

Step 2 : Find the average cost of diesel sold in 2017 by increasing the cost in 2016 by 25%: 1,2*1,25=1,5.

Step 3 : Find the volume of diesel sold in 2017 by increasing the amount sold in 2016 by 20%: 15*1,2=18.

Step 4 : multiply these figures together to get the turnover in hundreds of thousands of pounds: 18*1,5=27.

Step 5 : write this out in full: 27 hundred thousand=2.700.000.

## Pregunta 230

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDoxTzM4MWpURWZVOHV0d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=37

### Enunciado

The table above shows data on the rate of defects in cell phones produced at a factory. Suppose that in a particular week, 1.000.000 phone of the "X2" model are produced. How many would be predicted to have a defect that was NOT an aesthetic issue?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0297-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 520
- **B.** 710
- **C.** 190
- **D.** 52
- **E.** 19

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 520

Step 1 : Calculate the rate of defects which are not aesthetic issues by subtracting the rate of aesthetic issues per 10.000 defects from the total rate: 7,1-1,9=5,2.

Step 2 : Find the factor by which 1.000.000 is larger than 10.000: 1.000.000/10.000=100.

Step 3 : Multiply the rate by this factor: 5,2*100=520.

## Pregunta 231

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDoxTzM4MWpURWZVOHV0d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=38

### Enunciado

Based on the data in the table, what would be the predicted turnover in euros of the restaurant on a day in 2017 where it sold only 200 pizzas and 50 lasagna mains?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0298-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 2.850
- **B.** 2.200
- **C.** 650
- **D.** 3.000
- **E.** 2.831

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 2.850

Step 1 : Find the average turnover per pizza sold by dividing the turnover from selling pizzas by the number of pizzas sold: 683.705/62.155=11.

Step 2 : Use this to work out the estimated turnover from selling 200 pizzas: 11*200=2.200.

Step 3 : Find the average turnover per lasagna sold by dividing the turnover from selling lasagnas by the number of lasagnas sold: 157.274/12.098=13.

Step 4 : Use this to work out the estimated turnover from selling 50 lasagnas: 13*50=650.

Step 5 : Add the estimated turnover from the pizzas and lasagnas to find the total estimated turnover: 2.200+650=2.850

## Pregunta 232

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDoxTzM4MWpURWZVOHV0d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=39

### Enunciado

The increase in services employees between 1985 and 2000 in Estonia was double the increase between 2000 and 2015. If the number of services employees who were men remained constant between 1985 and 2000, how many women were employed in the services sector in 1985?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0299-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 186.736
- **B.** 261.972
- **C.** 259.640
- **D.** 260.820
- **E.** 128.654

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 186.736

Step 1 : Find the increase in services employees between 2000 and 2015: 480.684-407.780=72.904.

Step 2 : Multiply this by 2 to find the increase between 1985 and 2000: 72.904*2=145.808.

Step 3 : Find the number of services employees by subtracting this number from the figure for 2000: 407.780-145.808 = 261.972.

Step 4 : subtract the number of employees who were men in 1985 (the same as the number who were men in 2000): 261.972-75.236=186.736.

## Pregunta 233

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDoxTzM4MWpURWZVOHV0d2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=40

### Enunciado

What was the mean attendance at events at the Olympic Stadium in 2015?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0300-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 63.000
- **B.** 84.913
- **C.** 87.000
- **D.** 66.000
- **E.** 64.000

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 63.000

Step 1 : Find the total attendance at events at the Olympic Stadium in 2015: 46+10+6=62.

Step 2 : Find the total attendance at the Olympic Stadium in 2015: 3.906*1.000=3.906.000.

Step 3 : Divide the total attendance by the total number of events: 3.906.000/62=63.000.

## Pregunta 234

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDprSFdqYkw3ZkxFd3Vod2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=1

### Enunciado

Between 1990 and 2011, total emissions dropped by 20%. Between 1990 and 2000, the reduction in waste emissions was 7,5 percentage points greater than between 2000 and 2011. What percentage of total emissions was from waste in 1990?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0301-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 3,9%
- **B.** 2,8%
- **C.** 3,0%
- **D.** 3,2%
- **E.** 3,6%

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **E**: 3,6%

Step 1 – Calculate the total emissions in 2011

1.501,0 + 562,8 + 913,9 + 328,0 + 441,3 + 133,0 + 299,0 = 4.179

Step 2 – Calculate the total emissions in 1990

4.179/0,80 = 5.223,75

Step 3 – Calculate the emissions from waste in 2000 and 1990

2000: 133,0/0,875 = 152 (100 – 12,5 = 87,5%)

1990: 12,5 + 7,5 = 20%

152/0,8 = 190 (100 – 20 = 80%)

Step 4 – Calculate the percentage of total from waste

190/5.223,75 x 100 = 3,630284213 (3,6%)

## Pregunta 235

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDprSFdqYkw3ZkxFd3Vod2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=2

### Enunciado

In the world as a whole, GDP grew by 50% between 2005 and 2012 while GDP per capita grew by 25%. By how much has the population of the world grown in the same period?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0302-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 5%
- **B.** 20%
- **C.** 50%
- **D.** 12,5%
- **E.** 2%

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **B**: 20%

Step 1 – Work out the GDP of the world in 2005

55.500.000/1,5 = 37.000.000 million

Step 2 – Work out the GDP per capita in 2005

7.500/1,25 = 6.000

Step 3 – Calculate the population of the world in 2005 and 2012

2005: 37.000.000/6.000 = 6.166,666667 million

2012: 55.500.000/7.500 = 7.400 million

Step 4 – Calculate the difference in world population

7.400/6.166,666667 = 1,2 (20%)

Tip: GDP/population = GDP per capita so GDP/GDP per capita = population.

So as a result, the increase (or decrease) in population is a factor of GDP/GDP per capita.

If the GDP grew by 50% and the GDP per capita grew by 25%, then the population grew by 1,5/1,25 = 1,2 (20%).

## Pregunta 236

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDprSFdqYkw3ZkxFd3Vod2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=3

### Enunciado

If Ireland’s bovine population decreased by 3% between 2006 and 2011, how much did the dairy cow population change in the same period?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0303-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 13,9%
- **B.** 3,0%
- **C.** 6,7%
- **D.** 7,2%
- **E.** 12,4%

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **D**: 7,2%

Step 1 – Calculate the bovine population in 2006 and 2011 in Ireland

2006: 2.180/0,344 = 6.337,209302 thousand

2011: 6.337,209302 x 0,97 = 6.147,093023 thousand

Step 2 – Calculate the number of cows in Ireland in 2011

6.147,093023 x 0,358 = 2.200,659302 thousand

Step 3 – Calculate the dairy cow population in 2006 and 2011

2006: 2.180 x 0,469 = 1.022,42

2011: 2.200,659302 x 0,498 = 1.095,928332

Step 4 – Calculate the % change in dairy cows in Ireland between 2006 and 2011

1.095,928332/1.022,42 = 1,071896415 (7,2%)

## Pregunta 237

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDprSFdqYkw3ZkxFd3Vod2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=4

### Enunciado

In 2013 compared to 2012, employment in Bulgaria for the age group 20 to 64 years increased by 640.000. How many more people would have needed to be employed in 2012 in Bulgaria to meet the 2020 target?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0304-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 2.080.000
- **B.** 14.720.000
- **C.** 16.000.0000
- **D.** 2.240.000
- **E.** 16.640.000

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **E**: 16.640.000

Step 1 – Work out how much 640.000 people represents as a percentage

640.000 = 0,5% (63,5 – 63,0)

1% is therefore 640.000/0,5 = 1.280.000

Step 2 – Work out the percentage points difference in employment between 2012 and the 2020 target

76,0 – 63,0 = 13,0%

Step 3 – Work out how many people this represents

1.280.000 x 13,0 = 16.640.000

## Pregunta 238

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDprSFdqYkw3ZkxFd3Vod2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=5

### Enunciado

What was the percentage increase in the spending on commercial construction between 2003 and 2007, given that the increase between 2006 and 2007 was 20%?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0305-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 140%
- **B.** 240%
- **C.** 120%
- **D.** 80%
- **E.** 60%

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 140%

The question is best answered by using percentage multipliers.

Step 1 : Find the multiplier for the increase between 2003 and 2006: 24/12=2.

Step 2 : Find the multiplier for a 20% increase: 1+(20/100)=1,2.

Step 3 : multiply these multipliers together: 2*1,2=2,4.

Step 4 : convert this multiplier to a percentage increase: 100(2,4-1)=140.

## Pregunta 239

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDprSFdqYkw3ZkxFd3Vod2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=6

### Enunciado

What was the difference in crimes reported by the police between Germany and Belgium in 2007?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0306-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 448%
- **B.** 499%
- **C.** 600%
- **D.** 399%
- **E.** 500%

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **E**: 500%

Step 1 – Calculate the crimes recorded by police in Belgium in 2007

1.030.000/1,03 = 1.000.000

(index 103=1,03)

Step 2 – Calculate the crimes recorded by police in Germany in 2007

5.640.000/0,94 = 6.000.000

(index 94=0,94)

Step 3 – Calculate the % difference in crimes recorded between Germany and Belgium

6.000.000/1.000.000 = 6 which equals a 500% increase

Note that the columns for 2008,2009 and 2010 are index numbers as stated above the columns.

## Pregunta 240

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDprSFdqYkw3ZkxFd3Vod2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=7

### Enunciado

How much more did residents of Denmark spend on trips to Spain per night than residents of Belgium?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0307-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** €4,9
- **B.** €36
- **C.** €48
- **D.** €260
- **E.** €12

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **B**: €36

Step 1 – Work out the total nights spent in Spain and the total expenditure in Spain by residents of Belgium and then the average spend per night

Nights: 87.856 x 0,868 x 0,107 = 8.159,713856 thousand

Expenditure: 6.987 x 0,91 x 0,094 = 597,66798 million

Average spend/per night: 597,66798/8.159,713856 x 1.000 = 73,2461935

Step 2 – Work out the total nights spent in Spain and the total expenditure in Spain by residents of Denmark

Nights: 1.254.095 x 0,548 x 0,08 = 54.979,5248 thousand

Expenditure: 116.186 x 0,624 x 0,083 = 6.017,505312 million

Average spend/per night: 6.017,505312/54.979,5248 x 1.000 = 109,4499331

Step 3 – Calculate the difference between the average spend between Denmark and Belgium

109,4499331 – 73,2461935 = 36,20373961

## Pregunta 241

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDprSFdqYkw3ZkxFd3Vod2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=8

### Enunciado

What was the percentage increase in users of the tennis courts from January to April, given that the increase in users between March and April was 100%?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0308-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 200%
- **B.** 300%
- **C.** 150%
- **D.** 75%
- **E.** 400%

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 200%

The question is best answered by using percentage multipliers.

Step 1 : Find the multiplier for the increase between January and March: 30/20=1,5.

Step 2 : Find the multiplier for a 100% increase: 1+(100/100)=2.

Step 3 : multiply these multipliers together: 1,5*2=3.

Step 4 : convert this multiplier to a percentage increase: 100(3-1)=200.

Remember: a three fold increase does not equal 300% but a 200% increase.

## Pregunta 242

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDprSFdqYkw3ZkxFd3Vod2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=9

### Enunciado

What was the percentage difference in the average number of passengers per flight in Frankfurt Main between 2009 and 2010?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0309-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 5,95% increase
- **B.** 2,90% increase
- **C.** 5,36% increase
- **D.** 5,95% decrease
- **E.** 5,36% decrease

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **C**: 5,36% increase

Step 1 – Calculate the total air transport in 2009 in Frankfurt

52.646/1,041 = 50.572,52642 thousand

Step 2 – Calculate the total number of passenger flights in Frankfurt in 2009

100 – 1,2 = 98,8%

433/0,988 = 438,2591093 thousand

Step 3 – Calculate the average number of passengers per flight in 2009 and 2010

2009: 50.572,52642/438,2591093 = 115,394125

2010: 52.646/433 = 121,5842956

Step 4 – Calculate the percentage difference between 2009 and 2010

121,5842956/115,394125 = 1,053643724 (5,36% increase)

## Pregunta 243

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDprSFdqYkw3ZkxFd3Vod2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=10

What was the percentage growth in spending on full-time unemployment benefits between 2008 and 2011?

## Pregunta 244

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDprSFdqYkw3ZkxFd3Vod2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=11

### Enunciado

Given that people aged 0-24 years make up 15% of the total EU population and people aged 25-54 years make up 65% of the total EU population, what percentage of the total who used the internet at least once a week were aged 55+?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0311-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 19,1%
- **B.** 9,0%
- **C.** 29,2%
- **D.** 10,3%
- **E.** 4,6%

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **B**: 9,0%

Step 1 – Calculate the percentage of people aged 55+ in the EU and from this the number

100 – (15 + 65) = 20%

505.730.000 x 0,2 = 101.146.000

Step 2 – Calculate the percentage of people aged 55+ who use the internet at least once a week

101.146.000 x 0,325 = 32.872.450

Step 3 – Work out total individuals who use the internet at least once a week

505.730.000 x 0,72 = 364.125.600

Step 4 – Work out the % of total who use internet at least once a week who are aged 55+

32.872.450/364.125.600 x 100 = 9,027777778%

## Pregunta 245

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDprSFdqYkw3ZkxFd3Vod2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=12

### Enunciado

What was the percentage change in nights spent across both tourist accommodation and hotels in Belgium between 2012 and 2013?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0312-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** -9,2%
- **B.** -90,8%
- **C.** -9,1%
- **D.** -10,2%
- **E.** 10,2%

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: -9,2%

Step 1 – Calculate the nights spent in tourist accommodation establishments in 2012 in Belgium

1.665/0,9 = 1.850 (100 – 10 = 90%)

Step 2 – Calculate the nights spent in hotels in 2012 in Belgium

100 – 8,2 = 91,8%

1.243/0,918 = 1.354,030501

Step 3 – Total the nights spent in both forms of accommodation in 2012 and 2013

2012: 1.850 + 1.354,030501 = 3.204,030501 nights

2013: 1.665 + 1.243 = 2.908 nights

Step 4 – Calculate the percentage change between 2012 and 2013

2.908/3.204,030501 x 100 = 90,76068405%

100 - 90,76068405 = 9,239315946% decrease

## Pregunta 246

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDprSFdqYkw3ZkxFd3Vod2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=13

Given that tax revenue increased by 35% in Spain between 2002 and 2012, what was the change in GDP in Spain in the same period

## Pregunta 247

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDprSFdqYkw3ZkxFd3Vod2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=14

In 2013, the seven countries in the table below accounted for 77% of total EU28 CO2 emissions in 2013. Total emissions in 2012 were 3.439 million tons. What percentage did these countries make up of the total in 2012?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0314-01.png)

- **A.** 72%
- **B.** 76%
- **C.** 78%
- **D.** 73%
- **E.** 79%

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **B**: 76%

Step 1 – Calculate the emissions in these countries in 2012

Germany :760/1,02 = 745,0980392

UK : 455/0,976 = 466,1885246 (100 – 2,4 = 97,6)

France : 346/1,006 = 343,9363817

Italy : 342/0,934 = 366,1670236

Poland : 290/1,003 = 289,1326022

Spain : 224/0,874 = 256,2929062

Netherlands : 162/.997 = 162,4874624

## Pregunta 248

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDprSFdqYkw3ZkxFd3Vod2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=15

### Enunciado

Between 2010 and 2012, expenditure on public order and safety in Switzerland grew by 21%, a 0,1 percentage point increase in GDP and a 1 percentage point increase in total expenditure. What was total government expenditure as a percentage of GDP in Switzerland in 2012?

Step 2 – Calculate the GDP in Switzerland in 2012

1,71 + 0,1 = 1,81%

8.609,15/0,0181 = 475.643,6464 million

Step 3 – Calculate total government expenditure in Switzerland in 2012

5,07 +1,0 = 6,07%

8.609,15/0,0607 = 141.831,1367 million

Step 4 – Calculate total government expenditure as a % of GDP in Switzerland in 2012

141.831,1367/475.643,6464 x 100 = 29,81878089 (29,8%)
