## Pregunta 250

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDprSFdqYkw3ZkxFd3Vod2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=17

### Enunciado

In 2011 in the EU28, 3 in 25 people were aged 15 – 24 while in Algeria 1 in 5 people were aged 15 – 24 years. In 2006 in EU28 the share of the population aged 15 – 24 was 0,6 percentage points higher than in 2011 while in Algeria the share of the population aged 15 – 24 was 2 percentage points higher than in 2011. How much bigger was the EU28 than Algeria in 2006?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0317-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 16 times
- **B.** 15 times
- **C.** 14 times
- **D.** 13 times
- **E.** 12 times

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **B**: 15 times

Step 1 – Work out the percentage share of population aged 15 – 24 in EU and Algeria in 2011

EU: 3 in 25 = 12 in 100 = 12%

Algeria: 1 in 5 = 20 in 100 = 20%

Step 2 – Work out the percentage share of population aged 15 – 24 in EU and Algeria in 2006

EU: 12 + 0,6 = 12,6%

Algeria: 20 + 2 = 22%

Step 3 – Calculate the total population in EU and Algeria in 2006

EU: 62.748/0,126 = 498.000

Algeria: 7.304/0,22 = 33.200

Step 4 – Work out how much bigger the EU was than Algeria in 2006

498.000/33.200 = 15 times

## Pregunta 251

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDprSFdqYkw3ZkxFd3Vod2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=18

### Enunciado

Given that the EU population grew by 2,5% between 2005 and 2010, how many prisoners were there in the EU in 2005?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0318-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 590.000
- **B.** 650.000
- **C.** 600.000
- **D.** 620.000
- **E.** 580.000

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **C**: 600.000

Step 1 – Calculate the EU population in 2010

Number of prisoners/(population/100.000) = number of prisoners per 100.000 inhabitants

So number of prisoners/number of prisoners per 100.000

inhabitants = population/100.000

635.000/127 = 5.000 (which is 500 million inhabitants)

Step 2 – Calculate the EU population in 2005

5000/1,025 = 4.878,04878 (which is 487,804878 million inhabitants)

1,025 here represents the 2,5% population growth. Remember, we need to find the number that grew by 2,5% and resulted in 5000 million inhabitants. So, X *1,025 =5000 million. That is done by dividing the result (5000) with the percent it grow by. Not by simply multiplying 5000 with (1-0,025)

Step 3 – Calculate the number of prisoners in 2005

Number of prisoners per 100.000 inhabitants x population/100.000 = number of prisoners

So 123 x 4.878,04878 = 600.000

Note: You can also work this out as follows: 127/123 = 1,032520325 % change in prison population per 100.000 between 2005 and 2010

1,032520325 x 1,025 = 1,058333333 combined with the percentage change in population between 2005 and 2010

635.000/1,058333333 = 600.000

## Pregunta 252

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDprSFdqYkw3ZkxFd3Vod2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=19

### Enunciado

In Belgium in 2012, the average length of domestic trips was 4,26 nights. What was the average length of non-domestic trips?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0319-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 3,5 nights
- **B.** 8,3 nights
- **C.** 4,7 nights
- **D.** 7,1 nights
- **E.** 7,9 nights

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **E**: 7,9 nights

Step 1 – Calculate the total number of trips and from this the number of non-domestic trips

2.750/0,22 = 12.500

12.500 x 0,78 = 9.750 (100 – 22 = 78%)

Or 12.500 – 2.750 = 9.750

Step 2 – Calculate the total number of nights and from this the number of non-domestic nights

11.715/0,132 = 88.750

88.750 x 0,868 = 77.035 (100 – 13,2 = 86,8%)

Or 88.750 – 11.715 = 77.035

Step 3 – Calculate the average number of nights spent on non-domestic trips

77.035/9.750 = 7,901025641 nights

## Pregunta 253

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDprSFdqYkw3ZkxFd3Vod2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=20

### Enunciado

Of the total 250.000 million vehicle loads in Poland in 2007, 50.000 million were for international transport with an average vehicle load of 15,4 tonnes. The remaining vehicle loads were for national transport. What was the average vehicle load for national transport in Poland in 2007?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0320-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 15,8 tonnes
- **B.** 11,7 tonnes
- **C.** 13,6 tonnes
- **D.** 12,2 tonnes
- **E.** 14,1 tonnes

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **D**: 12,2 tonnes

Step 1 – Calculate the total vehicle loads in tonnes in Poland in 2007

12,84 x 250.000 = 3.210.000 million

Step 2 – Calculate the total vehicle loads in tonnes for international transport in Poland in 2007

15,4 x 50.000 = 770.000 million

Step 3 – Calculate the total vehicle loads in tonnes for national transport in Poland in 2007

3.210.000 – 770.000 = 2.440.000 million

Step 4 – Work out the average vehicle load for national transport

2.440.000/200.000 = 12,2 tonnes

## Pregunta 254

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDprSFdqYkw3ZkxFd3Vod2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=21

### Enunciado

Expenditure on education grew by 5,7% between 2011 and 2012 in the UK. What was the GDP in the UK in 2011?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0321-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 850.151,86 million EUR
- **B.** 1.771.490,23 million EUR
- **C.** 1.871.490,34 million EUR
- **D.** 625.634,57 million EUR
- **E.** 1.872.465,41 million EUR

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **B**: 1.771.490,23 million EUR

Step 1 – Calculate the total amount spent on education in 2012

5.314/0,0457 = 116.280,0875 million

Step 2 – Calculate the total amount spent on education in 2011

116.280,0875/1,057 = 110.009,5435 million

Step 3 – Work out the GDP in the UK in 2011

110.009,5435/0,0621 = 1.771.490,23 million

## Pregunta 255

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDprSFdqYkw3ZkxFd3Vod2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=22

### Enunciado

In 2013, in Sweden, 74,6% of men aged between 15 and 64 years were employed. What percentage of women aged between 15 and 64 years were employed in Sweden?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0322-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 69,6%
- **B.** 73,5%
- **C.** 72,2%
- **D.** 67,8%
- **E.** 68,4%

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 69,6%

Step 1 – Calculate the total population employed in Sweden

9.500.000 x 0,7211 = 6.850.450

Step 2 – Calculate the total male population (15 – 64) in Sweden and from this the female population

Male: 9.500.000 x 0,502 = 4.769.000

Female: 9.500.000 – 4.769.000 = 4.731.000

Step 3 – Work out the total male population employed and from this the total female population employed

Male: 4.769.000 x 0,746 = 3.557.674

Female: 6.580.450 – 3.557.674 = 3.292.776

Step 4 – Work out the percentage of women aged 15 – 64 that were employed

3.292.776/4.731.000 x 100 = 69,6%

## Pregunta 256

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDprSFdqYkw3ZkxFd3Vod2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=23

### Enunciado

A chain of supermarkets in a US city records the number of customers and the total spent by customers each year at different branches. Between 2010 and 2015 the number of customers at the Green Park store increased by one third, while the customer spending at the same store increased by 20%. By how many dollars did the average amount spent by each customer at the Green Park store decrease in that period?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0323-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** $8
- **B.** $7
- **C.** $6
- **D.** $5
- **E.** $9

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: $8

Step 1 : Calculate the average spending per customer in 2015: We may just divide the relevant figures from the table as both figures are in thousands: 4.320/60=72.

Step 2 : Calculate the multiplier for an increase of 1/3: 1+(1/3)=4/3.

Step 3 : calculate the number of customers in 2010: 60/(4/3)=(3/4)*60=45.

Step 4 : Calculate the percentage multiplier for a 20% increase: 1+(20/100)=1.2.

Step 5 : Calculate the customer spending in 2010: 4.320/1,2=3.600.

Step 6 : Calculate the average spending per customer in 2010: 3.600/45=80.

Step 7 : Calculate the decrease in spending per customer: 80-72=8 dollars.

Tip: As both customer numbers and customer spending are stated in thousands you can save time by for example only writing 42 instead of 42.000 in the calculator.

## Pregunta 257

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDprSFdqYkw3ZkxFd3Vod2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=24

### Enunciado

If Spain’s total area given to oil seeds rose by 2% between 2000 and 2007, how much did the organic area specialising in sunflowers increase during this period?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0324-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 21%
- **B.** 3%
- **C.** 16%
- **D.** 58%
- **E.** 45%

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **E**: 45%

Step 1 – Calculate the total area given to oil seeds in 2000 and 2007

2000: 20.500/0,032 = 640.625

2007: 640.625 x 1,02 = 653.437,5

Step 2 – Calculate the organic area given to oil seeds in 2007

653.437,5 x 0,038 = 24.830,625

Step 3 – Work out how much was given to sunflowers in 2000 and 2007

2000: 20.500 x 0,005 = 102,5

2007: 24.830,625 x 0,006 = 148,98375

Step 4 – Calculate the increase in organic sunflower production between 2000 and 2007

148,98375/102,5 = 1,4535 (45%)

## Pregunta 258

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDprSFdqYkw3ZkxFd3Vod2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=25

### Enunciado

In the EU27 despite the decline in the number of cows by 16% between 2001 and 2011, the production of milk increased by 1,5% in the same period. By what percentage did the average cows’ milk yield increase between 2001 and 2011?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0325-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 21,6%
- **B.** 20,4%
- **C.** 22,3%
- **D.** 20,8%
- **E.** 19,7%

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **D**: 20,8%

Step 1 – Calculate the number of dairy cows in 2001 in the EU

22.596/0,84 = 26.900 (100 – 16 = 84%)

Step 2 – Calculate the milk production in 2001 in the EU

150.220/1,015 = 148.000

Step 3 – Calculate the yield in 2001 in the EU

148.000/26.900 = 5,501858736

Step 4 – Calculate the percentage increase between 2001 and 2011

6,648/5,501858736 = 1,208318919 (20,8%)

## Pregunta 259

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDprSFdqYkw3ZkxFd3Vod2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=26

### Enunciado

In 2009, 2.000.000 children under 18 lacked fruit and vegetables in the EU. Given the EU population, aged 18 and under grew by 2% between 2009 and 2011, how many children under 18 were at risk of poverty in the EU in 2011?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0326-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 12,5 million
- **B.** 12,3 million
- **C.** 12,1 million
- **D.** 12,7 million
- **E.** 11,9 million

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 12,5 million

Step 1 – Calculate the number of children under 18 in the EU in 2009

2.000.000/0,044 = 45.454.545,45

Step 2 – Calculate the population under 18 in the EU in 2011

45.454.545,45 x 1,02 = 46.363.636,36

Step 3 – Calculate the number of children under 18 at risk of poverty in 2011

46.363.636,36 x 0,27 = 12.518.181,82

## Pregunta 260

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDprSFdqYkw3ZkxFd3Vod2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=27

### Enunciado

If Greece’s GDP shrunk by 2% between 2009 and 2010, how much did tax revenue from income tax decrease during this period?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0327-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 12,79%
- **B.** 4,13%
- **C.** 7,82%
- **D.** 3,04%
- **E.** 5,44%

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **C**: 7,82%

Step 1 – Calculate GDP in Greece in 2009

75.868/0,332 = 228.518,0723 million

Step 2 – Calculate the GDP in Greece in 2010

228.518,0723 x 0,98 = 223.947,7108 million

Step 3 – Calculate the tax revenue in Greece in 2010

223.947,7108 x 0,335 = 75.002,48313 million

Step 4 – Work out the tax revenue from income tax in 2009 and from this the % change

2009: 75.868 x 0,074 = 5.614,232

2010: 75.002,48313 x 0,069 = 5.175,171336

% change: 5.175,171336/5.614,232 = 0,9217950623

1 - 0,9217950623 = 0,07820493774 (7,8% decrease)

## Pregunta 261

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDprSFdqYkw3ZkxFd3Vod2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=28

### Enunciado

What was the average inflation rate in Belgium in the 8 months between May and December 2013?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0328-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 1,3
- **B.** 1,0
- **C.** 1,1
- **D.** 1,2
- **E.** 0,9

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 1,3

Step 1 – Calculate the total inflation rate over 12 months between May 2013 and Apr 2014 in Belgium

1,2 x 12 = 14,4

Step 2 – Calculate the total inflation rate in the four months Jan – Apr 2014

1,0 + 0,9 +1,0 +1,1 = 4,0

Step 3 – Calculate the total inflation rate between May 2013 and Dec 2013

14,4 – 4,0 = 10,4

Step 4 – Work out the average inflation rate between May and Dec 2013

10,4/8 = 1,3

## Pregunta 262

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDprSFdqYkw3ZkxFd3Vod2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=29

### Enunciado

The total crop output value was 43.890 million euro in 2012 at a price of EUR 13,2 thousand per tonne. What was the difference in the total crop value between 2011 and 2012?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0329-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** +5,0%
- **B.** +4,5%
- **C.** +5,2%
- **D.** -4,5%
- **E.** -5,0%

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **B**: +4,5%

Step 1 – Calculate the total crop volume in 2012

43.890/13,2 = 3.325 thousand tonnes (million/thousand = thousand)

Step 2 – Calculate the total crop volume in 2011

3.325/0,95 = 3.500 thousand tonnes

Step 3 – Work out the price in 2011

13,2/1,1 = EUR 12 thousand per tonne

Step 4 – Calculate the total crop output in 2011 and from this the difference between 2011 and 2012

3.500 x 12 = 42.000 million tonnes

43.890/42.000 = 1,045 (4,5% increase)

## Pregunta 263

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDprSFdqYkw3ZkxFd3Vod2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=30

### Enunciado

In 2013 in Denmark, there were 2.805.600 men aged 15 -64 years making up 50,1% of the total population in that age group. How much more did men of that age, as a group earn on average per month than women of the same group in Denmark?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0330-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 1,6 thousand million EUR
- **B.** 3,2 thousand million EUR
- **C.** 2,8 thousand million EUR
- **D.** 2,6 thousand million EUR
- **E.** 1,8 thousand million EUR

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **D**: 2,6 thousand million EUR

Step 1 – Work out the total population (15 – 64) and from this the total female population

2.805.600/0,501 = 5.600.000

So 5.600.000 x 0,499 = 2.794.400 (or 5.600.000 – 2.805.600 = 2.794.400)

Step 2 – Calculate the average monthly earnings in the male working population

2.805.600 x 29,62 x 158 x 0,756 = 9.926.352.407

Step 3 – Calculate the average monthly earnings in the female population

2.794.400 x 24,7 x 150 x 0,711 = 7.361.162.172

Step 4 – Work out the difference in earnings

9.926.352.407 - 7.361.162.172 = 2.565.190.235 EUR

## Pregunta 264

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDprSFdqYkw3ZkxFd3Vod2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=31

### Enunciado

In 2012 compared to 2011, GDP in Belgium dropped by 327,6 million euros. What was the difference in GDP in Belgium between 2009 and 2012?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0331-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 13.028 million
- **B.** 3.638 million
- **C.** 14.286 million
- **D.** 12.700 million
- **E.** 5.465 million

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **D**: 12.700 million

Step 1 – Work out the GDP in Belgium in 2012 and 2011

327,6 represents a 0,1% drop

So 3.276 = 1%

And 3.276 x 99,9 = 327.272,4 million (100 – 0,1 = 99,9%) is GDP in 2012

From above, we can see that the GDP in 2011 was:

3.276 x 100 = 327.600 million

Step 2 – Work out the GDP in 2010

327.600 represents a 1,8% growth between 2010 and 2011

So 327.600/1,018 = 321.807,4656 million

Step 3 – Work out the GDP in 2009

321.807,4656 represents a 2,3% growth between 2009 and 2010

so 321.807,4656/1,023 = 314.572,3027 million

Step 4 – Calculate the difference between 2012 and 2009

327.272,4 – 314.572,3027 = 12.700,0973 million

## Pregunta 265

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDprSFdqYkw3ZkxFd3Vod2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=32

### Enunciado

In 2010 in the EU, 7.400 large enterprises with e-commerce made a turnover of 45.000.000 EUR. The total number of large enterprises in 2010 is at the same level as 2008 but large enterprise turnover from e-commerce has increased by 20% in the same period. What was the average total turnover (both e-commerce and non-e-commerce) per large enterprise in 2008?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0332-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 8.400 EUR
- **B.** 33.800 EUR
- **C.** 5.900 EUR
- **D.** 12.500 EUR

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **D**: 12.500 EUR

Step 1 – Calculate the total number of large enterprises in 2008 and 2010

7.400/0,37 = 20.000

Step 2 – Calculate the turnover from e-commerce in 2008

45.000.000/1,2 = 37.500.000

Step 3 – Calculate the total turnover for large enterprises in 2008

37.500.000/0,15 = 250.000.000

Step 4 – Calculate the average total turnover per large enterprise in 2008

250.000.000/20.000 = 12.500

## Pregunta 266

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDprSFdqYkw3ZkxFd3Vod2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=33

### Enunciado

Egypt saw a threefold increase in the number of tourists between 2001 and 2010 while the percentage of tourists from the EU has shrunk by 30% in the same period. How many tourists from the EU did Egypt have in 2001?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0333-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 6.908.370
- **B.** 1.611.953
- **C.** 3.289.700
- **D.** 9.869.100
- **E.** 7.675.967

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **C**: 3.289.700

Step 1 – Calculate the number of tourists in Egypt in 2001

14.730/3 = 4.910

Step 2 – Calculate the percentage of tourists from the EU in 2001

100 – 30 = 70%

46,9/0,7 = 67%

Step 3 – Calculate the number of tourists from the EU in 2001

4.910 x 0,67 = 3.289,7 thousand

## Pregunta 267

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDprSFdqYkw3ZkxFd3Vod2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=34

### Enunciado

In 2010 in the EU, 6.048 more non-residents stayed in hotels between June and September than in 2011. What percentage of the total that stayed in hotels in the EU in 2010 were residents?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0334-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 47%
- **B.** 48%
- **C.** 52%
- **D.** 55%
- **E.** 53%

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **C**: 52%

Step 1 – Calculate the number of non-residents in 2010 in the EU

339.552 + 6.048 = 345.600

Step 2 – Calculate the total hotel residents in the EU in 2010

754.560/1,048 = 720.000

Step 3 – Calculate the percentage of residents in the EU in 2010

345.600/720.000 x 100 = 48% is non-residents so 52% are residents

or (720.000 – 345.600)/720.000 x 100 = 52%

## Pregunta 268

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDprSFdqYkw3ZkxFd3Vod2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=35

### Enunciado

Total emissions in 1999 were 5.060 million tonnes. By how much did energy as a total change as the source of greenhouse gas emissions between 1999 and 2008?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0335-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 51 million tonnes
- **B.** 356 million tonnes
- **C.** 55 million tonnes
- **D.** 25 million tonnes
- **E.** 36 million tonnes

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **C**: 55 million tonnes

Step 1 – Calculate the total emissions in 2008

100 – 2,5 = 97,5%

(2,5 represents the percentage change as found in the table)

5.060 x 0,975 = 4933,5

Step 2 – Calculate the share of energy as a source in 1999

5.060 x 0,782 = 3.956,92

Step 3 – Calculate the energy as a source in 2008

4933,5 x 0,791 = 3.902,3985

Step 4 – Calculate the change in energy as a source of energy emission between 1999 and 2008

3.956,92 – 3.902,3985 = 54,5215

## Pregunta 269

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDprSFdqYkw3ZkxFd3Vod2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=36

### Enunciado

General government expenditure was EUR 6.380 billion in 2012 in the EU. GDP grew by 3,2% between 2009 and 2012 while the percentage spent on social protection dropped by 1 percentage point in the same period. How much was spent on social protection in 2009 in the EU?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0336-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 2.353 billion
- **B.** 2.772 billion
- **C.** 2.557 billion
- **D.** 2.503 billion
- **E.** 2.608 billion

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **E**: 2.608 billion

Step 1 – Work out the percentage of GDP spent on general government expenditure in the EU in 2012

6,7 + 1,5 + 1,9 + 4,1 + 0,8 + 0,8 + 7,3 + 1,1 + 5,3 + 19,8 = 49,3%

Step 2 – Work out the GDP in 2012

6.380/0,493 = 12.941,17647 billion

Step 3 – Work out the GDP in 2009

12.941,17647/1,032 = 12.539,89968 billion

Step 4 – Calculate the amount spent on social protection in 2009

(remember we need to find the number before it dropped by 1 percent)

19,8 + 1,0 = 20,8%

12.539,89968 x 0,208 = 2.608,299134 billion

## Pregunta 270

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDprSFdqYkw3ZkxFd3Vod2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=37

### Enunciado

Between the second half of 2012 and the second half of 2013, the highest percentage increase in household electricity prices were registered in one country in the table below and the highest percentage decreases in electricity prices were registered in another country in the table below. What was the difference in the average price per 100 kWh between these two countries in the second half of 2011?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0337-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 11,7
- **B.** 17,8
- **C.** 12,0
- **D.** 15,6
- **E.** 13,7

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **E**: 13,7

Step 1 – Identify the two countries with the highest increase and the highest decrease between 2nd half of 2012 and 2013

Highest increase: Estonia 21,7%

Highest decrease: Cyprus -14,7%

Step 2 – Calculate the average price per 100 kWh in the 2nd half of 2012 for both countries

Estonia: 13,7/1,217 = 11,25718981

Cyprus: 24,8/0,853 = 29,07385698 (100 - 14,7 = 85,3%)

Step 3 – Calculate the average price per 100 kWh in the 2nd half of 2011

Estonia: 11,25718981/1,078 = 10,44266216

Cyprus: 29,07385698/1,206 = 24,10767577

Step 4 – Work out the difference between the two prices in 2011

24,10767577 – 10,44266216 = 13,66501361

## Pregunta 271

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDprSFdqYkw3ZkxFd3Vod2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=38

### Enunciado

Between 2001 and 2011 in Estonia, the bovine population dropped by 10% while the percentage share of cow population and dairy cow population dropped by 3,2 and 12,5 percentage points respectively. How many dairy cows were there in Estonia in 2001?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0338-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 104 thousand
- **B.** 84 thousand
- **C.** 136 thousand
- **D.** 128 thousand
- **E.** 68 thousand

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **D**: 128 thousand

Step 1 – Calculate the bovine population in Estonia in 2001

234/0,9 = 260 thousand (100 – 10 = 90%)

Step 2 – Calculate the percentage of cow population and the number of cows in Estonia in 2001

46,5 + 3,2 = 49,7%

260 x 0,497 = 129,22

Step 3 – Calculate the percentage of dairy cows and the number of dairy cows in Estonia in 2001

86,9 + 12,5 = 99,4%

129,22 x 0,994 = 128,44468

## Pregunta 272

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDprSFdqYkw3ZkxFd3Vod2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=39

### Enunciado

In the second half of 2011, what was the difference in the average price per 100 kWh between the two countries which had the lowest and highest prices per 100 kWh in the second half of 2013?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0339-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** €9,4
- **B.** €6,5
- **C.** €9,5
- **D.** €8,1
- **E.** €5,3

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: €9,4

Step 1 – Identify the two countries with the lowest and highest prices per 100 kWh in 2nd half of 2013

Lowest: Romania – €3,1

Highest: Sweden – €12,2

Step 2 – Work out the average prices for these two countries in the second half of 2012

Romania: 3,1/1,099 = 2,820746133

Sweden: 12,2/0,993 = 12,28600201 (100 – 0,7 = 99,3%)

Step 3 – Work out the average prices for these two countries in the second half of 2011

Romania: 2,820746133/1,044 = 2,701864112

Sweden: 12,28600201/1,018 = 12,06876425

Step 4 – Calculate the difference between the average prices in these two countries in the 2nd half of 2011

12,06876425 – 2,701864112 = 9,366900141 (€9,4)

## Pregunta 273

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDprSFdqYkw3ZkxFd3Vod2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=40

### Enunciado

What was the change in the difference between median incomes in the fifth and first quintiles in Austria between 2009 and 2010?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0340-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** -2,9%
- **B.** +2,8%
- **C.** +2,9%
- **D.** -2,8%
- **E.** +1,5%

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **D**: -2,8%

Step 1 – Calculate the median of the first quintile in 2009 in Austria

11.898/1,017 = 11.699,11504

Step 2 – Calculate the median of the fifth quintile in Austria in 2009

100 – 1,4 = 98,6%

36.822/0,986 = 37.344,82759

Step 3 – Calculate the difference between the fifth and first quintiles in 2009 and 2010

2009: 37.344,82759 – 11.699,11504 = 25.645,71255

2010: 36.822 – 11.898 = 24.924

Step 4: Calculate the % change between 2009 and 2010

24.924/25.645,71255 x 100 = 97,18583545%

100 – 97,18583445 = 2,814164545% decrease

## Pregunta 274

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpnd2pTU1g5XzFFd3RJUWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=1

### Enunciado

In Belgium, the percentage of the population aged 65 years and over is expected to increase by 45% from 2010 to 2060 while the percentage of the population aged 80 years and over is expected to increase by 5 percentage points. If the total population is expected to grow by 2,5% over the same period, how many people are expected to be aged between 65 to 79 years in 2060?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0341-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 1.671 thousand
- **B.** 2.199 thousand
- **C.** 1.100 thousand
- **D.** 2.771 thousand
- **E.** 1.587 thousand

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 1.671 thousand

Step 1 – Calculate the expected total population in 2060

10.840 x 1,025 = 11.111 thousand

Step 2 – Calculate the % of the population aged 65+ in 2060 and from this the population

17,2 x 1,45 = 24,94%

11.111 x 0,2494 = 2.771,0834

Step 3 – Calculate the % of the population aged 80+ and from this the population

4,9 + 5 = 9,9%

11.111 x 0,099 = 1.099,989

Step 4 – Calculate the number of people aged 65 to 79

2.771,0834 – 1.099,989 = 1.671,0944 thousand

## Pregunta 275

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpnd2pTU1g5XzFFd3RJUWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=2

### Enunciado

10% more people in the 65+ age group participated in tourism in 2011 than in 2006. This age group increased by 6% in the same period. The EU population in 2011 was 506 million. What percentage of the 65+ age group participated in tourism in 2006?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0342-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 13,92%
- **B.** 21,20%
- **C.** 17,20%
- **D.** 20,75%
- **E.** 19,27%

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **E**: 19,27%

Step 1 – Calculate how many people were in the 65+ age group from the population as a whole in 2011 and 2006

2011: 506 x 0,17 = 86,02 million

2006: 86,02/1,06 = 81,1509434 million

Step 2 – Calculate the number of people 65+ that were tourists in 2011 and 2006

2011: 86,02 x 0,2 = 17,204

2006: 17,204/1,1 = 15,64

Step 3 – Calculate the percentage of 65+ age group who were tourists in 2006

15,64/81,1509434 x 100 = 19,27272727%

## Pregunta 276

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpnd2pTU1g5XzFFd3RJUWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=3

### Enunciado

How much more milk would Portugal have to produce in 2011 to match the same yield per cow as Denmark?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0343-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 35 thousand tonnes
- **B.** 121 thousand tonnes
- **C.** 48 thousand tonnes
- **D.** 427 thousand tonnes
- **E.** 62 thousand tonnes

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **B**: 121 thousand tonnes

Step 1 – Calculate the yield per cow in Denmark

4.880.000/579.000 = 8,428324698 tonnes per cow

Step 2 – Calculate the milk that needed to be produced in Portugal to match Denmark’s yield

Yield = Milk production/number of cows

So Yield x number of cows = Milk production

8,428324698 x 242.000 = 2.039.654,577 tonnes (or 2.039,654577 thousand tonnes)

Step 3 – Calculate how much more milk would need to have been produced in Portugal to meet this yield

2.039,654577 – 1.919 = 120,6545769 thousand tonnes

## Pregunta 277

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpnd2pTU1g5XzFFd3RJUWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=4

### Enunciado

During 2013, a farmer sold 40 tonnes of wheat and 25 tonnes of barley. What was his turnover in euros?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0344-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** EUR 1.155
- **B.** EUR 1.265
- **C.** EUR 1.265.000
- **D.** EUR 1.315.000
- **E.** EUR 1.315

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **D**: EUR 1.315.000

Step 1 – Calculate the price of wheat

8.400/400 = 21 thousand per tonne

Step 2 - Calculate the price of barley

3401/179 = 19 thousand per tonne

Step 3 – Calculate the farmer’s turnover

(40 x 21) + (25 x 19) =1.315 thousand euros

## Pregunta 278

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpnd2pTU1g5XzFFd3RJUWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=6

### Enunciado

In 2011 in the EU, € 26 billion was spent on holiday trips by tourists aged 65 or over, 20% of the total spending of EU residents. What percentage of tourists were 65 and over?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0346-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 17,63%
- **B.** 13,33%
- **C.** 25,43%
- **D.** 34,22%
- **E.** 11,55%

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **B**: 13,33%

Step 1 – Calculate the total amount spent on holiday trips in the EU

26/0,2 = 130 billion

Step 2 – Work out the total number of tourists in the EU

130.000.000.000/1.300 = 100.000.000

Step 3 – Calculate the number of tourists 65+

26.000.000.000/1950 = 13.333.333,33

Step 4 – Calculate the percentage of tourists over 65+

13.333.333,33/100.000.000 x 100 = 13,33%

## Pregunta 279

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpnd2pTU1g5XzFFd3RJUWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=7

### Enunciado

Italy’s new electricity capacity in 2012 was 37% of the total of Germany’s and Spain’s capacity in 2012, a growth rate of 25% in 2007. What was Italy’s new electricity capacity in 2007?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0347-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 7.619
- **B.** 3.911
- **C.** 3.130
- **D.** 4.876
- **E.** 6.095

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **D**: 4.876

Step 1 – Work out the new capacity in Germany in 2012

We have to multiply the capacity from 2007 with the growth in the period 2007-2012 which was 127% per the table. Keep in mind that the multiplier in that case is 2,27 and not 1,27 as 1,27 would suggest only a 27% increase.

5.900 x 2,27 = 13.393

Step 2 – Work out the new capacity in Spain in 2012

8.800 x 0,35 = 3.080 (100 – 65 = 35%)

Step 3 – Work out the new capacity in Italy in 2012

13.393 + 3.080 = 16.473

16.473 x 0,37 = 6.095,01

Step 4 – Work out the new capacity in 2007

6.095,01/1,25 = 4.876,008

## Pregunta 280

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpnd2pTU1g5XzFFd3RJUWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=8

### Enunciado

In 2009 in the EU, the number of serious accidents was down 12,5% and the number of fatal accidents was down 20% on the previous year but the breakdown between male and female accidents remained the same. How many more men were injured or killed in 2008?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0348-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 328.912
- **B.** 176.760
- **C.** 72.038
- **D.** 289.918
- **E.** 467.233

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 328.912

Step 1 – Work out the number of serious accidents in 2008 and from this, the number of additional men hurt

2.800.000/0,875 = 3.200.000 (100 – 12,5 = 87,5%)

So (3.200.000 – 2.800.000) x 0,82 = 328.000

Step 2 – Work out the number of fatal accidents in 2008 and from this the number of additional men killed

3.800/0,80 = 4.750 (100 – 20 = 80%)

So (4.750 – 3.800) x 0,96 = 912

Step 3 – Add the two figures

328.000 + 912 = 328.912

## Pregunta 281

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpnd2pTU1g5XzFFd3RJUWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=9

### Enunciado

In 2011, Norway’s share of energy from renewable sources exceeds Estonia’s by 160% and is 162,5 times bigger than Malta’s share. What is the difference between Norway’s share and Malta’s share?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0349-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 39,8
- **B.** 64,8
- **C.** 39,6
- **D.** 25,8
- **E.** 64,6

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **E**: 64,6

Step 1 – Work out Norway’s share of energy resources in 2011

25,0 x 2,60 = 65,0

Step 2 – Work out the Malta’s share of energy resources in 2011

65,0/162,5 = 0,4

Step 3 – Work out difference between Norway and Malta

65,0 – 0,4 = 64,6

## Pregunta 282

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpnd2pTU1g5XzFFd3RJUWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=10

### Enunciado

Between 2002 and 2009, the euro appreciated in percentage terms against the Romanian leu by 5 times more than between 2009 and 2012. How many more leu could you buy for €100 in 2012 compared to 2002?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0350-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 17,10 leu
- **B.** 68,40 leu
- **C.** 35,84 leu
- **D.** 88,35 leu
- **E.** 102,60 leu

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **D**: 88,35 leu

Step 1 – Work out by how much the Romanian leu appreciated between 2009 and 2012

4,4460/4,2750 = 1,04 (4%)

Step 2 – Work out the exchange rate in 2002

4 x 5 = 20%

We now know that the EUR appriciated 20% between 2002 and 2009.

Knowing that and the 2009 exchange rate we have to apply reverse pecentages to find the starting exchage rate from 2002.

We do this by dividing the 2009 exchange rate with 1,2 (representing 20%)

4,2750/1,2 = 3,5625

Step 3 – Work out the difference in leu for €100 between 2002 and 2012

2012: 100 x 4,4460 = 444,60 leu

2002: 100 x 3,5625 = 356,25 leu

444,60 – 356,25 = 88,35 leu

## Pregunta 283

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpnd2pTU1g5XzFFd3RJUWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=14

### Enunciado

In 2011 in Belgium, 474.320 children, aged 16 and under, were living with their mother only. How many children, aged 16 and under, lived without either parent?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0354-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 121.000
- **B.** 4.157.560
- **C.** 87.120
- **D.** 658.778
- **E.** 12.100

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 121.000

Step 1 – Calculate the total number of children aged 16 and under in Belgium

474.320/0,098 = 4.840.000

Or 474.320/9,8 = 48.400

Step 2 – Calculate the percentage without parents

100 – (85,9 + 1,8 +9,8) = 2,5%

Step 3 – Calculate the children not living with either parent

4.840.000 x 0,025 = 121.000

Or 48.400 x 2,5 = 121.000

## Pregunta 284

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpnd2pTU1g5XzFFd3RJUWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=15

### Enunciado

Between 2004 and 2007, the number of letter-post items dropped in Italy and the Netherlands by 8% and 11% respectively while the price per letter increased by 20% and 10% respectively. How much did Italy and the Netherlands spend on posting letters in 2004?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0355-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 2.837.000 thousand Euros
- **B.** 3.987.000 thousand Euros
- **C.** 2.878.000 thousand Euros
- **D.** 2.852.809 thousand Euros
- **E.** 4.333.403 thousand Euros

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **C**: 2.878.000 thousand Euros

Step 1 – Work out how number of letters in 2004 in Italy and Netherlands

Italy: 3.109.600/0,92 = 3.380.000 thousand (100 – 8 = 92%)

Netherlands: 2.643.300/0,89 = 2.970.000 thousand (100 – 11 = 89%)

Step 2 – Work out price per letter in 2004 in Italy and Netherlands

Italy: 0,60/1,2 = 0,50

Netherlands: 0,44/1,1 = 0,40

Step 3 – Work out how much Italy and the Netherlands spent in 2004

Italy: 3.380.000 x 0,5 =1.690.000 thousand

Netherlands: 2.970.000 x 0,4 = 1.188.000 thousand

Step 4 – Work out the total spent in Italy and the Netherlands

1.690.000 + 1.188.000 = 2.878.000 thousand

## Pregunta 285

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpnd2pTU1g5XzFFd3RJUWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=16

### Enunciado

In the EU27 in 2012, the amount of total expenditure on central government shrunk by 0,9 percentage points while the % share of GDP spent on central government grew by 1%. The total expenditure in 2012 was EUR 213.332.000 million. What was the GDP in EU27 in 2012?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0356-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** EUR 1.192.545.350 million
- **B.** EUR 3.274.000.000 million
- **C.** EUR 1.800.540.055 million
- **D.** EUR 3.271.980.021 million
- **E.** 418.899.607,279 million

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **E**: 418.899.607,279 million

Step 1 – Calculate the amount of total expenditure spent on the central government in 2012 in EU27

36,4-0,9 = 35,5%

213.332.000*0,355 = 75.732.860 million

Step 2 – Calculate the % of GDP spent on the central government in 2012 in EU27

17,9 x 1,01 = 18,079%

Step 2 – Calculate the GDP in the EU27 in 2012

75.732.860/0,18079 = 418.899.607,279 million

## Pregunta 286

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpnd2pTU1g5XzFFd3RJUWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=18

### Enunciado

In 2010, there were 86,6 million pupils in the EU within pre-primary to secondary levels of education. How many girls were in education in the EU in 2010?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0358-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 42,28 million
- **B.** 27,97 million
- **C.** 44,36 million
- **D.** 43,73 million

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 42,28 million

Step 1 – Work out by how many pupils were in pre-primary education in the EU in 2010 and from this the number of girls

86,6 x 0,172 = 14,8952 million

Ratio is 1,05 boys to 1,00 girl so 2,05 parts in total

14,8952 x 1,00/2,05 = 7,26595122 million girls

Step 2 – Work out pupils were in primary education and from this the number of girls

86,6 x 0,323 = 27,9718 million

Ratio is 1,06 to 1,00 girl so 2,06 parts in total

27,9718 x 1,00/2,06 = 13,57854369 million girls

Step 3 – Work out pupils were in secondary education and from this the number of girls

86,6 x 0,505 = 43,733 million

Ratio is 1,04 to 1,00 girl so 2,04 parts in total

43,733 x 1,00/2,04 = 21,4377451 million girls

Step 4 – Calculate the total number of girls in education

7,26595122 + 13,57854369 + 21,4377451 = 42,28224001 million

## Pregunta 287

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpnd2pTU1g5XzFFd3RJUWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=19

### Enunciado

In 2011 in Ireland, there were 150.000 third-country nationals spread across the three age groups in the ratio 5:6:4. What percentage of the total third-country nationals acquired citizenship in 2011?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0359-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 15,8%
- **B.** 15,0%
- **C.** 27,0%
- **D.** 25,0%
- **E.** 50,0%

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **B**: 15,0%

Step 1 – Work out the number of third-country nationals in each age category in Ireland

5:6:4 = 15 parts in total

So 0 – 14 years: 5/15 x 150.000 = 50.000 (5 parts of 15)

15 – 39 years: 6/15 x 150.000 = 60.000 (6 parts of 15)

40+ years: 4/15 x 150.000 = 40.000 (4 parts of 15)

Step 2 – Calculate the number of third-country nationals who have acquired citizenship for each age group

0 – 14 years: 50.000 x 0,04 = 2.000

15 – 39 years: 60.000 x 0,155 = 9.300

40+ years: 40.000 x 0,28 = 11.200

Step 3 – Calculate the percentage of total third-country nationals who have acquired citizenship in Ireland

2.000 + 9.300 + 11.200 = 22.500

22.500/150.000 x 100 = 15%

## Pregunta 288

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpnd2pTU1g5XzFFd3RJUWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=20

### Enunciado

In heat one, the winner accelerated to full speed over the first 40 m and then ran at a constant speed, crossing the line at 13,56 m/s. How long did it take the winner to reach full speed?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0360-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 6,03 s
- **B.** 6,14 s
- **C.** 5,81 s
- **D.** 5,92 s
- **E.** 6,16 s

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **C**: 5,81 s

Step 1 – Calculate the time taken once the winner had reached full speed

Speed = distance/time

So 13,56 = (100-40)/time (winner reached constant speed for the last 60m of the race)

Time = 60/13,56 = 4,424778761 seconds

Step 2 – Work out how long it took to reach top speed

10,23 (fastest time) – 4,424778761 = 5,805221239 seconds

## Pregunta 289

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpnd2pTU1g5XzFFd3RJUWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=21

### Enunciado

Between 1990 and 2011, electricity generation from hydropower increased by 7% while its share of total renewable electricity generation shrank to 40% over the same period. Wind power generation doubled over the same period. What share of total renewable electricity generation did wind power have in 2011?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0361-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 2,4%
- **B.** 15,0%
- **C.** 2,7%
- **D.** 18,3%
- **E.** 7,4%

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 2,4%

Step 1 – Work out how hydropower was generated in 2011

300.000 x 1,07 = 321.000

Step 2 – Work how much total renewable electricity was generated in 2011

321.000/0,40 = 802.500

Step 3 – Work out much wind power was generated in 2011

9.630 x 2 = 19.260

Step 4 – Work out the share of total renewable electricity from wind power 2011

19.260/802.500 x 100 = 2,4%

## Pregunta 290

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpnd2pTU1g5XzFFd3RJUWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=22

### Enunciado

What was the total turnover of the activity with the most people employed per enterprise and the activity with the highest turnover per person employed?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0362-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 230.931 million Euros
- **B.** 178.106 million Euros
- **C.** 200.213 million Euros
- **D.** 177.626 million Euros
- **E.** 265.577 million Euros

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **C**: 200.213 million Euros

Step 1 – Work out the number of persons employed per enterprise

Activity                      Number of employees per enterprise

Fruit & vegetables                        418/44,5 = 9,39

Meat & meat products                 178/23,6 = 7,54

Dairy products                              115/14,4 = 7,98

Sugar & confectionery                  70/10 = 7

Coffee, tea, cocoa                         38/5,5 = 6,91

Other                                            374/40,1 = 9,33

Fruit & vegetables has the most people employed per enterprise.

Note: Sugar & confectionery and coffee, tea, cocoa can be quickly discarded.

Step 2 – Work out the turnover per employee

Activity                                            Turnover per employee

Fruit & vegetables                                    143.842/418 = 344,12

Meat & meat products                             87.089/178 = 489,26

Dairy products                                          56.371/115 = 490,18

Sugar & confectionery                              33.784/70 = 482,63

Coffee, tea, cocoa                                     15.076/38 = 396,74

Other                                                        121.735/374 = 325,49

Dairy products has the highest turnover per employee

Step 3 – Calculate the turnover for both activities

143.842 + 56.371 = 200.213 million

## Pregunta 291

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpnd2pTU1g5XzFFd3RJUWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=24

### Enunciado

In 2011, the average life expectancy of males in Slovakia was 72,3 years. What was the average life expectancy of females in Slovakia in 2011?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0364-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 76,9 years
- **B.** 81,4 years
- **C.** 78,5 years
- **D.** 80,2 years
- **E.** 79,8 years

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **E**: 79,8 years

Step 1 – Calculate the total life expectancy for the population

76,1 x 5.400 = 410.940 years

Step 2 – Work out the male and female population

Females: 5.400 x 0,51 = 2.754

Males: 5.400 x 0,49 = 2.646 (or 5.400 – 2.754)

Step 3 – Calculate the total life expectancy of the male population

2.646 x 72,3 = 191.305,8

Step 4 – Calculate the average life expectancy of the female population

410.940 – 191.305,8 = 219.634,2 total life expectancy of females

219.634,2/2.754 = 79,75098039

## Pregunta 292

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpnd2pTU1g5XzFFd3RJUWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=25

### Enunciado

The number of passengers who travelled by rail in France grew by 60% between 2005 and 2011, while turnover doubled during the same period. What was the rise in the average price of rail travel per passenger in France between 2005 and 2011?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0365-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** EUR 4,40
- **B.** EUR 1,20
- **C.** EUR 2,40
- **D.** EUR 16,00
- **E.** EUR 48,00

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **D**: EUR 16,00

Step 1 – Calculate the number of passengers in France in 2005

96/1,6 = 60

Step 2 – Calculate the turnover in France in 2005

7.680/2 = 3.840 million euro

Step 3 – Calculate the average price per passenger in 2005 and 2011

2005: 3.840/60 = € 64

2011: 7.680/96 = € 80

Step 4 – Calculate the difference between the two averages

80 – 64 = € 16

## Pregunta 293

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpnd2pTU1g5XzFFd3RJUWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=26

### Enunciado

If France’s GDP increased by 3% between 2004 and 2009, how much did spending on primary education increase during this period?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0366-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 4,60%
- **B.** 5,20%
- **C.** 6,10%
- **D.** 4,89%
- **E.** 7,25%

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **C**: 6,10%

Step 1 – Calculate the GDP in France in 2004 and 2009

2004: 96.300/0,058 = 1.660.344,828 million

2009: 1.660.344,828 x 1,03 = 1.710.155,172 million

Step 2 – Calculate public expenditure in education in France in 2009

1.710.155,172 x 0,0589 = 100.728,1397

Step 3 – Calculate the spending on primary education in 2004 and 2009

2004: 96.300 x 0,209 = 20.126,7

2009: 100.728,1397 x 0,212 = 21.354,36561

Step 4 – Calculate the percentage change in spending on primary education between 2004 and 2009

21.354,36531/20.126,7 = 1,060996865 (6,1%)

## Pregunta 294

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpnd2pTU1g5XzFFd3RJUWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=27

### Enunciado

If the Czech Republic’s GDP increased by 6,6% between 2006 and 2010, how much did business R&D increase during this period?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0367-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 2,59%
- **B.** 3,90%
- **C.** 7,28%
- **D.** 3,26%
- **E.** 2,34%

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 2,59%

Step 1 – Calculate the GDP in Czech Republic in 2006 and 2010

2006: 1.670/0,0149 = 112.080,5369

2010: 112.080,5369 x 1,066 = 119.477,8523

Step 2 – Calculate the R&D expenditure in Czech Rep in 2010

119.477,8523 x 0,0156 = 1.863,854496

Step 3 – Calculate the business spending on R&D spending in 2006 and 2010

2006: 1.670 x 0,532 = 888,44

2010: 1.863,854496 x 0,489 = 911,4248485

Step 4 – Calculate the percentage change in business spending on R&D between 2006 and 2010

911,4248485/888,44 = 1,025871019 (2,59%)

## Pregunta 295

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpnd2pTU1g5XzFFd3RJUWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=30

### Enunciado

The population in the EU in 2011 was 506 million. If the Czech Republic had the same GDP per capita as the EU, what size of the population would have been big enough to produce the Czech Republic’s GDP in 2011?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0370-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 2,9 million
- **B.** 14,8 million
- **C.** 6,2 million
- **D.** 7,8 million
- **E.** 8,5 million

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **C**: 6,2 million

Step 1 – Calculate the GDP per capita in the EU in 2011

12.638.000/506 = 24.976,28458 (note: million/million)

Step 2 – Calculate the population required in the Czech Republic to make the GDP, assuming the same GDP per capita as the EU

GDP per capita = GDP/population

So Population = GDP/GDP per capita

155.000/24.976,28458 = 6,205887007 million

## Pregunta 296

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpnd2pTU1g5XzFFd3RJUWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=31

### Enunciado

In 2010 in the United States, the pupil-teacher ratio was 40% higher than that of Saudi Arabia while the number of teachers was 8⅓ times more than Saudi Arabia. How many pupils were there in Saudi Arabia in 2010?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0371-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 6.600
- **B.** 6.600.000
- **C.** 13.000
- **D.** 13.000.000
- **E.** 77.000.000

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **B**: 6.600.000

Step 1 – Work out the pupil–teacher ratio in Saudi Arabia

From the table, we can see that the ratio for the United States is 14. We also know that this is 40% higher than the ratio for Saudi Arabia.

To find the ratio for Saudi Arabia, we need to apply reverse percentage calculations as shown below:

14 ÷ 1.40 = 10

Step 2 – Work out the number of teachers in Saudi Arabia

According to the table, the United States has 5,500 thousand teachers, and we know that this is 8⅓ times more than the number in Saudi Arabia.

5,500 ÷ 8⅓ = 660 thousand

Step 3 – Work out the number of pupils in Saudi Arabia

Now that we have both figures, we can easily calculate the number of pupils in Saudi Arabia.'

660 × 10 = 6,600 thousand

## Pregunta 297

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpnd2pTU1g5XzFFd3RJUWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=33

### Enunciado

How many more people needed to be employed in Cyprus to achieve the target set?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0373-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 15,63 thousand
- **B.** 3,91 thousand
- **C.** 3,60 thousand
- **D.** 18,75 thousand
- **E.** 19,53 thousand

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **E**: 19,53 thousand

Step 1 – Calculate the total employed the previous year, 2011, in Cyprus

375/0,96 = 390,625 (100 – 4 = 96%)

Step 2 – Calculate the total employed if target had been reached

390,625 x 1,01 = 394,53125

Step 3 – Calculate the difference between target total and actual total

394,53125 – 375 = 19,53125

Note: Another way is to find 1% and work from there:

375/96 = 3,90625 (1%)

Difference between -4% and 1% is 5%

3,90625 x 5 = 19,53125

## Pregunta 298

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpnd2pTU1g5XzFFd3RJUWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=38

### Enunciado

In 2011 the total expenditure on tourism was € 25,5 billion in the EU. How much was spent on package travel in 2011?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0378-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** € 9,56 billion
- **B.** € 5,74 billion
- **C.** € 2,55 billion
- **D.** € 5,95 billion
- **E.** € 7,65 billion

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **D**: € 5,95 billion

Step 1 – Find the amount of total tourism expenditure for one of the age groups:

15-24: 25,5 x 0,1 = 2,55

25-44: 25,5 x 0,35 = 8,925

45-64: same as 25-64

65+: 25,5 x 0,2 = 5,1

Step 2 – Find the amount spent on package travel as a percentage of tourism expenditure for one of the age groups:

15-24: 2,55 x 23⅓/100 = 0,595

25-44: 8,925 x 0,15 = 1,33875

45-64: 8,925 x 0,25 = 2,23125

65+: 5,1 x 0,35 = 1,785

Step 3 – Find total spent on package travel

15-24: 0,595/0,1 = 5,95

25-44: 1,33875/0,225 = 5,95

45-64: 2,23125/0,375 = 5,95

65+: 1,785/0,3 = 5,95

## Pregunta 299

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDphZmgxdGtoa3RFc2tLZ2plMHIwTnVn/review?q=4

### Enunciado

During the period, 2002 to 2006, the total number of EU patent applications increased by a constant 2,8% per annum, while during the period 2006 to 2010, the reduction in number of patent applications was a constant 1,3% per annum. How many patent applications were there in 2010?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0384-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 60.417
- **B.** 32.998
- **C.** 54.449
- **D.** 30.682
- **E.** 72.896

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **C**: 54.449

Step 1 – Calculate the growth in EU patent applications between 2002 and 2006

51.375 x 1,0284 = 57.375,21071

(2,8% x 4 years = 1,0284)

Step 2 – Calculate the reduction in EU applications between 2006 and 2010

57.375,21071 x 0,9874 = 54.449,37564

(reduction of 1,3% for 4 years = (100%-1,3%)4 = 0,9874

Note: The student can calculate each year separately, but the use of compound percentages is far quicker

Also observe that the technology patent applications are only a subgroup of all applications and therefore not relevant to the questions as they are already included among "patent applications"

## Pregunta 300

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpFM01tTERzOVhrc2RwUWplMHIwTnVn/review?q=7

### Enunciado

How much bigger is the area of China than the area of the EU?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0397-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 132.081 km²
- **B.** 5.350.000 km²
- **C.** 5,35 km²
- **D.** 132.081.000.000 km²
- **E.** 132.081.000 km²

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **B**: 5.350.000 km²

Step 1 – Calculate the area of China

Population/area = population density

So Population/population density = area

1.353,6/141,0 = 9,6

9,6 x 1.000.000 = 9.600.000 km2 (as population is in millions)

Step 2 – Calculate the area of the EU using the method above

499,8/117,6 = 4,25

4,25 x 1.000.000 = 4.250.000 km2

Step 3 – Work out how much bigger China is than the EU

9.600.000 – 4.250.000 = 5.350.000 km2

## Pregunta 301

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpabUwtdkZHNFhFckxtQWplMHIwTnVR/review?q=4

### Enunciado

What were the exports in the EU28 area in January 2014?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0474-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 135,2 billion EUR
- **B.** 128,8 billion EUR
- **C.** 144,2 billion EUR
- **D.** 155,8 billion EUR
- **E.** 123,5 billion EUR

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **B**: 128,8 billion EUR

Step 1 – Calculate the exports in the period Jan to Mar 2014

420,0 x 0,97 = 407,4

Step 2 – Calculate the exports in Mar 2014

153,0 x 0,93 = 142,29

Step 3 – Calculate the exports in Feb 2014

135,0 x 1,01 = 136,35

Step 4 – Calculate the exports in Jan 2014

407,4 – (142,29 + 136,35) = 128,76

## Pregunta 302

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpabUwtdkZHNFhFckxtQWplMHIwTnVR/review?q=5

### Enunciado

What is the percentage point decrease in approval of the government between March and July?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0475-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 13
- **B.** 28
- **C.** 23
- **D.** 8
- **E.** 4

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 13

As we are finding the "percentage point decrease" we just need the difference in the percentages between March and July.

Step 1 : Identify the percentage who approved of the government in March (46) and July (33).

Step 2 : Subtract the percentage approval in July from the percentage approval in March: 46-33=13.

## Pregunta 303

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpabUwtdkZHNFhFckxtQWplMHIwTnVR/review?q=7

### Enunciado

What is the total area of a "D323" size photo in square inches?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0477-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 7 1/2
- **B.** 7
- **C.** 5 7/12
- **D.** 6 1/12
- **E.** 7 3/4

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 7 1/2

Step 1 : Write the width and height of the photo as improper fractions: 3 1/3=10/3, 2 1/4=9/4.

Step 2 : Multiply the numerators to find the numerator of the answer: 10*9=90.

Step 3 : Multiply the denominators to find the denominator of the answer: 3*4=12.

Step 4 : Write the answer as an improper fraction and simplify: 90/12=(90/6)/(12/6)=15/2.

Step 5 : Write the improper fraction as a mixed number: 15/2=7 1/2.

## Pregunta 304

Fuente: https://www.epsoprep.com/practice/attempt/en/UXVlc3Rpb25Qb29sQXR0ZW1wdDpabUwtdkZHNFhFckxtQWplMHIwTnVR/review?q=8

### Enunciado

On the 28th June, items weighing 32,58 million kilograms were shipped. The average weight of each item was the same as for the items shipped on the 18th June. How many items were shipped on the 28th June?

### Imagen

![Imagen pregunta](../assets/numerico/numerico-0478-01.png)

### Opciones disponibles

- **A.** 7.240.000
- **B.** 9.582.000
- **C.** 10.509.000
- **D.** 7.757.000
- **E.** 8.354.000

---

### Respuesta y explicacion

La respuesta correcta es la **A**: 7.240.000

Step 1 : Find the average weight of an item on 18th June by dividing the total weight by the number of items (both measured in millions: 31,41/6,98=4,5 kilograms.

Step 2 : Divide the total weight on 28th June by this average weight: 32,58/4,5=7,24.

Step 3 : Convert this into millions by multiplying by 1.000.000: 7,24*1.000.000=7.240.000.
